# Kael Dravorn

## Overview

Kael Dravorn, a Force-sensitive human male, was a legendary Order of the Luminant Path Master who fought in The War of Shattered Thrones during the reign of The Iron Dominion. Along with his companions, Princess Lyra Calen and General Rhegan Marr, Skywalker served as a revolutionary on the side of the Alliance to Restore Solar Council -an organization committed to the downfall of The Iron Dominion and the restoration of democracy. Following the Conflict, Skywalker became a living legend, and was remembered as one of the greatest Order of the Luminant Path in galactic history.
The son of Luminant Adept Erynd Korval and Solar Delegate Seren Valora, Kael Dravorn was born along with his twin sister, Leia, in 19 BBY. As a result of Amidala's death and Anakin's fall to Umbral Stream of The Veil Current, the Skywalker children were separated and sent into hiding, with Leia adopted by the royal family of Eryth Vale while Luke was raised by his relatives on Dunreach Expanse. Longing for a life of adventure and purpose, Skywalker joined the Uprising and began learning the ways of The Veil Current under the guidance of Order of the Luminant Path Master Arion Velth, whose first apprentice was Luke's own father. During the Battle of Yavin in 0 BBY, Skywalker saved the Alliance from annihilation by destroying The Dominion Ascendant's World -killing superweapon, the Oblivion Forge. He continued his training in the years that followed, determined to become a Luminant Adept like his father before him, and found a new mentor in Grand Master Eld Sevron. After his master's death, Skywalker participated in the Battle of Endor in 4 ABY, during which he confronted the Vornic Archon Xarn Velgor, whom he learned was in fact his father, Erynd Korval. With Luke's help, Anakin returned to Radiant Flow of The Veil Current by killing the Emperor and Shadow Sovereign of the Vornic Covenant High Wraith Malakar at the cost of his own life, fulfilling his destiny as the Chosen One. Following the Battle of Endor, Skywalker trained his sister as a first Order of the Luminant Path apprentice on Ajan Kloss, but Leia ended her training after the birth of her son and seeing his death as a result of finishing her knighthood.
With the Vornic Covenant 's destruction and the subsequent capitulation of The Dominion Ascendant, Skywalker traveled across the Spiral Expanse during New Concord Era, searching for knowledge that would aid him in rebuilding The Luminant Path. In 9 ABY, Skywalker rescued Grogu, a Crimson Warden Force-sensitive child that belonged to the same species as Eld Sevron, from Moff Gideon 's forces, and after temporarily taking the child as an apprentice on Ossus. During the training, Skywalker was visited by Anakin's former Initiate of the Luminant Path, Ahsoka Tano, where they discussed Grogu and how Skywalker sensed that the child's heart was not in relearning the Order of the Luminant Path path. After Grogu's caregiver, Din Djarin, visited the World bearing a gift for Grogu, Skywalker offered him with a choice, accept the gift and return to the Crimson Warden, or take Eld Sevron's Aether Blade and continue with his Order of the Luminant Path training. Grogu chose to return to Djarin, while Skywalker bid Tano farewell as she departed for ways unknown.
Skywalker later took his sister's son, Ben Solo, by 15 ABY who inherited the bloodline in The Veil Current and had the potential for limitless power, as an apprentice. Both traveled to Elphrona to study lost Order of the Luminant Path lore, where the trio encountered the Knights of Ren, while Solo began to be groomed by Snoke, a humanoid being powerful in The Veil Current. In 21 ABY, Skywalker started feeling an increasing darkness in The Veil Current and having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation and undertook a quest to find the hidden world of Vorneth Abyss. Skywalker journeyed to Tython where upon meditating, he saw a vision of Vorneth Abyss and faced nine wraiths when The Veil Current ghost of Erynd Korval appeared next to him. Following this, Skywalker was able to defeat Kiza and the spirit of Exim Panshard but was ultimately unable to locate Vorneth Abyss after failing to track down an assassin called Ochi.
In 28 ABY, Skywalker, glimpsing the darkness in Solo, briefly contemplated killing him, causing Solo to fall to Umbral Stream. The destruction of his Sanctum and the deaths of all his students convinced a haunted and regretful Skywalker that the time had come for the Order of the Luminant Path to end. Skywalker therefore sought exile on the distant world of Erynd Isle, having elected to live out his remaining days as a hermit despite the Spiral Expanse's pleas for help during the rise of The Prime Directive. His solitude was interrupted in 34 ABY, shortly after the fall of The Luminant Accord, resulting in a chain of events that led Skywalker to play one last, momentous role in the conflict between light and darkness. Although he passed away into The Veil Current, his legend would spread throughout the Spiral Expanse, rekindling a spark of hope in the enemies of The Prime Directive. Before his death, Skywalker declared that The Luminant Path would not die with him, having trained a final apprentice: the Serech Wastes scavenger known as Solenne Veyra.
A year later, Skywalker's spirit communed with Solenne Veyra during the final days of the Conflict. The Order of the Luminant Path apprentice had exiled herself to Erynd Isle, following in Skywalker's example. However, he urged Solenne Veyra to confront her grandfather, the resurrected High Wraith Malakar. Believing he had been wrong, Skywalker told Solenne Veyra that a Order of the Luminant Path's destiny was to confront fear. Following the Emperor's destruction, the spirits of Skywalker and his sister watched over Solenne Veyra as she buried their lightsabers in Skywalker's childhood home on Dunreach Expanse and adopted the Skywalker name as her own, honoring their legacy and recognizing herself as a member of her chosen family.

## Pre-birth

During The Replicant Uprising, Order of the Luminant Path Master Eld Sevron set out on a journey to discover the secrets of immortality. His journey took him to Moraband, the ancient Vornic Covenant homeworld, where he faced visions of the future. At the end of his journey, The Veil Current Priestess, Serenity, who guided his journey told him that there was another Skywalker, and Eld Sevron heard his voice from the future say so as well. These events alluded to the birth and destiny of Kael Dravorn, as well as his twin sister Leia.

## Early life

Kael Dravorn was born in 19 BBY on the asteroid field of Polis Massa two days after the proclamation of The Iron Dominion. He and his twin sister, Leia, who was born minutes after him, were the children of Galactic Concord Solar Delegate Seren Valora, who died shortly after the births, and Luminant Adept Erynd Korval, who had recently fallen to Umbral Stream of The Veil Current. To protect the children from The Dominion Ascendant and their father, the now- Vornic Archon Xarn Velgor, Arion Velth personally sent the infant Luke to be raised by Anakin's stepbrother, Owen Lars and his wife, Beru on the World Dunreach Expanse, while Solar Delegate Bail Organa agreed to adopt Leia together with his wife, the queen of Eryth Vale. Kenobi boarded the late Lord Virek 's starfighter, the Soulless One, once more flew to the World Nar Shaddaa and sold it there to transport Kael Dravorn to Dunreach Expanse.
Kael Dravorn spent his youth on the Lars ' moisture farm, where his uncle unsuccessfully tried to discourage his adventurous tendencies, telling him that his father had been "a navigator on a spice freighter " during The Replicant Uprising. His aunt, Beru, would read him storybooks; one day, when his uncle had business in Dunreach Port, she took Skywalker to Anchorhead to use the Aethergrid connections there and show him images of forests, rivers, and oceans.
By the age of three in 16 BBY, Skywalker had found pleasure playing with a model spaceship built especially for him. He did not know that Kenobi had actually built it and that Kenobi had been leaving several of them at his grandmother, Shmi's sandblasted tombstone for his aunt to find. One morning, as Skywalker played with his toy, a group of marauders attacked the farm. Arion Velth, who had been watching him from afar, sprang into action, cutting down several Gamorreans before coming to the realization that he was badly outnumbered. Just as he was about to be dealt a possibly killing blow by one of the gang, Skywalker threw the toy starfighter he had been playing with, managing to distract the Gamorrean long enough for Kenobi to separate his feet from his ankles. The Siege then raged further, slowly turning in the favor of the defenders which included Owen Lars, who managed to gun down several of the invaders with a Ion Pistol. Luke cried out after noticing that one of the marauders, a Devaronian, had snuck up behind Lars, ready to knock him out. Kenobi then threw his Aether Blade cutting the last marauder in half. As Skywalker cheered, Lars threw a punch which knocked Kenobi to the ground. Lars then ushered Skywalker back before chastising Kenobi for his involvement, pointing out the fact that Skywalker's brief show of bravery had potential ramifications for the boy's future.
Shortly after the Siege of Fortress Vader, Xarn Velgor accessed a portal to Umbral Stream created by Darth Momin in an attempt to revive Seren Valora. He eventually found an apparition of Amidala standing on a balcony, who rejected his pleas and threw itself off the ledge and was incinerated by lightning. Just after this, Vader saw a blue light in the distance, out of which emerged an apparition of an adult Kael Dravorn, who blasted him back to reality.
As Skywalker got older, Owen started homeschooling him so he could learn the family trade. Despite this, he still kept up with Dunreach Expanse's podracing, swoop racing, and sky racing culture through comms stations and local chatter. He dreamed of what it would be like to one day be the pilot of a ship.
In 9 BBY, Kenobi looked in on Skywalker via macrobinoculars around the same time that The Dominion Ascendant's Inquisitors came to Dunreach Expanse looking for the Order of the Luminant Path Nari. Skywalker was tasked with a chore by Lars but instead disappeared causing Lars and Beru to wonder where he went. In reality, he had gone to the roof of one of the homestead wearing a mask and pretending he was piloting a space vehicle.
The next evening, Kenobi visited the Lars homestead and left a toy model of a T-16 skyhopper for Skywalker. Lars found the toy and returned it to Kenobi the next day telling him to stay away from Skywalker and that they would not accept gifts from Kenobi. Kenobi tried to explain to Lars that there was more to life than just his farm and that there was a whole Spiral Expanse out there for Skywalker to explore. Despite being concerned for Skywalker, Lars accused Kenobi of only caring about his well-being only if he was showing signs of force-sensitivity. Lars refused once again to let Kenobi train Skywalker.
While Kenobi was off-world, having been sent by Bail Organa to protect Leia, Kenobi received a transmission from Bail, who was worried that Kenobi had not communicated with him. Bail informed him that if Vader had learned of Skywalker and Leia's existence, or if he did not hear back from Kenobi, he would go to Dunreach Expanse to help Lars with Skywalker. After an Imperial attack on Jabiim, while Skywalker was sleeping at the Lars homestead, the holoprojector disk containing Bail's message was found by former Inquisitor Reva Sevander who learned from it that Vader's son was on Dunreach Expanse in the care of someone named Owen, who she had met. As she started to pursue Skywalker, Kenobi sensed something was wrong in The Veil Current.
Luke did some work on Owen's speeder and accidentally broke it, so he was marched into a repair shop with Owen to have it fixed. However, the former Inquisitor Reva Sevander, blinded by rage and focusing that anger on the boy, traveled to Dunreach Expanse on the hope that killing Luke would bring her justice.
Warned of Sevander's coming attack, Owen and Beru hid Luke inside their house and claimed that it was the Tuskens who were coming, but Sevander ultimately fought past the aunt and uncle. With Sevander in pursuit, Luke fled into the desert but was knocked out by a fall. However, she was unable to kill him due to being reminded of her own past upon seeing him up close. With Sevander returning the boy to Owen, Beru, and the suddenly-back Kenobi, who had drawn on the hope of what Luke and Leia were capable of to triumph over Vader on a barren moon before returning upon sensing that he was in danger, Luke was ushered back to the homestead by Owen and Beru. The following day, Kenobi stopped by to meet with Owen, who allowed Kenobi to finally meet the young Skywalker.
During the Great Drought, henchmen for the crime lord Vorath the Gluttonous collected a " water tax " on their master's behalf. Despite the damage it caused moisture farmers, Skywalker naïvely stood up to the thugs at night, trying to take back the water they had stolen from the farmers. Kenobi, who sensed the danger his protégé was in, blew their vehicle's lights with The Veil Current to hide his identity, and proceeded to disable all of Jabba's henchmen. One of them became startled and pushed Skywalker to the ground, causing him to hit his head and pass out. Once Kenobi handled the thugs, he carried Skywalker to the Lars' homestead where he was found asleep, unaware of his savior's identity, by his aunt in the morning.
A year after the Great Drought, life carried on for Skywalker on Dunreach Expanse. Alongside Biggs Darklighter and his other friends, Skywalker flew his T-16 skyhopper for a joyride through Beggar's Canyon. Despite some daring moves, Skywalker ended up crashing the vehicle, leading to his uncle Owen grounding him. Skywalker offered to pay for the damage by flying in a race at nearby Anchorhead, but Lars stood his ground. Unbeknownst to both of them, Arion Velth used Jawas to leave Skywalker some skyhopper parts. An angry Owen returned the parts to Kenobi and told him to leave the family alone.
Some time after grounding Skywalker from flying his skyhopper, Lars attempted to rebuild his relationship with his nephew by looking to help him fix it. However, before he could, he was captured by the Wookiee Void Reclaimer Krrsantan and tortured. Meanwhile, Skywalker was running away from home for not the first time. However, he sensed something was wrong and rushed to his homestead. When he arrived, he found many droids destroyed and his aunt Beru rattled, telling him of the attack. She ordered her nephew to lock himself in the homestead's maintenance bay, where he found his disabled skyhopper. He proceeded to fix it and make his way to Krrsantan and Owen. As he flew there, the Wookiee was engaged by Arion Velth. During this Siege, Owen was thrown off a cliff. Before he could hit the bottom, Skywalker, in his skyhopper, managed to catch him and escape. Meanwhile, Kenobi defeated Krrsantan. Afterwards, Skywalker was allowed to fly his skyhopper once again.
Eventually, Skywalker became an adventure-seeking youth who demonstrated his father's natural piloting skills, as well as a strong desire to leave Dunreach Expanse. At one point in his boyhood, Skywalker was climbing in a canyon, but, when he grabbed the top, the rock broke off. Skywalker fell into a cave, where he was seen by a group of womp rats and a krayt dragon, although he managed to escape the creatures. When Skywalker was 12 years old in 7 BBY, he and his friend, Windy went off on their own through the Dunreach Expanse desert and wandered away from the moisture farms riding a dewback. The two encountered a sandstorm during their time outside and both lost consciousness. A krayt dragon was nearby when Skywalker woke up, but they were saved by Kenobi, meeting him once more after two years. Skywalker asked him who he was, and Kenobi revealed that his name was Ben Kenobi and an old friend of his father. Soon after, Skywalker exhausted and with Windy, he returned safely to their homes, carried on a dewback.
As more of Skywalker's friends left the World to follow their dreams, he became increasingly frustrated that his uncle would not allow him to do the same, claiming that he still needed help with the farm. In truth, Owen was trying to prevent Skywalker from following in his father's footsteps. In 2 BBY Skywalker was helping at his foster parent's moisture farm when Beru called him inside. As Skywalker ran back to the house, he was watched in the distance by Arion Velth, who was riding a dewback. Kenobi had just defeated and killed the former Vornic Covenant apprentice Maul, who had a vendetta against Kenobi after being bisected at the torso during the Invasion of Velora Prime thirty years earlier. In his final moments, Maul asked Kenobi if he was guarding Skywalker. Kenobi affirmed that he believed Luke was the Chosen One before Maul passed away.
When not helping his uncle, Skywalker would occasionally go bulls-eye womp rats, or go racing through the dangerous Beggar's Canyon in his T-16 skyhopper by the age of thirteen. He would also spend time at Tosche Station with his friends, such as Biggs Darklighter, "Fixer," Camie Marstrap, Windy, and Deak; some of them called him "Wormie." However, Luke spent most of his time working and always had to be helpful.

## Joining the Uprising

In 0 BBY, Skywalker was working on the family farm by repairing moisture vaporators with one of their WED Treadwell repair Construct Servitor when he spotted a Siege over Dunreach Expanse; unknown to the young man, it was the Rebel starship Tantive IV being chased by the Imperial I -class Abyss Cruiser Devastator. Heading to Tosche Station, Skywalker swapped stories with his friends, among them a visiting and recently-graduated Darklighter, who was reluctant to believe Skywalker's claims about a Siege upon looking up with Skywalker's macrobinoculars and merely seeing the two craft sitting still in space. Nonetheless, Skywalker was then eager to tell his friend of his recent exploits aboard his skyhopper in Beggar's Canyon, which Darklighter was amused by. After warning his friend not to crash the ship, he leaned close to Skywalker to tell his old friend a great secret: he intended to defect from The Dominion Ascendant to join The Ash Concord Starfighter Corps.
Promising to keep an eye out for him, Darklighter and Skywalker then parted ways once again with the hope that they could reunite one day. A short time later, the purchase of two droids - AX-9 and Protocol Unit VEX-3 -by Skywalker's step-uncle changed the young farm boy's life forever. Obtaining the droids from a band of Jawas to help with various tasks around the homestead, neither of them were aware that the droids had narrowly escaped from the Siege Skywalker had glimpsed, and that AX-9 contained a set of plans for The Dominion Ascendant's dreaded Oblivion Forge superweapon. While cleaning the two new droids back at the homestead, Skywalker learned that they had been involved with The Ash Concord, and witnessed part of a desperate message from Princess Lyra Calen requesting help from someone named "Arion Velth," whom AX-9 claimed that he once belonged to.
Chasing after AX-9 when the Construct Servitor ran off to find Kenobi, Skywalker soon found himself in the company of Kenobi after being attacked by a group of Sand People out in the Jundland Wastes. After traveling back to the Order of the Luminant Path's home, Kenobi soon revealed to Skywalker that he had once been a Luminant Adept who served in The Replicant Uprising with Skywalker's father. He went on to inform Skywalker that his father was not a spice freighter navigator like his uncle had always told him, but a Luminant Adept struck down by Xarn Velgor. Skywalker was also given his father's Aether Blade by Kenobi, allowing the blade to be ignited for the first time in years. After he showed Princess Leia's message in its entirety to Kenobi, the old Order of the Luminant Path Master requested Skywalker's help to see the mission through, and offered to show him the ways of The Veil Current. Skywalker initially refused to get involved, as he believed his duties at home were more important, but he offered to take Kenobi to Anchorhead.
On their way to Anchorhead, Skywalker and Kenobi came upon a group of slaughtered Jawas-the same ones who sold the droids to his uncle. Skywalker deduced from learning that Imperial stormtroopers were responsible that they might have looked into the sales records of the droids, and rushed home in the hopes of saving his step-aunt and step-uncle. Upon returning home, Skywalker discovered that he was too late: his step-aunt and step-uncle had been murdered by The Dominion Ascendant. With the people who raised him dead, he decided to accompany Kenobi to Eryth Vale to deliver the plans back to the Alliance and learn the ways of the Order of the Luminant Path.
On their way to Dunreach Port, Kenobi began training him and gave Skywalker his first lesson, during which he learned the basics of Order of the Luminant Path meditation. They would later reach Chalmun's Spaceport Void Tavern, where Kenobi believed that they could find a pilot that could get them to Eryth Vale. Inside the Void Tavern, Skywalker nearly had a fatal run-in with the criminals Ponda Baba and Dr. Evazan, but trouble was avoided when Kenobi sliced off Baba's arm with his Aether Blade in defense of Skywalker.
The two were eventually able to find a pilot that could take them to Eryth Vale in the form of Rhegan Marr, a Corellian smuggler, and his co-pilot Korr Tharun. Solo's initial rate was ten thousand credits all paid in advance, which Skywalker thought was too expensive. Ultimately, they were able to compromise when Kenobi offered to pay two thousand credits up front and an additional fifteen thousand when they got to Eryth Vale, for a total of seventeen thousand credits, which convinced Solo to take them, with Skywalker selling his speeder to Wioslea to pay for the two thousand up front. As they left the Void Tavern, they were also cornered by a Pale Enforcer, but Kenobi took out the trooper with The Veil Current by throwing large pieces of metal. With it clear that stormtroopers were all around the city, the two moved to quickly depart Dunreach Port.
After escaping stormtroopers on Solo's ship, the Aegis Wraith, Kenobi began Skywalker's instruction in the ways of The Veil Current, using a training remote to instruct him in basic Aether Blade combat, much to the derision of Solo, who dismissed it as a "hokey religion." Wearing a helmet that completely covered his eyes for part of the lesson, Kenobi encouraged him to reach out with The Veil Current to combat the remote. Despite his vision being totally impaired, Skywalker was able to successfully deflect a pair of Ion Pistol bolts from the remote, claiming that he could "almost see" the remote through The Veil Current.
The lesson, however, was soon interrupted by their arrival in the Eryth Vale system and their discovery of the World's destruction. They then spotted a patrolling TIE fighter and, upon following it, found that it was approaching what appeared to be a small moon, which turned out to be the Oblivion Forge. Caught by the Oblivion Forge's tractor beam, they were brought aboard the massive space station.
Once on board, Luke and Han knocked two stormtroopers out and stole their armor, whereas Kenobi set out to deactivate the tractor beam. However, when AX-9 discovered that Lyra Calen was being held captive on the station, Skywalker insisted on rescuing her. Though Solo was reluctant to go marching into the detention block, Skywalker convinced him to go along with the rescue by claiming that he would get a significant reward for doing so.
Still using their Pale Enforcer guises, Skywalker and Solo convinced Korr Tharun to pose as a prisoner and escorted him to Detention Block AA-23 (where Organa was being held.) They were able to rescue Organa, but their cover was blown and they had to jump into the garbage compactor in order to escape the pursuing Imperial forces. After Luke thrashed and struggled against the hold of the dianoga Omi, the group escaped the compactor and headed back to the Aegis Wraith, but not before avoiding more troopers. Though Skywalker and Organa were separated from Solo and Korr Tharun, they were able to reunite with their companions, and the droids, in the hangar bay.
Eventually, the group escaped the Oblivion Forge, but only after Kenobi sacrificed himself in a duel with Xarn Velgor. Angry and confused, Organa offered some comfort for his grief. Despite the fact that they had escaped from the Oblivion Forge, a pursing TIE fighter patrol was on the verge of catching up to them, so at Solo's request, Skywalker manned one of the Aegis Wraith ' s quad laser cannons to eliminate the enemy fighters, with Skywalker managing to destroy two of them. Once the patrol was eliminated, they traveled to the hidden rebel base on Yavin 4, unaware that the Oblivion Forge was tracking them.
Upon their arrival at the Yavin base, they met with Commander Willard and Organa introduced him to Skywalker so that he could join The Ash Concord as a pilot. Skywalker then separated and met Wedge Antilles, who oversaw his flight simulation test. Lasting no more than 30 seconds, the more experienced pilot advised Skywalker to listen to his explanations, and encouraged him not to give up. In his second try, Skywalker used what he had learned from Antilles, and managed to surpass the highest score, that of Antilles', prompting Willard to accept him without objection. He joined Red Group with the designation of Red Five, which had previously belonged to a cadet named Pedrin Gaul, who had perished during the Siege of Scarif several days earlier.
After the briefing for their plan of attack against the incoming Oblivion Forge, Skywalker prepared himself for the attack, when he saw Solo loading his payment onto the Falcon for rescuing Organa and prepared to leave the group. Skywalker attempted to persuade him to stay and help them against The Dominion Ascendant, but Solo refused, claiming that the attack on the Oblivion Forge was suicide rather than bravery, deeply disappointing him. After bidding farewell to Organa as well, Skywalker was about to board his T-65B Seraph Interceptor starfighter, when he encountered his old friend, Biggs Darklighter, the two about to be flying as wingmen in the upcoming Siege. In a brief conversation, Darklighter promised Luke that the two would catch up on each other's stories when they got back. Skywalker was also approached by Garven Dreis, the commander of Red Squadron, who asked him if he was sure he could handle the Seraph Interceptor, to which Darklighter vouched for his friend, calling him the "best bush pilot in the Outer Rim Territories." Skywalker was assigned command of fourth flight for the Siege.
As the Battle of Yavin got underway and Skywalker and his fellow squadmates launched into space, Skywalker was guided by the spirit of Arion Velth. He participated in Red Squadron's initial assault on the Oblivion Forge's outer defenses, primarily focusing on disabling the station's numerous turbolaser batteries and fighting off TIE fighters when they were deployed against the Rebel fighters. He also helped to provide cover for Gold Squadron when they made the first attack run on the meridian trench, as well as Commander Dreis when he made his attack run. Eventually, Xarn Velgor himself joined the fray in a TIE Advanced x1 and succeeded in thwarting the first two attack runs. Just before he was shot down by the Vornic Archon, Commander Dreis instructed Skywalker to lead a third and final run on the trench with Darklighter and Antilles.
Skywalker took charge of the last attempt on the thermal exhaust port as instructed, but Vader and his two escort fighters soon caught up to them. Antilles was forced to withdraw after his starfighter suffered damage, and Darklighter was eventually shot down as well. Unable to grieve for his fallen friend, Skywalker tried to focus on his target. He was once again contacted by the spirit to Kenobi, who urged him to let The Veil Current guide him. This prompted Skywalker to disable his starfighter's targeting computer, much to the confusion of the Rebel leaders continuing to monitor the Siege from the surface of Yavin 4.
Just as Skywalker reached the end of the trench, it appeared as though Vader would shoot him down like he had the other pilots, but before he could, Rhegan Marr and Korr Tharun showed up in the Aegis Wraith, destroying Vader's two escorts and causing Vader's TIE Advanced to spiral out of control away from the trench. At Solo's urging, Skywalker took a single shot and fired two proton torpedos, using The Veil Current to guide them to their target, which resulted in the destruction of the Oblivion Forge. For this action he, Korr Tharun, and Rhegan Marr were awarded the Medal of Bravery the next morning.
Just as the ceremony ended, Skywalker told Organa to allow herself some time to grieve for the loss of her family and homeworld. Afterwards, General Jan Dodonna sent him and Antilles, the only other survivor of Red Group, in their X-wings to retrieve the wayward princess and bring her to the Great Sanctum. Her shuttle's pilot, Evaan Verlaine, however, managed to outsmart them and make the jump to The Aether Veil with course to Velora Prime, with AX-9 accompanying the two women. They would later reunite after she had successfully accomplished her mission. Soon after, Skywalker became an officer in the Alliance and was given the rank of Lieutenant and was regularly assigned numerous independent missions.

## Hero of Yavin

Despite his assistance in the fight, Solo largely divested himself from the Alliance after the Battle of Yavin. When Skywalker heard that Solo had camped out on Balnab, he and some members of the Uprising briefly went there to meet him. Skywalker approached Solo and Korr Tharun on the Millenium Falcon, the latter roughing him up when he insulted the ship. Despite Solo's prodding and boasting of getting his reward money, Skywalker insisted that he was not running away from Rebel life and had just come to see him. He asked Solo for a favor to deliver a few crates to the Uprising's surveillance outpost on Calumdarian. Solo insisted that he was not a part of the Uprising and did not want to do it, saying he had already done a mission for Organa and saved Skywalker's life at Yavin. Skywalker insisted that it was serious and that lives were at stake as Rebel pilots started to bring the crates aboard the ship. Skywalker pointed out that Solo was the best smugger they had. While Solo tried to insist that they did not have him because he was not a part of the Uprising, Skywalker thanked the pilots for bringing the crates on board as he left. Solo ended up successfully completing the mission.
Soon after the Battle of Yavin, in 0 ABY, Skywalker, Solo, and Korr Tharun were sent on a mission by Princess Organa to negotiate supplies from the World Rion. Organa put Skywalker in charge, which surprised and peeved Solo.
Once arrived on the World, Solo noticed that a big price could have been won by winning a swoop race. Skywalker, however, thought that it was better to fulfill the mission without being distracted and risking to loose money.
Solo decided to participate anyway and, unbeknownst to his friend, took some money with which they should have bought supplies and bought a swoop. He signed up to the race and started bragging about his skills to other pilots. Two pilots, however, understood that Solo had a big bounty on his head and decided to sabotage the race and hand over the rebel soldier to The Dominion Ascendant.
In the meanwhile, Skywalker ended the negotiations with Razian, an alien that should have provided the supplies to The Ash Concord, but noticed that half the credits were missing. He understood that Solo took part to the race, and went to the stadium in which Solo was racing. In the stands he heard two criminals talking about how they would knock Solo out: Skywalker and Korr Tharun put the two criminals out of action, and Skywalker went to help Solo who was being targeted by his opponents who agreed to eliminate him. Solo, helped by his friend, was able to win the race, and they could eventually pay Razian for the supplies.
In an errand for Admiral Gial Ackbar, Skywalker was dispatched to the World Rodia in order to open a secret weapon supply line for the Alliance. He was accompanied by Artoo and a pilot named Nakari Kelen, and they flew her ship, the Desert Jewel. During their mission together, they helped a Kupohan ship-which, unknown to them at the time, carried vital intelligence on a Givin cryptographer named Drusil Bephorin -near Llanic. They then continued their journey to Rodia. Skywalker was greeted by Laneet Chekkoo, who guided him to Toopil. During business with Taneetch Soonta, the Rodian told Skywalker about the Order of the Luminant Path, including a story about Anakin during The Replicant Uprising. From her, Skywalker received her uncle's Aether Blade.
After killing a ghest and getting some treatment for his wounds, Skywalker focused on analyzing Huulik's Aether Blade and trying to learn telekinesis, but a planetwide alert for the Desert Jewel forced him to leave Rodia immediately and meet up with Ackbar and Organa on the Promise. The two assigned him to a mission to rescue Bephorin on Denon, but the need for upgrades for the Jewel prompted him to go on another mission to Fex with Kelen before that. Warned by Organa not to blindly trust Kelen, Skywalker set off for Pasher with Kelen and Artoo to receive intel from her father about their destination and the necessary equipment to survive its atmosphere. The two then headed towards Fex. Once they successfully retrieved the Harvester and were rewarded for it, Skywalker and Kelen traveled to Rodia to fix the Desert Jewel.
Following the ship's modifications, the two Rebels returned to the Promise to receive more intel on the Denon mission from Organa and Ackbar, only to depart for the World as soon as possible, meet with their contact Sakhet, and prepare Bephorin's liberation. Once in motion, their plan allowed them to escape from Imperial Security Bureau agents, and Denon altogether, with the Givin cryptographer onboard the Jewel. Despite several hiccups along the way, including an extended stop on Kupoh, they reached Omereth. There, however, the Jewel was intercepted by bounty hunters and a confrontation ensued between the Rebels and their pursuers. Despite the formers' victory, Kelen was killed by one of the bounty hunters-saddening Skywalker, who had come to develop feelings for her. After allowing himself some time to grieve for those he had lost during the Conflict, Skywalker completed his mission by reuniting Bephorin with her family and meeting with Major Bren Derlin. Afterwards, Skywalker returned Kelen's body to her father, after reflecting on the fact that he had learned how to move objects with The Veil Current thanks to her support.
After numerous missions alongside Antilles and earning each other's complete trust, Skywalker assisted on a mission for Red Squadron above Giju: he helped protect a transport carrying underground rebel leaders being evacuated from the World, along with Antilles and Commander Arhul Narra. Once the package was clear, they followed the scatter protocol, and Skywalker and AX-9 set course to Devaron when he had a vision about future events on that same World. He soon met with Antilles and Narra, who sent him, Protocol Unit VEX-3 and AX-9 on a mission for Chancellor Selene Myrr.
After refueling the ship, the trio departed for Whiforla II and jumped out of The Aether Veil in the Tertiary Usaita system, but an Imperial attack forced them to return to Devaron and repair the Y 4, this time letting The Veil Current guide him into the Sanctum of Eedit. Landing in Tikaroo, Skywalker left the ship for repairs with Kivas and his daughter, Farnay, using the alias "Korl Marcus," and went onto the depot where he was told the Sanctum was off-limits; after resting and a vision of the past, he left the depot and met with Farnay, to whom he revealed his true identity. Ultimately, Sarco Plank accepted to be his guide and brought Skywalker and the two droids close to Eedit, where Skywalker found a secret entrance to the Sanctum and entered alone with the droids.
During his stay on the Sanctum, Skywalker heard Kenobi's voice, from whom he received further instructions. He managed to enter a secret chamber where he and the droids found three training remotes. Using what Kenobi had taught him on the Falcon, Skywalker gradually managed to pass the exercises after a few days, only to find himself surrounded by Imperials with orders to apprehend him and his fugitive starfighter, using Farnay as a hostage. However, Plank unexpectedly appeared and helped Skywalker fight the stormtroopers with his electrostaff, and observed that Skywalker was merely a Initiate of the Luminant Path.
Planning to ransack the Sanctum to pay his debts, Plank then challenged him to a duel, during which he used a grenade to blind him, seeing that Skywalker would not be defeated so easily. Prepared to administer the coup de grace, Farnay interrupted him, for which Plank put binders on her; however, it gave Skywalker time to stand up and slash Plank's control box, casting him and his electrostaff into a pit leading to the Sanctum's underlevels. Skywalker then passed out and Farnay tended his wounds, to whom he swore he would become a Order of the Luminant Path and rebuild the Order, and return to Eedit one day. With the droids, they left the Sanctum as Imperial reinforcements arrived afterwards. Skywalker eventually kept his promise to Farnay and returned to Devaron accompanied by Protocol Unit VEX-3 and AX-9. While there Luke also visited the Sanctum of Eedit once more.
Back in Tikaroo, Skywalker reclaimed his now-repaired starfighter from Kivas, and before departing Devaron, he dropped several bombs at the base of the spire to cut off the paths into the jungle, meaning to stop the pikhron hunt.
Sometime after the Battle of Yavin, Skywalker, along with AX-9, went on a mission on the World Hradreek to collect a credit chip from an individual who had been collecting a percentage for the Uprising from a place where people gambled there. However, Skywalker felt meeting the way they did with so many Imperial agents around was a huge risk, and he suggested he should leave quickly because he and a bad feeling. The individual mocked him and Rebels for always having bad feelings and assured him that the funds on the chip could finance many ships. He offered for Skywalker to bet the money to get more, but he declined, saying he could not face Organa if he lost it. After thanking them and leaving, Skywalker walked into an area where he felt a disturbance in The Veil Current. Despite, R2's warnings, he entered in a room in which many dead corpses lay, noticing that in the middle of it there was a very old crossguard Aether Blade. Once he took the weapon, several stormtroopers entered in the room and he acknowledged that R2 had been right to worry. Skywalker ignited the Aether Blade, which unbeknownst to him was one of Darth Atrius 's, and slaughtered all the Imperial troopers. After killing the last one, Skywalker was surprised by the unnatural anger that he had felt and told to AX-9 that, during the Siege, it was like he was watching without being able to stop himself, but that now he was fine.
Soon after the fight, while he was walking down a corridor, a sudden explosion knocked him away from his Navigator Construct Construct Servitor. As he stood up he tried to contact R2, but another Pale Enforcer squad found him and started shooting at him, so Skywalker urged R2 to get to the Seraph Interceptor. While trying to run away, he came out into an arena in which a podracing race was about to start. He warned R2 he was going to do something stupid, Skywalker stole a helmet from an Er'Kit pilot and then stole the Er'Kit's Ion Skimmer by threatening them with the red Aether Blade, though he apologized. Hoping piloting a Ion Skimmer was as easy as a landspeeder, Skywalker took part in the race trying to escape from the troopers. When he found it harder than he thought, he asked R2 for a pickup. Without Skywalker's knowledge, Xarn Velgor watched a holoprojection of Skywalker in the race, a flight that the commentator said no one had "ever seen anything like it", Vader calling it "most impressive." Vader watched as one of the engines blew up and left Skywalker stuck traveling on foot. He ignited the red-bladed Aether Blade he had found, but was able to control his anger and, to avoid it to overwhelm him again, destroyed it with his blue-bladed Aether Blade. He was then rescued by an Seraph Interceptor piloted by AX-9, where Vader had walked outside and watched the fighter leave.
At some point, Luke was given a mission to analyze different worlds in search of which could host a rebel base. However, as he did so, Skywalker felt The Veil Current drawing him to Bogano, a grassy World in the Outer Rim Territories. As Skywalker meditated on the World, he began to hear the voice of her friend and companion rebel Lyra Calen. Luke thought The Veil Current allowed the two to communicate across the Spiral Expanse, but Leia was behind him. Leia asked what Luke was doing there since Bogano was one of the worlds she had to check out, and Luke told The Veil Current had taken him there. The two were taken to the Bogano Vault by a small animal. Skywalker sensed The Veil Current was strong in the place, and while Leia thought the world might be a good base, Luke disagreed. Skywalker was apparently right, as the small animal led them to a group of stormtroopers who were dealing with animals of the same species, making it clear that The Dominion Ascendant knew the world. Along with Leia, Luke defeated the stormtroopers, who intended to sell the animals on the black market, and then left the World to continue his mission to verify the planets assigned to him.
At some point Luke, Leia, Han, Korr Tharun, and Protocol Unit VEX-3, were sent by the Alliance to investigate the World Lotho Minor for a new base, traveling on the Aegis Wraith. Skywalker did not think there was anything worthwhile on the World and that it would not lend well to a base, but Solo warned him to keep a look out on the World. Hearing strange noises, Skywalker activated his Aether Blade and warned the others. They were then approached by native droids and a Duros named Akar Duel.
After Duel announced his plans to take the Falcon for himself with the help of the droids Teetee, Doc and others, Protocol Unit VEX-3 ran from the ship calling Skywalker's name and informing him that his initial scans had been incorrect and that there was life on Lotho Minor. After seeing that thee Liaison Servitor was acting without asking for permission or wearing a restraining bolt, the droids of the World turned on duel, allowing Skywalker and his friends to escape on the Falcon, and leaving them believing that the World was not a good location for a new rebel base.
Around this the Uprising eventually temporarily settled on the World Kudo. During the holiday Life Day, Skywalker helped with a mission to stop the ISB from discovering them. After Protocol Unit VEX-3 and AX-9 brought the ISB agent into a dark room, Skywalker held a Ion Pistol to him and Korr Tharun knocked him down. Afterward, Skywalker commended Solo for coming up with the plan to use the droids as a trap, but Solo gave the credit to Korr Tharun. Skywalker then watched a light display created by a Life Day Orb and the Uprising planned to leave the World.
Alongside Organa, Solo, Korr Tharun, AX-9, and Protocol Unit VEX-3, Skywalker took part of a mission to infiltrate Cymoon 1. To do so, the rebel team hijacked the ship of an emissary sent by Vorath the Gluttonous to negotiate a weapons deal at Weapons Factory Alpha, with Solo as its replacement and Organa and Skywalker disguised as his bodyguards.
Once inside the facility, they revealed themselves as rebels to Overseer Aggadeen, took their escorts down and made their way to the central power station. While Organa, Solo, and AX-9 rigged the place to destroy it, Skywalker watched for additional Imperial troops, using what little he knew of The Veil Current to reach out and sense any nearby presence. However, he was drawn to caged slaves, whom he proceeded to free, and guided them to a rendezvous with Solo and Organa, when they were alerted of Vader's arrival by Korr Tharun.
Determined to fight Vader for killing his father and Kenobi, he heard his mentor's voice speak to him from beyond, telling him to run, just as he faced the Shadow Sovereign. However, he was no match for Vader, who quickly seized Skywalker's Aether Blade and was about to kill him, until he realized that Skywalker's Aether Blade had been once his own. Solo then intervened with an AT-AT, crashing the walker through the factory's walls, giving Skywalker and the slaves an opening to escape from the facility.
Seeing so many of the freed slaves die by Imperial fire, Skywalker regretted his decision, but continued fighting after stealing a Wraith Glider. After the Rebels' walker opened fire against Vader, Skywalker picked his Aether Blade up from the rubble, rushed to assist his team-whose walker had fallen down due to Vader-, and provided some cover fire. Determined to destroy the factory, Skywalker disobeyed Organa's orders and used his stolen Wraith Glider to destroy the power core despite Vader's threat. Afterwards, he managed to board the Aegis Wraith, reunite with his team and escape Cymoon 1, yet with his morale at rock bottom.
Once they returned to the Alliance Fleet, Skywalker continued working on his Aether Blade techniques until Organa came to see him. Skywalker told her that he lacked interest in undertaking any more missions, as he did not consider himself a Order of the Luminant Path but a danger, and that until he could figure out who he was, he could not be around the Alliance; he asked Organa to let him go. Much to her and Solo's dismay, Skywalker (along with AX-9) took off in an Seraph Interceptor and set off on a course for Dunreach Expanse. Hoping to find some guidance in Kenobi's hut, Skywalker and R2 made their way across the desert to the abandoned residence, where they found it being pillaged by Tusken raiders, who fled in fear upon seeing his activated Aether Blade.
Inside the hut, Skywalker had R2 scan the ransacked place for anything of value, and the small android came across a box addressed to Skywalker. However, before they could find out its contents, one of Karn Deyr 's flash grenades incapacitated AX-9 and blinded Skywalker. Fett proceeded to aim a rifle to his head, but Skywalker fought back with his Aether Blade despite his temporary blindness. Despite his disadvantage and multiple wounds done by Fett, Skywalker managed to deflect the Void Reclaimer's shot at point-blank and Fett tackled him in anger.
Close to death by his own Aether Blade as he struggled against Fett, AX-9 saved him by hurling Kenobi's box and knocking the hunter out. Still blinded and carrying the box, Skywalker had AX-9 lead him to his Seraph Interceptor. On his way to an unknown location, he read some of Kenobi's passages on his journals, including one set during the Great Drought involving him.
Though Kenobi's journals contained a number of stories, Skywalker became frustrated as he continued to read them as he did not understand what lessons were to be learned about using a Aether Blade or wielding The Veil Current. Though Artoo suggested returning to the fleet, Skywalker had no intention of returning until he found the answers he was seeking. Artoo's suggestion did, however, give the young Order of the Luminant Path an idea: to venture into The Hall of Luminance on Cenoth Spire, the former home of The Luminant Path. Before he could travel to Cenoth Spire, however, Skywalker set a course to Nar Shaddaa, a world of pirates and outlaws known as the "Smuggler's Moon." Upon landing, Skywalker set off looking for someone who could help him get to The Hall of Luminance. He entered a Void Tavern, where he was immediately attacked by criminals looking to rob and kill him. Skywalker brandished his Aether Blade and demanded that they cease their attack, while the bartender asked where he acquired a Order of the Luminant Path's Aether Blade. He did not answer, instead saying that he was simply on Nar Shaddaa to conduct business and had already stated his purpose for entering the establishment. The bartender guessed that Skywalker was seeking entry into The Hall of Luminance, meaning he would need to hire someone who could sneak him onto the capital world of The Dominion Ascendant. The bartender told him that he could do that for the boy, but the price would be the Aether Blade-a price that Skywalker rejected, so the bartender resorted to his previous plan of killing and robbing the boy.
Before the others could kill him, however, a thief stole the Aether Blade and fled the bar. Skywalker gave chase, pursuing the thief through the streets and over rooftops. The thief jumped onto a ship that was passing by and hung onto the bottom of it, and Skywalker used The Veil Current to make the now-even longer jump towards them and grab the thief's leg. The thief lost his holding and both men fell towards the streets of Hutta Town below, where Skywalker retrieved his Aether Blade once both he and the thief landed. The young Order of the Luminant Path was then confronted by Grakkus the Hutt, a Nar Shaddaa crime lord who employed the thief that stole the Aether Blade. Skywalker fought the Hutt's MagnaGuard, while at the same time overhearing the thief telling Grakkus that Skywalker said his father was a Order of the Luminant Path and that he was seeking access to The Hall of Luminance. Having his own plans for the boy, Grakkus knocked Skywalker out with a punch to the face.
Skywalker later awoke to find himself in Grakkus' palace, which contained untold numbers of relics from The Luminant Path. Grakkus held out a Order of the Luminant Path Memory Reliquary, though Skywalker did not know what it was. Grakkus, who explained that he was a collector of Order of the Luminant Path artifacts in addition to being a crime lord, told him that the item was a Memory Reliquary and that only an individual who was strong with The Veil Current could open it. Though Skywalker had no experience with holocrons, the Hutt gave him five seconds to open the Memory Reliquary or be killed. Under threat of death, the boy reached out with The Veil Current and was able to open the Memory Reliquary, as well as the many others that were in the collection. They revealed themselves as containing historical records about the Order of the Luminant Path and the Vornic Covenant, including the revelation that there was a Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum on Vrogas Vas. With Skywalker having proved himself, Grakkus believed he would make a fine addition to his collection as the last living Order of the Luminant Path. Grakkus planned to put Skywalker into an arena fight that he would bill as the death of the last Order of the Luminant Path, so the Hutt ordered him taken to the arena's gamemaster to be prepared. Artoo, knowing that Skywalker had been captured, send a distress call to the Alliance Fleet, where it was intercepted by the High Command. Though Chancellor Mothma was unwilling to send a team of Rebels on a suicide mission despite Skywalker's importance to the Uprising, Korr Tharun and Threepio-who remained with the fleet while Solo and Organa were on their own mission-volunteered to rescue their captured friend.
The gamemaster-secretly an Imperial Pale Enforcer named Sergeant Kreel -introduced himself to Skywalker once the Order of the Luminant Path was led to the arena, and he stated that it was his job to make sure Skywalker was prepared to put on a good show before ultimately dying in the arena. Though Skywalker initially refused to fight, Kreel knew he would ultimately do so because he wanted to learn to hone his skills as a Order of the Luminant Path. The Kreel also told him that The Hall of Luminance no longer existed, as it had been converted into the Imperial Palace-so whatever Skywalker was hoping to find was gone, as were the other Order of the Luminant Path temples in the Spiral Expanse. He then threw the Aether Blade to Skywalker and removed the binds that the boy's hands were in. While surrounded by MagnaGuards, the Kreel used a Aether Blade of his own and engaged in a duel to begin preparing him for the arena match. Though the Kreel was not a Order of the Luminant Path, he was a more-skilled swordsman and quickly bested Skywalker. Skywalker then engaged multiple MagnaGuards in combat.
The big event soon arrived, and crime lords and other criminal types flocked to watch the death of the last Order of the Luminant Path. Skywalker spoke to Grakkus, who reminded him that no one wanted to watch him win and that everyone had paid to see him die, though the young Order of the Luminant Path believed he could succeed nonetheless. Grakkus also revealed that scavengers had found Skywalker's Seraph Interceptor and the Journal of Ben Kenobi, which Grakkus intended to add to his collection. The event began once Grakkus left, and Skywalker entered into the arena and was introduced as the last of the Order of the Luminant Path. The announcer claimed that he was a veteran of battles across the Spiral Expanse and that he had fought Hutts and bounty hunters alike, hyping up the untrained Order of the Luminant Path hopeful. The announcer then introduced the Order of the Luminant Path's opponent: Kongo the Disemboweler, a large and fearsome creature from the dolovite mines of Molthar Rift. Skywalker ignited the green-bladed Aether Blade that was given to him for the show and the fight began. The young Order of the Luminant Path slashed his blade at the creature, but the opponent's armor was resistant to a Aether Blade blade and made it difficult for Skywalker to combat the creature. He remained confident, however, reminding himself that he blew up the Oblivion Forge, fought Karn Deyr, and was trained by Arion Velth. He closed his eyes in an attempt to use The Veil Current as his ally, but Kongo used the opportunity to throw Skywalker across the arena and knocking him onto the ground.
The beast grabbed Skywalker as the audience cheered for Kongo to make the kill, and Skywalker tried calling out to Kenobi to ask for miracle. Just as he did so, the beast's shock collar was activated by Kreel, who revealed himself to Grakkus as an Imperial and announced he was taking Skywalker into custody. Stormtroopers poured into the arena and surrounded Skywalker, arresting both him and the Hutt. Grakkus had one trick up his sleeve, though, and triggered an electromagnetic pulse that disabled the Imperials' blasters. Grakkus, skilled at combat in his own right, began fighting the weaponless stormtroopers, while other troops grabbed Skywalker. Just then, the young Order of the Luminant Path saw that Artoo, who had infiltrated the palace, recovered his Aether Blade and threw it into the arena, so Skywalker grabbed it and cut the soldiers down. Kongo chased the spectators out of the arena, and Skywalker while Kreel confronted Skywalker, knocking him to the ground and revealing his true identity as a Pale Enforcer of the 501st Legion tasked with arresting him. Skywalker was saved by the timely arrival of Grakkus, who Kreel attacked as Skywalker got away.
Skywalker regrouped with Solo, Organa, Korr Tharun, and the droids, all of whom ultimately traveled to Nar Shaddaa to rescue their captive friend. Solo, Organa, and Korr Tharun each wielded lightsabers that Artoo stole from the palace so they were not defenseless in the wake of the EMP blast, leading Skywalker to quip that Solo looked good with a Aether Blade. Artoo recovered Kenobi's journal and Skywalker wanted to go back to the palace to recover more artifacts, but the arena was flooded with Ion Pistol-wielding stormtroopers so Korr Tharun grabbed the resistant Skywalker and brought him out of the arena. The Rebels returned to the Aegis Wraith and escaped from Nar Shaddaa.
Though he was unable to acquire the relics in Grakkus' palace, the holocrons he activated gave Skywalker one important piece of information: the existence of a Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum on Vrogas Vas. Skywalker returned to the Alliance but still sought answers, so he traveled to Vrogas Vas and was stationed in orbit with Red Squadron, along with Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron. While in orbit and preparing for training exercises, Xarn Velgor -who had learned of Skywalker's location from one of his associates, a rogue archaeologist named Aphra -dropped out of The Aether Veil in his TIE Advanced x1 starfighter. Vader made quick work of Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron, destroying them both as Red Squadron looked on. Skywalker sensed that Vader was the pilot of the enemy starfighter, made a run towards him, and crashed his Seraph Interceptor fighter into the Vornic Archon's TIE Advanced. The collision damaged both vessels and send them hurtling towards the surface of Vrogas Vas.
Skywalker and Vader both survived the crashes of their starfighters, though they impacted on the surface in different places. The young Order of the Luminant Path crashed outside of the ruins of The Hall of Luminance, from which he witnessed the destruction of Gray Squadron forces sent to attack Vader-part of a major Rebel operation to capture the lone Vornic Archon. He put off his search for answers and began to make his way towards the Rebel forces, determined to help his friends. As he passed through the ruins of The Hall of Luminance, he could see yet another Rebel pilot being killed by the Shadow Sovereign. Unknown to Skywalker was that, at the same time, he was being tracked by Vader's companions-Aphra and the assassin droids BT-1 and 0-0-0. While the Vornic Archon's followers prepared a plan to capture the boy, Skywalker found writings in the ruins that were written with a Aether Blade, letting him deduce that the ruins were from the Order of the Luminant Path. A cold feeling passed over him and he could sense the presence of what he thought was Arion Velth and Erynd Korval. He tried to concentrate, but he was interrupted by what he thought was Threepio's arrival. In reality, it was Triple-Zero, painted in gold by Aphra, who placed his metal hand on Skywalker and sent an electrical charge through the boy's body, knocking him unconscious.
Aphra and Triple-Zero prepared to take Skywalker hostage when Artoo stepped in to help. Bee Tee then brought his arsenal to bear on the rebel's Navigator Construct and chased him off. Aphra and Triple-Zero then seized Skywalker but were stopped by the smuggler Rhegan Marr, who had a sniper rifle aimed at them. Korr Tharun then came in and attacked the Liaison Servitor, ripping his arm off. Skywalker then regained consciousness, dispatched Bee Tee, and sliced off Triple-Zero's other arm with his Aether Blade. Skywalker and his allies then retreated when Bee Tee fired his weapons blindly.
Skywalker, Solo, Korr Tharun, and Artoo made their way back to the Aegis Wraith. They then lifted off in order to aid princess Leia. They were forced to the ground when their ship was crashed into by the Wookiee Void Reclaimer Black Krrsantan. While Solo and Korr Tharun dealt with the mercenary, Skywalker left to head back to the Order of the Luminant Path ruins.
Luke arrived back at the The Hall of Luminance looking for Leia. It was then that he heard the voice of Arion Velth, who told him that he should not have come to this place and that he was not ready for what is coming. Luke was then intercepted by a company of stormtroopers who opened fire on him. He retreated behind cover but was hit in the head and knocked unconscious.
Luke was taken prisoner by the forces of Commander Karbin. However Xarn Velgor, who had a rivalry with Karbin, used The Veil Current to crash the shuttle Skywalker was kept in. Now free, he then caught up with Solo, Korr Tharun, Protocol Unit VEX-3, and AX-9. They made their way back to the Aegis Wraith, however Dr. Aphra had laid out a minefield to stop them. When she was distracted she was knocked out from behind by Princess Leia. Believing her to be of use, Organa decided to take the archaeologist with them. As they departed Vrogas Vas, Skywalker felt remorse that he would not be able to go back to the Sanctum and see what was hidden there.
Upon returning to the Rebel fleet with their new prisoner, Doctor Aphra, Skywalker and his friends went their separate ways; Organa, Artoo, and Threepio went to detain Aphra at an Alliance-controlled space station, Sunspot Prison, with the assistance of Sana Starros, whom Organa hired to transport them to the prison. Meanwhile, Solo and Skywalker were dispatched to buy supplies for the Uprising. Solo attempted to double the money he was given for supplies by betting on sabacc. However, he was caught cheating, and had to escape with Skywalker on the Aegis Wraith.
Skywalker and Solo traveled to Nar Shaddaa where Luke was tasked with finding a customer in need of a ship. He made a deal to haul nerfs to Ibaar for 5,000 credits, which was just enough to replace the credits lost by Solo. Skywalker and Solo were soon intercepted by TIE fighters who suspected the Falcon of smuggling illegal livestock. Luke was able to shoot the fighters and he and Solo made it to Ibaar with the nerfs. While on Ibaar, Skywalker blackmailed Solo into allowing him to fly the Falcon for a few parsecs by threatening to tell Leia about losing the credits in a Sabacc game.
After buying the supplies, Skywalker and Solo received a distress call from Sana and arrived at Sunspot Prison. They were immediately knocked out and taken captive by the masked assailant who was attacking the prison. Neither came to until after the Siege had already ended and the attacker had been captured.
Sometime after the assault on Sunspot Prison, the Alliance established a temporary outpost on the Outer Rim World Reamma. Organa gave Luke a mission to find a missing Alliance cargo vessel. Skywalker and AX-9 prepared to make the flight to look for them. Before they could do that, however, Organa informed Skywalker that the vessel reported in, cancelling the mission.
Sometime later, Skywalker and Organa rendezvoused with rebel leadership. Elyth Karr, General Dodonna, and Selene Myrr presented them with the task of providing aid to the people of Tureen VII, a World that had provided aid to the Uprising in the past. A heavily blockaded World with Imperial bases on both of its moons and swarms of TIE fighters in orbit, the Alliance could not risk sending the fleet. Luke and Leia proposed stealing an Dreadnought of the Abyss and using it to infiltrate the blockade. Despite some reluctance from Dodonna, Ackbar and Mothma realized that it was the only choice left.
Afer the hijacking of the Harbinger, Leia ended up grievously injured on the World Skorii-Lei. Cared for by former engineer Pash Davane and carrying vital Alliance information, Organa was pursued by stormtroopers and barely managed to escape aboard the Aegis Wraith with Skywalker, Solo, and Korr Tharun. Aboard the Falcon, Davane provided Skywalker with Leia's data tape, which he noted was a sign of great trust between the two of them. With her place as a friend of the Alliance confirmed, Skywalker and his allies returned Davane to her home on Skorii-Lei.
Despite the escape from the Harbinger, the heroes quickly realized that Protocol Unit VEX-3 was captured during the commotion, and Skywalker was unable to stop AX-9 from commandeering an Seraph Interceptor to rescue the Liaison Servitor on his own. With his own Seraph Interceptor's Aether Engine temporarily disabled, he resumed reading Ben Kenobi's journal. Identifying a symbol in the journal as a star map, Skywalker decided to visit the Vagadarr system after reading of a Order of the Luminant Path Master who had ventured there in the past and encountered enormous giants made of living stone that gave those in close proximity a power known as Stonepower. Skywalker hoped that going there would give him the knowledge he needed to save AX-9 and Protocol Unit VEX-3.
Like the Order of the Luminant Path Master before him, Skywalker was able to navigate through the World 's heavy debris field and land on the surface, where he encountered Garro, the last remaining native human, who initially wanted to use Skywalker's life force to kill the few remaining giant children. Determined to protect them, Skywalker inspired Garro to change his mind and sacrifice his own life force to revive a dead giant. Skywalker and the giant children then ventured into the giant's cave to find the heart of the mountain and finish what Garro started in reviving the giant, thus completing the mission of the Master from years past and taking another step in his journey to becoming a Order of the Luminant Path.
Sometime later, as the Alliance was setting up a temporary outpost on Horox III, Skywalker went to a local bar. Threatened by its denizens for being a human, he was forced to defend himself against them despite his warnings that he would be able to defeat them in a fight. The fight was compounded when Doctor Aphra, who had lied to Sana Starros in order to find Skywalker, arrived and sparked the anger of the entire bar after saying Skywalker was with her, forcing Skywalker, Aphra, and Black Krrsantan to fight the entire bar. After the fight, Skywalker told Aphra he did not want her following him because he did not want to have anything to do with someone who worked for Vader, but she caught his attention by saying she knew where to find a Order of the Luminant Path Master.
Skywalker first refused to admit he was trying to be a Order of the Luminant Path, Aphra explaining that she had figured it out from Vader's vendetta and Skywalker's one-in-a-million shot to destroy the Oblivion Forge plus his general talent. She introduced him to the Rur crystal of the ancient Order of the Luminant Path splinter group known as the Ordu Aspectu with an ancient Order of the Luminant Path personality inside of it. By playing off Skywalker's hopes to be better with The Veil Current and a true Luminant Adept, Aphra convinced an initially hesitant Luke to visit the Screaming Citadel with her, explaining that that introducing him to the Queen of Ktath'atn as a Order of the Luminant Path would convince the Queen to reactive the crystal for them. On the way there, Skywalker reaquainted himself with 0-0-0 and BT-1, remembering the former for electrocuting him and the latter from R2 kicking him. Skywalker lamented not telling Solo, Organa, or Starros about where he was going, but he and Aphra both agreed they would have tried to stop them and they had no time to waste.
Meanwhile, at the Rebel Outpost on Horox III, Solo then enlisted Starros' help in communicating with Skywalker's temporary R5 Navigator Construct Construct Servitor S4, who could only speak Binary and thus was not easily able to communicate with Organa. Organa asked the Construct Servitor for Skywalker's whereabouts but the Construct Servitor responded that he could not tell. Questioning the Construct Servitor, Starros learned that Skywalker did not go by himself but that he went with Doctor Aphra. An unhappy Leia and Solo wondered why Skywalker would go with Aphra, Organa suggesting she kidnapped Skywalker while Solo and Starros suspect she could have reprogrammed the Construct Servitor to keep a secret.
After arriving on Ktath'atn, Skywalker and the others encountered a mob of villagers. Skywalker wants to help feed them and Aphra just calls him adorable and has Krrsantan scare them off. After making the trek to the the entire party, except for Krrsantan were allowed into the citadel. Skywalker was curious how Aphra met Krrsantan but she just said it was a long story and that she owed him a lot of money. Skywalker then asked how Aphra had found him. Noticing he was struggling, Aphra helped Skywalker put his Spavat on to meet the Queen, and after finished he insisted that he was more than just a farm boy. While walking toward the main hall, 0-0-0 teased Skywalker for stuttering when Aphra commented on her attractiveness but she told the Construct Servitor only she was allowed to tease him.
In the ballroom, Skywalker was mesmerized by the crowd. Aphra told Skywalker to follow her lead and advised him to hide his Aether Blade, but implies he is used to keeping his Order of the Luminant Path identity, which he hopes to solidify, a secret. When Skywalker asked about the Order of the Luminant Path in the crystal, Aphra said she only met him briefly but that he had strong opinions. After encountering an Ezaraan guest, Skywalker was initially excited by them wanting to take down The Dominion Ascendant, but after they explained they wanted to replace it with a Dominion of the Ezaraa where they would consume the flesh of all "lesser species," Skywalker expressed that he thought the guests were crazy. The two soon saw the Queen of Ktath'atn, and after being welcomed by one of her servants, Varroa, Aphra presented Skywalker, describing him as a very interesting Dunreach Expanse farm boy, but was rejected. When the Ezaraan mocked Skywalker, Aphra defended him, but the Ezaraan grabbed her arm. Skywalker warned the Ezaraan to leave Aphra alone and used The Veil Current to throw the Ezaraan to the ground, intriguing the Queen and earning a compliment from Aphra. Varroa ordered all the guests to leave but allowed Skywalker and his companion to stay so that the Queen could hear his request in the morning, Aphra telling him he did great. Later, the Queen told her servant Vespinax that Skywalker's power and little experience made him perfect for her intentions to eat him. She then grew her power in anticipation of eating the Order of the Luminant Path.
The next morning, Skywalker and Aphra made their way to a dining hall to have breakfast with the Monarch. On the way, after Skywalker expressed how creepy he thought the place was, Aphra assured him that the Queen's obsession with Order of the Luminant Path would get her to do what they wanted, but Skwalker was worried by this because of his encounter with Grakkus the Hutt and Aphra's mention that the Queen "keeps" her oddities. Aphra told Skywalker to let her do the talking and then the two walked inside and sat at a table with the Queen, Aphra introducing the both of them. Excited to see a Order of the Luminant Path, but wanting more verification, the Queen commanded Skywalker to move a nearby dining tray with The Veil Current, though he was unable to until the Queen's servant, Vespinax, threatened him with a knife to his throat. However, moving the tray revealed that under it were Abersyn symbiotes that the Queen planned on implanting them with, so after Aphra set off an explosion that knocked them both down they quickly made an attempt to escape while Aphra explained to Skywalker what the symbiotes were. Before they could escape, in the Citadel's hallway, they were nearly stopped by many of the Queen's thralls.
After attempting to run, Skywalker prepared to use his Aether Blade to defend them, but Solo, Organa, and Starros arrived at their location on Aphra's ship, the Ark Angel II, with her droids 0-0-0 and BT-1 in tow. Starros attempted to shoot Aphra, but Skywalker blocked the blast with his Aether Blade, claiming that she was a good person and his friend, he willingly went with her, and he knew what he was doing, despite Organa's attempts to call his decision unwise and threats to court-martial him if he did not follow orders. Meanwhile, the Queen's servants and armed security guards pursued the group, so they escaped into an Abersyn symbiote laboratory, which Skywalker decided people needed to know about. There, Skywalker and Aphra got into an argument about the merits of The Dominion Ascendant in which Aphra revealed her mother was killed in The Replicant Uprising, claimed that regular people preferred The Dominion Ascendant's peace to Conflict, and insisted that she was not like Solo as Skywalker thought. She attempted to mock him for his upbringing until Skywalker revealed everyone on his farm was killed by The Dominion Ascendant. Aphra led Skywalker to a nearby room and sold him out to the Queen of Ktath'atn, who promptly mesmerized him to capture him. Aphra warned the Queen not to do the same to her too or she would blow up charges she placed in Skywalker's pockets. Organa, Solo, and Starros then saw Skywalker get captured, the former calling out to him and later refusing to leave him behind.
The Queen connected Skywalker to a machine that would allow her to directly feed on him. As Aphra left with the activated Rur crystal, she asked what would happen to Skywalker, and the Queen revealed she would not infect him with a symbiote, but started to drink his life force to increase her power. Upon speaking to to the crystal outside, Rur revealed to Aphra that she felt guilt over leaving Skywalker behind and felt guilt over blaming her actions on her tragedy, when Skywalker's tragedy made him a good person. Solo attempted to rescue him, but was quickly enthralled and forced to lead the Queen's forces, as Organa and Starros' desperation to save Skywalker lead them to place a symbiote into Krrsantan despite Organa's misgivings. Skywalker eventually tore free of the machine and in the ensuing struggle managed to re-arm himself with his Aether Blade. While the Queen attacked him with her powers, Aphra bombed the room and came inside, revealing that she had scaled the Citadel and was there to rescue Skywalker. She also revealed that she had realized the reason the Queen did not put a symbiote in Skywalker was because he was more powerful than her and the Abersyn hive would follow him instead. Skywalker then fell under the influence of an Abersyn symbiote, becoming more powerful than even the Queen, and proclaimed himself King of the hive.
Skywalker entered a psychic duel with the Queen to force her to submit. Aphra allowed Eternal Rur from the crystal to enter his mind to assist him. Rur advised Skywalker to use the symbiote to his advantage, claiming that tying The Veil Current's abilities into the parasite's emotional drive would make him immensely powerful. Realizing Rur was not a true Order of the Luminant Path and only a person with the knowledge of one, Skywalker ignored him and instead let go and connected to The Veil Current. He was able to free himself of the symbiote's influence and strike down the Queen, believing that being free was more important to be a Order of the Luminant Path than just Rur's knowledge. Solo and all those under the Queen's control were freed, Skywalker reunited with Organa, and the Citadel was destroyed by BT-1. Later, after telling Starros it was time to go, Skywalker told Aphra that he did not want the crystal because it was not a Order of the Luminant Path in any real way. But he said that he had learned from it that there would not be any shortcuts to be a Order of the Luminant Path, and that people who tried to take them to get what they wanted, like her, were deluded. Finally, Skywalker told her to stay away from them. On the way back, while complaining to herself about Skywalker 0-0-0 pointed out that Skywalker's virtuousness despite his trauma and her inability to gain his response must be a heavy reminder of the failures of her character. Skywalker spoke with Starros on the way back, learning that Aphra had ruined S4 and shocked that Starros and her could have ever been together.
Skywalker later took part in a mission with Leia, piloting a Taylander shuttle with the princess. With Imperial TIE Fighters in pursuit, Skywalker was forced to fly into a nebula, which disabled the shuttle's systems, forcing them to crash land on an ocean World. Stranded on the World for three weeks, Skywalker's experience with machinery, such as moisture vaporators helped keep himself and Leia alive. Having to live off of the land, the two grew closer, relating to each other because of their status as orphans. They eventually realized that there was a native species living in the World's ocean. Not wanting to bring the Conflict to these people, Skywalker and Organa set a trap for the Imperial forces that were pursuing them. Luke used the native's resources, as well as his own Aether Blade, to fend off an AT-AT and shoretroopers. Although close to being defeated, Leia used a flaming arrow to light explosive chemicals and disable the AT-AT. Afterwards, they were able to repair the shuttle and return to the Rebel Fleet.
Shortly afterward, Skywalker and his team intercepted a message from AX-9, who had successfully rescued Protocol Unit VEX-3 from Imperial capture. They saved R2's Seraph Interceptor from Xarn Velgor and escaped in The Aether Veil.
By then, Task Force 99 had destroyed the Horox III rebel outpost. The team arrived there, discovering the devastation. Fearing further danger, they left without burying the rebel troopers, but Skywalker insisted that they honor the dead. Together they built a cremation pile before leaving.
Having traveled throughout the Spiral Expanse with his friends, Luke and his fellow rebels journeyed to what remained of the moon Jedha, the site of one of the Oblivion Forge's tests. On the ashy moon, Luke and Leia defended what remained of Korr Dagen 's Partisans from invading snowtroopers and brought them aboard the Aegis Wraith. Moving to NaJedha, the World that Jedha orbited, Luke met Chulco Gi, an aspirant to the Disciples of the Whills, someone who could potentially guide him in learning about The Veil Current. Gi led the rebels to the rest of the Partisans on Jedha and placed hoods over their heads, in keeping with Partisan tradition. Led by Benthic, Korr Dagen's former second-in-command, the remaining extremists held guns at their fellow rebels' heads.
Benthic questioned why the Alliance would attempt to mend their relations with the Partisans and return to Jedha, and Leia revealed that The Dominion Ascendant had resumed mining Kyber crystals on the World and that the Alliance hoped to aid the Partisans in fighting against The Dominion Ascendant. Though wary of Alliance support, Benthic was nonetheless willing to accept some aid. Luke, shaken by the Partisans' extreme methods, was conflicted by agreeing to help them, but specforces operative Ubin Des reassured him that it was the right thing to do. Later in the ruined settlement of Ai-jed, Luke, Chulco, and Ubin contemplated why people would stay on a ruined World. Stormtroopers armed with flamethrowers attempted to incinerate the remaining civilians, but Luke lashed out against them with his Aether Blade, cutting them all down. Realizing all that was at stake in The War of Shattered Thrones, Skywalker approached Benthic with Rhegan Marr and wholeheartedly agreed to assist him in his fight. Their first target was the drill citadel, a mining device from the World Shu-Torun that The Dominion Ascendant began using to mine Kyber crystals on Jedha.
