Benthic and Luke scaled the drill citadel and the Tognath ruthlessly killed the station's guards, much to Skywalker's chagrin. Benthic held a technician at gunpoint and forced him to lower the citadel's shields before killing him as well. This allowed Rhegan Marr, aboard the Aegis Wraith, to destroy the drill citadel after Benthic and Luke escaped. Later, a disillusioned Chulco asked Luke to accompany him to meet the Central Isopter, a death-worshipping cult that Gi claimed was wise in The Veil Current. Although Luke was hesitant to seek the guidance from someone other than a disciple of the Order of the Luminant Path, he nonetheless was willing to go along. Leia attempted to convince him that his duty to the Alliance was most important, but Luke believed that becoming a Order of the Luminant Path and avenging all those slain by Xarn Velgor was the most important duty to him. Entering a chasm alongside Gi, the duo were attacked by a sand slug, a giant, normally herbivorous animal negatively influenced by Umbral Stream of The Veil Current. Able to slay the beast, Skywalker and Gi were finally confronted by the Central Isopter, who claimed that they had come to join them "in the shadow of death."
At the edge of the World's blast crater, Chulco and the Central Isopter presented Luke with the Sanctum of Central Isopter, a shrine surrounded by Umbral Stream of The Veil Current. The cult implored Skywalker and Gi to meditate and listen to the chasm's call. They both did, but Luke refused to let Umbral Stream take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by the darkness and attacked Luke, forcing the Order of the Luminant Path trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced that they had received a distress call from Korr Tharun, who was being pursued by a TIE fighter over NaJedha. Luke borrowed a ship, was able to destroy the TIE fighter and bring an injured Chewie to the World, where he provided the Alliance with the plans for the Leviathan, a mobile fortress able to strip mine Kyber crystals from Jedha.
Han, Leia, and Benthic formed a strategy to destroy the Leviathan using the plans Korr Tharun obtained. After the meeting, Luke ruminated on the sacrifice Rogue One made to get the Oblivion Forge plans to the Alliance, ultimately leading to him making the shot that destroyed the Siege station. A newly motivated Luke accompanied Leia, Protocol Unit VEX-3, and AX-9 aboard the crawler and readied themselves for taking control of it. Skywalker made his way to the bridge of the Leviathan and held its commander, Kanchar, hostage but the Imperial's cybernetic arm was equipped with a blade able to defend against Luke's Aether Blade.
Kanchar pursued Luke throughout the ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and flamethrower. Eventually leading the officer to the crawler's hangar, Skywalker activated an ion torpedo, which shut down Kanchar's cybernetics and allowed Luke to knock him out. Having taken total control of the Leviathan with the help of the double-crossing Queen Trios, the rebels brought it to Jedha's chasm, destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what remained of Jedha and made allies in the form of the Partisans and Queen Trios, Luke and his friends left Jedha.
After a mission with a plan made by Solo went awry, Skywalker, who had helped fight in his Seraph Interceptor, escaped to Baraan-Fa with the rest of the Alliance. Organa then put him in charge of a mission to the World Crait to survey its facilities as a base. Skywalker, flying there in a GR-75 medium transport, believed the mission was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he directed those on the mission to land. Once there, they met up with Trusk Berinato, who led them all to the mines of the World on V-4X-D ski speeders. After receiving new orders from Organa, Luke stated that he found the mission particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they arrived, Solo encouraged him to enjoy the mission since they were not in danger. He told everyone to follow him.
Skywalker and his team, which included Antilles, began using the farming equipment to make trenches in Crait's surface, though he said he would rather do anything else. Green worms then appeared from the ground and Antilles and Solo tried to shoot them, but Skywalker realized they would leave you alone if you left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Skywalker reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Skywalker took Antilles with him on the ski speeders and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Skywalker rushed to tell the others. He reached Organa and told her he resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Skywalker realized Berinato forged environmental reports, Solo warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.
Skywalker got in a ski speeder and attempted to shoot at the incoming troopers, but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his Aether Blade, a determined Skywalker lighting his too. The two dueled, Skywalker turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you pushed them. The worms he saw earlier then tackled Kreel and Skywalker cut the Pale Enforcer's Aether Blade in half. Skywalker made his way back to the Falcon and revealed that SCAR squadron lived, but that they needed to get out of the storm or they would die. Later, in orbit on the Falcon, Organa asked how Skywalker was and he said that he was the same, living a small life and trying to make a difference. She told him not to change.
Skywalker joined Organa, Solo, Ackbar and others to meet with Grand Admiral Dors Urtya of the Mon Cala Mercantile Fleet at an island preserve on Mon Cala with the hope of getting the fleet to join the Uprising. Skywalker was surprised The Dominion Ascendant cared about nature preservation, but Organa clarified that they wanted to exploit it. After Han revealed he used to smuggle from there, which upset Skywalker, Ackbar and Organa began to negotiate as an Imperial LAAT/le patrol gunship approached. Skywalker immediately assumed they had been betrayed, but Solo thought otherwise and advised Skywalker not to use his Aether Blade while he spoke with them. It fired on Urtya's ship, the explosion knocking Skywalker down. When Solo's plan resulted in stormtroopers pursuing them, Skywalker went with the others through the forest to a boat where Korr Tharun was waiting. On the boat, Organa continued to try an convince Urtya, but he said that he did not want to sacrifice Mon Cala, but Skywalker shot back, insisting he could not talk to the Princess of Eryth Vale about sacrifice. They eventually made it back to the Falcon and then to the Mako-Ta Space Docks that the Uprising was using as a production center, where Skywalker rejected going drinking with Solo.
However, Skywalker did go with Solo to the bar, but insisted on only getting milk, to Solo's dismay. There, someone put their hand on Skywalker's shoulder so he pushed them away with The Veil Current, but it turned out to be Wedge Antilles and the two had a cheerful reunion, Skywalker insisted that he had not forgot about Antilles and declared that Red Squadron was forever. Solo then tried to get Skywalker a drink to celebrate, but Skywalker just ordered the biggest milk they had. Antilles introduced Skywalker to Hobbie and Zev Senesca, who he explained they had met during Alliance training exercises, which he clarified were to ensure enough people could operate cruisers. Senesca said he was honored to meet Skywalker, but Skywalker just told him not to use honorifics and told him he would have also taken the shot. Solo then lead a toast to Red Squadron, Skywalker reciting all its members that had died. Solo was surprised he remembered all the names, but Skywalker expressed that he wished to know the names of all the Rebels that had died. Organa then arrived and told them to finish up because they had a mission.
During the mission, Skywalker accompanied Organa and Solo to the Dex Acquisitions Depot on Meor Ain to retrieve a Clawdite. With Protocol Unit VEX-3 unable to slice the door to the room where he was, Skywalker used his Aether Blade to cut around and remove the locking mechanism before Korr Tharun kicked the door. After retrieving the shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be Bail Organa, the group left.
After debriefing the Clawdite, whose name was Tunga Arpagion, that he was to impersonate the Moff of the Calamari sector Tan Hubi while they kidnapped him, Skywalker insisting the ordeal would make Arpagion more famous. At the Moncaladrome on Mon Cala, Skywalker impersonated a Pale Enforcer, Organa ordering him to wait until they drugged the Moff and then bring him back to their hideout. Solo and Korr Tharun went into a refresher and anticipated kidnapping Hubi while Skywalker stood outside as a Pale Enforcer and told everyone around that the room was for the Moff's private use, though one Imperial officer did manage to Force themself through. After successfully kidnapping Hubi, Skywalker returned to Organa and warned her to be quick so that others would not notice the switch. After Arpagion copied Hubi's appearance, Skywalker told him "May The Veil Current be with you" before departing with the others and a tied up Hubi in a Mon Cal ship to rescue Mon Cal King Lee-Char from Strokill Prime.
Once there, Skywalker commented that it made sense they would keep Lee-Char on a water world, though admitted the place looked bleak. After avoiding the World's security using Hubi's biosignature, they took the ship to the World and underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature. Organa realized that the creature was attracted to their ship because of the light. Solo was skeptical, but Skywalker told him not to question her and informed them he was going to carry out a plan with Korr Tharun's help and to be ready. Donning a full body diving suit while Korr Tharun set up an underwater sled, Skywalker shot out far away from the ship after Korr Tharun gave Skywalker a hug and he thanked the Wookiee. Away from the ship in the expanse of the ocean, Skywalker ignited his Aether Blade, leading the creature away with the light. Korr Tharun then used a water sled and a breath mask to quickly go after Skywalker and bring him back to the ship as he turned off his Aether Blade.
With the creature distracted, they all safely made it to the underwater base, where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then discovered that Lee-Char was hooked up to a machine that was the only thing keeping him alive, and Solo said Skywalker jinxed them. Skywalker attempted to calm an angry Solo, and Lee-Char suggested that they could just carry his words to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Skywalker to suggest they search for a recording Construct Servitor there.
After bringing back a camera Construct Servitor, Skywalker watched as the King recorded his message, before noticing stormtroopers and screaming out to everyone that they had been noticed. The stormtroopers shot into the room, killing Lee-Char, but the group escaped with the recording back to their ship, Skywalker defending them with a Ion Pistol and directing them to get onboard and get to The Aether Veil quickly. He then pointed out that the Falcon with its touchy Aether Engine would not have been able to pull that off, but Solo warned him to stop disparaging the ship.
They met back up with Protocol Unit VEX-3 in a deserted cove on Mon Cala, Skywalker revealing to the Construct Servitor that the king could not be moved but having hope that his message was worth it because people were counting on them. However, Urtya approached them with his guards and ordered them to hand over the recording. As he forcefully took it from Organa, Skywalker screamed that Lee-Char would be ashamed, though Solo told him to top talking and that they needed to get out of there. However, Urtya decided to show the footage too all of the fleet captains. Back on the Falcon, Skywalker heard on an earpiece that the people on the Mon Calamari ships were rebelling because of footage of Lee-Char. Because of this, however, the Imperial fleet arrived.
Later, on the Falcon, Skywalker revealed that they were getting a message from the regent on the World. The regent apologized for acting without warning and revealed he had called the Rebel fleet. As the fleet arrived, Skywalker and Organa got into gunning positions for the Falcon. Skywalker tried to ask her if she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing TIE bombers approach, Solo told Skywalker he was going to give him an angle to take them out, and Skywalker successfully did. Antilies then commed the Falcon and asked if they were having trouble, but Skywalker just expressed his happiness that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of their situation. Skywalker kept shooting to help protect jetisonned escape pods, and the Falcon escaped to The Aether Veil after a Mon Calamari ship full of coaxium crashed into the Abyss Cruiser and exploded it. Later, Skywalker accompanied Organa, Solo, and Korr Tharun to see Arpagion off on the Cosmatanic Steppes.
Later, Skywalker participated in a celebration of the commencement of the Rebel fleet at the Rebel Base at Mako-Ta, using his Seraph Interceptor to create a display trail of blue light alongside other X-Wings creating trails of other colors. After landing back in the hangar bay, Skywalker expressed his enthusiasm that amount Rebels present could definitively defeat The Dominion Ascendant, though had to assure General Dodonna that he was not being overconfident and was just excited. However, due to a betrayal by Queen Trios of Shu-Torun, the commencement failed and the Imperial Navy arrived, which Skywalker saw from a window and suspected was led by Vader. He prepared to get in his Seraph Interceptor and fight, assuring R2 that everything would be fine, but the bay doors were sabotaged by Trios and kept them trapped there.
He then watched with shock as one of the Alliance's cruisers was destroyed and others started to be, but still assured Zev that they had survived the Oblivion Forge and would not die there. Skywalker and Antilles then spotted the Falcon taking on the fighters, both confused as to what Solo was doing there. Unbeknownst to them Solo was trying to get to them to let them know that the bay doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.
Skywalker was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch the ships get destroyed, but Solo sent Protocol Unit VEX-3 in an escape pod to the hangar bay, who informed him that they could leave by flying at the doors. Skywalker rallied all of the fighters to get ready to launch and readied R2. Dodonna commed Skywalker, who informed him of the situation, but Dodonna warned him that they needed to test the doors first, which Skywalker ignored.
As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Skywalker realized that his situation was making him feel how Lira Cor must have felt when she took her rogue mission to steal the Oblivion Forge plans. Though R2 warned that the doors might not open, he continued, trusting in The Veil Current, but saying he would blame his potential death on Protocol Unit VEX-3. The doors opened and the rest of the fighters went to meet Skywalker, him telling Wedge that he was glad for their company. Wedge advised against sentiment and asked Skywalker what their call sign should be. He told Antilles that sentiment was timely because they were acting like Lira Cor, and named the squadron Rogue Squadron after Erso's crew called Rogue One.

## Rebel Commander

Entering the Siege, Skywalker called out to Rogue Eight to warn them that someone was on their tail, but their Seraph Interceptor was destroyed. Solo advised the squadron to stay close to the destroyers, and as they did, Skywalker noticed the decrepit state of the Falcon and asked what happened to it, Solo merely replying that Xarn Velgor got to it and warning Skywalker that he was still there somewhere. After Solo's plan paid off, Skywalker made a joke to him by calling him an Admiral, which Solo told him never to do again. From the base, Organa hoped out loud that Skywalker would spread the word after learning that he was out there.
Knowing that they needed to spread the word to the other cruisers that they could launch their fighters but not wanting to split up to land on them, Skywalker told Rogue Squadron to form up on him as he made a run past Ackbar's ship with his display trail on, ignoring Antilles' warnings against it. As Ackbar watched, his attention grabbed by the display trail, Skywalker flew toward the bay doors on one of the cruisers, which opened. Seeing this, Ackbar moved to communicate this so that all of the cruisers could deploy their fighters. Solo then announced to Skywalker that the Falcon was too damaged and so he was flying an Seraph Interceptor.
Skywalker pursued a rogue TIE fighter that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to him was piloted by Organa, who decided to take it while fleeing Vader and his Abyss Cruiser the Dominion Reaper. Sensing something was off and noticing the pilot's skill, Skywalker had R2 try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ shot down another TIE, Skywalker realized the pilot was on their side. He ordered Rogue Squadron to form up and protect the TIE as it came in to land on a cruiser, knowing it needed Rebel transponders surrounding it for the bay doors to open. This was successful, but Skywalker's fighter was shot as he did this and crashed into the bay. Solo and Organa rushed to make sure he was all right, leaving Skywalker shocked Organa had been piloting the TIE, but all of them were happy things worked out well for them.
Due to the counter-codes Organa had retrieved from the Dominion Reaper, the cruiser escaped to The Aether Veil, though Skywalker wanted to go back and try and rescue more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Dodonna criticized him for this and told him the decision was not his, merely telling Skywalker and Organa to prepare for Conflict. After it was decided to attempt a rescue, Solo returned to Mako-Ta with Rogue Squadron, Skywalker telling them he would fly with them again soon, causing Organa to make a comment implying he was neurotic. As Organa told Selene Myrr to get the ambassadors to board their ship, he spotted a TIE fighter coming to them, but it was shot down by Solo. Soon after, Skywalker tried to inform Solo that they were almost ready to leave, but could not get ahold of him.
Organa and Skywalker told Mothma to leave in the main transport with everyone while they tried to save Solo, which they did by attaching cables to his Seraph Interceptor and then jumping to The Aether Veil. Skywalker brought them to a moon called Corosi 7 where Mothma sent them a transmission informing them that in the midst of the Alliance's dispersal, Skywalker was promoted to a Commander and Organa to a General, instructing them to find more ways to hurt The Dominion Ascendant. Receiving no information on the location of Korr Tharun or the Falcon, Skywalker wondered how they would find them. Solo decided to take them to Nar Shaddaa, where they met up with Sana Starros, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship the Volt Cobra.
Following the attack on the Mako-Ta Space Docks, Skywalker was promoted to the rank of commander. After being promoted, Skywalker was sent to the desert world of Er'Kit, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of mercenaries known as the Malachor Company. Upon arriving on the desert world, Skywalker sensed through The Veil Current that there was something more at play beyond the actions of a few mercenaries. He requested the assistance of an agent who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Imperial operatives in the sector that Imperial supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Skywalker and the agent arrested the mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Malachor Company, who had holed up in an empty village.
Among the mercenaries' possessions, Skywalker and the agent discovered a document proving that Malachor Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Skywalker and the agent to an Imperial base on the World. They subsequently attacked several Imperial bases until they found intelligence pertaining to The Dominion Ascendant's operation on the World. Once they located the base from which the Imperials deployed most of their forces on the World, Skywalker assaulted it with the assistance of Princess Organa. The Rebels razed the base, dealing an important blow to The Dominion Ascendant's operations on Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what The Dominion Ascendant's true purpose was on the World. Skywalker gradually discovered Imperial logs detailing how Malachor Company had been hired by the Imperial Security Bureau and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for Project Hidden Eye. This ISB operation was intended to create cybernetic sleeper agents who could be deployed throughout the Spiral Expanse and spy on galactic citizens on behalf of the ISB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its first wave of spies.
To prevent the operation from being carried out, Skywalker and the agent led their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its staging area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Imperial project for good. Skywalker thanked the agent for their service and then remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping The Ash Concord execute additional missions on the desert world.
Eventually, Organa presented an idea to Skywalker, Solo, and Korr Tharun to take down Shu-Torun and all of its facilities. Skywalker was on board immediately and thought it was time considering how much Queen Trios was responsible for. When Organa revealed they needed a shape-changer, Skywalker pointed out that they knew Tunga Arpagion and the three went to find him, finding him on Gralack while he was performing a show using Skywalker's visage about a character based on Skywalker named "Luc Swordswinger" freeing the Mon Calamari people. When stormtroopers tried to shut down the show for being treasonous material, Skywalker confronted them, but thinking he was just Arpagion in Skywalker's form, ignored him. Skywalker disarmed all of the Stormtroopers by destroying their blasters while yelling with rage as the others extracted Arpagion. Organa later revealed that they also needed people who were used to fighting on a hell world, which Skywalker realized meant they needed to go back to Jedha. While Organa recruited the remaining Partisans there, Skywalker went to the Sanctum of the Central Isopter with Solo in the hopes of receiving advice from them about the dangers of The Veil Current and controlling his anger but was surprised to find it empty, especially knowing that the cult hunted disasters like Jedha was experiencing and wanted to be around them.
Back on the Home One, Skywalker was not happy Starros was busy and could not help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they arrived on Shu-Torun, Skywalker had to assuage their slicer Meorti, who had become scared by Solo calling the mission suicidal, by insisting that they had a good plan and Solo liked to be dramatic. On the Falcon, Organa explained to Skywalker and the others her plan to destroy a massive tower called the Spike, thus systematically causing the destruction of the rest of the World's infrastructure. Skywalker then revealed that they were planning to get access to the master security controls and lock down everything on the World like they did to them at Mako-Ta. While Organa worked to get an eye scan of Trios to achieve this, Skywalker, Korr Tharun, and the Partisans waited on the Falcon.
Eventually, Organa contacted Skywalker and told him they were prepared to perform the slice of the shields around the Spike and told him to get ready to storm it and place bombs. He launched the Falcon and told Benthic and the Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Solo told Skywalker to take care of the ship, but Skywalker pointed out that the only danger it would be in would be if Solo and the others did not get the shields down in time. Once he got the word from Organa that he was able, Skywalker took the Falcon through the shields and he and the others stormed the base, noticing how weird it was that they had control and the element of surprise. Skywalker disarmed all the guards, but the Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Skywalker warning them not to push their luck. Skywalker revealed that they had control of all the doors now and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up the cores, but told Korr Tharun to get back to the ship to stay in contact with Organa.
They eventually took down three of the cores, and Skywalker took down the guards at the fourth one with his Ion Pistol and Aether Blade while the Partisans gave him cover, before instructing R2 to set it to explode. Skywalker told them they had one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go back to the ship and make contact. After clearing the room of guards at the last core, Benthic tried to get R2 to ignore Skywalker's intructions to set a charge and instead rig the entire Spike to explode and destroy the World. He told R2 to stop and told the Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen. Skywalker then feigned surrender as R2 emitted a bunch of gas into the gas, allowing Skywalker and the Construct Servitor to run off out of sight. Fearing the Partisans had gone crazy, Skywalker was determined to find a way to stop them.
Skywalker ran through the Spike, using his Aether Blade to deactivate a blast door's controls and close it. As the Partisans started to open it, Skywalker told R2 that they could not do anything without the Construct Servitor, instructing him to hide while he led the Partisans away. He did so successfully, but was cornered by the Partisans, who then contacted R2 and told him that if he did not come to them they would kill Skywalker. R2 did what they said despite Skywalker insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.
As R2 set the Spike to destroy the World, Skywalker told the Construct Servitor that he did not understand him because saving his life was not worth killing millions. Skywalker secretly hoped stormtroopers would show up and take down the Partisans. He unsuccessfully tried to talk Benthic into stopping, but Organa and Solo arrived and the former successfully talked him down. As Skywalker and the others escaped in the Falcon, refusing to give in to Imperial forces that had arrived, Protocol Unit VEX-3 revealed that R2 kept telling Skywalker to "trust in The Veil Current" when he was beeping. After making it to The Aether Veil, Skywalker asked what they would do about the Partisans, who he called traitorous, them all eventually deciding to drop them off on Salobea. Back at the Home One, Skywalker asked Meorti if they had gotten an appetite for the field, but she said she had not and sped off. Organa then listed off a bunch of possible locations for a new rebel base provided to her by General Dodonna, one of which was Kryne Abyss, Skywalker declaring that one of the places would be their new home.
Following their mission to Shu-Toran, Skywalker, along with Solo and Organa, returned to the Rebel fleet where General Carlist Rieekan, noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Skywalker a mission to the Inner Rim World Sergia to lure all Imperial forces and probe droids that had recently been released en mass from a refueling station The Ash Concord had established on the World. When Skywalker arrived, he found a massive Abyss Cruiser in orbit. After failing to lure it away, Skywalker landed on the World and tried to look for the Rebels on foot, though was upset that he could not influence the Abyss Cruiser's pilot with The Veil Current to do what he wanted like he believed Kenobi could have. He lamented not having a teacher and once again being on a desert World despite spending his whole life trying to leave one.
After destroying a probe Construct Servitor that was caught in a stampede of plain hundas with his his Aether Blade, Skywalker found that the rebels were operating out in the open, and sent AX-9 to deliver a message to the rebels to lie low until he could help them to escape. He spent hours attempting to find pods released by the Abyss Cruiser, eventually trying and failing to use The Veil Current to find them, before finding a town and deciding to check it out. There, in a bar, Skywalker saved thief Warba Calip from an individual that was threatening her because he believed she was cheating at sabacc by beating them with a broom. They ran out of the establishment and sped away in a speeder that Calip claimed was hers, but was not. Skywalker was annoyed by the experience and asked her to drop him off as soon as possible, but she was elated and asked why he was not, before suggesting that the reason she had won so much at the bar was because she knew The Veil Current, saying she recognized Skywalker as someone who sought it.
Eventually the speeder Skywalker and Calip were in was deactivated from afar by the owner and the two introduced themselves to each other. When he questioned his decision to rescue her, she claimed she had cheated earlier using a Order of the Luminant Path mind trick, though she was not a Order of the Luminant Path. She also saw his Aether Blade and told him to tell her all about it after they moved the speeder and then hid. Once they did, and saw the owner coming for it, they hid out in a forest of dead trees, though Calip no longer was interested in hearing about his Aether Blade or helping him with The Veil Current because she said using The Veil Current got people killed. He insisted he just needed a little help, before noticing that Calip had stolen his Aether Blade and pulling it back to him to activate it. After contacting someone named Tinga about the Aether Blade, Calip tried to suggest that Skywalker run away and settle down, but he insisted that he needed to learn about The Veil Current to fight The Dominion Ascendant and tried to convince her to help him with his mission to destroy the shuttles that had arrived there, explaining that he was unable to locate them with The Veil Current. She claimed that she could teach him to reach out with his mind and find them, but that it would take too long and they should just rob a bank instead, confusing Skywalker.
Skywalker was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal the money from the Imperial payroll garrison so they could buy the information about the shuttles from the secretary of the local sub-governor. Skywalker and Calip analyzed the defenses before she taught him the Guardian's Mantra, claiming he would become more powerful if he had the belief of the the priests who said that mantra. While saying the mantra, Skywalker took down Imperial droids with his Aether Blade and barged into the garrison, though he lost his Aether Blade in a flurry of blasterfire shot by turrets on the building, though Calip collected it. After an explosion rocked the building, Skywalker exited the building with a bag full of credits. He hugged Calip, saying that The Veil Current was with him and she was right that he would be able to do it despite his doubts. She said she was always right, which Skywalker said he would remember.
After escaping in an Imperial speeder, Skywalker expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take the money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Skywalker's insistences that he needed to find the patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Skywalker started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his training and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon stormtroopers threatening a person named Ranja. Skywalker wanted to take them down with his Aether Blade, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Skywalker once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Jedha near the Sanctum of the Kyber and had seen the Guardians of the Whills perform miracles, but had also seen The Dominion Ascendant's atrocities afterward. When he asked about The Veil Current, she instructed Skywalker to close his eyes and try to reach out and feel for it after explaining what it was. However, she was really using the opportunity to steal his Aether Blade and run off, which Skywalker quickly realized, screaming out for her.
As he ran after Calip, Protocol Unit VEX-3 reached out to Skywalker via comm and informed him that the World he and Korr Tharun were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Vader had arrived. Skywalker got no response from the Construct Servitor after and ran after Calip to get his Aether Blade, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in the speeder. The Rebels on Sergia then contacted Skywalker via AX-9 and informed them that an Imperial transport had found them. Skywalker told them to stay under cover before stealing a stormtroopers Wraith Glider and leading the transport away, instructing the Rebels to evacuate while he did so. He then crashed the Wraith Glider into the transport and brought it down, stormtroopers chasing him through the desert until he ran into Calip in her speeder, who reluctantly gave Skywalker his Aether Blade. While deflecting the stormtroopers blasterfire with his Aether Blade, Skywalker got in the speeder with Calip and they left, Skywalker joking that he used a Order of the Luminant Path mind trick on her and pointing out that in seemed like she was like his friend Solo and helping for more than just the money.
Meanwhile, Xarn Velgor had discovered that Korr Tharun was on the World K43 in the Aegis Wraith. He had realized it was a rebel trap for him and Imperial forces, but decided to use Korr Tharun to trap Skywalker. Skywalker commed Solo, telling the smuggler he had just been talking about him and was fleeing stormtroopers mounted on desert manglers, but Solo one-upped him and said he was trying to take down a Abyss Cruiser with a transport shuttle. After keeping the troopers away by swinging his Aether Blade at them, Skywalker instructed Calip to have faith and bring the speeder to some rocks, where Skywalker used a grappling hook to bring down the rocks on top of the troopers. Skywalker then made it back to R2 and his Seraph Interceptor, which Rebels had prepared and confirmed it was the right time for him to leave and avoid the Void Leviathan. Skywalker instructed them to take Calip and drop her on the next safe World. When Skywalker revealed he was going to K43, Calip revealed that she did not actually know much about The Veil Current and had not really taught Skywalker anything, warning he would die if he went to face Vader. Skywalker said he still needed to go, and boarded his Seraph Interceptor and took off while telling Calip that she was more than she seemed, and that he hoped they all were.
While landing his Seraph Interceptor on K43, the starfighter was hit by an electromagnetic pulse generated by the World's natives, the Kakrans, in an attempt to combat Vader. As a result, the Seraph Interceptor crashed and R2 was deactivated, leaving Skywalker worried and rattled as he noticed Vader approaching him with his red Aether Blade ignited. However, Vader was attacked with a rock by Korr Tharun before the Vornic Archon could reach Skywalker. After Korr Tharun knocked Vader down into a chasm, Skywalker ran to his friend. Finding his Aether Blade unable to activate, he decided to use a rock to bring an avalanche down on top of Vader while saying the Guardian's mantra, but ultimately caught caught in the rock slide and had to be saved from the Shadow Sovereign's clutches by Korr Tharun. Skywalker tried to get Korr Tharun to abandon him and save himself as Korr Tharun carried him and ran away from Vader and blasterfire shot by stormtroopers at the top of the chasm.
As they ran, Solo crashed his shuttle down in front of them, Skywalker saying they needed to get Vader before Solo could even ask how he was doing. Organa then emerged from the ship shooting stormtroopers with another man, who Solo revealed was named Dar Champion when Skywalker asked. After learning that that Vader had been weakened by the electromagnetic pulse, Skywalker insisted they would never get a better chance to take him down so they all rushed at him while shooting at him until he threw them all down and back with The Veil Current. After Vader ran off, Skywalker and the others started board the Falcon to evacuate the unstable World, but remembering R2 was still on his Seraph Interceptor, Skywalker ran to rescue the Construct Servitor, telling the others he would meet them in the air. Protocol Unit VEX-3 and the Kakrans then agreed that Skywalker was very thoughtful.
Skywalker eventually reunited with AX-9 at his Seraph Interceptor, but Vader was there too, with his Aether Blade ignited. Skywalker ignited his Aether Blade too, but before they could duel, the ground beneath them collapsed, forcing Skywalker to jump in the Seraph Interceptor as the World was destroyed. As Skywalker flew his starfighter in the aftermath, he discovered that Vader had survived the event and watched with relief as the Abyss Cruiser the man was in jumped to Chrono Drift before Vader could hurt him. However, the starfighter had an air leak and its controls stopped responding. Skywalker was saved by the rebels from Sergia, including Calip who had refused to let the others go somewhere safe and insisted they rescue Skywalker. Skywalker told her that he had known she would come through. Later, on the Rebel fleet, which was now over the World Kryne Abyss, Skywalker watched as Champion spoke to Rieekan and commented he was really good. Organa claimed the reason for this was that he had plans and did not just show up and hope for the best like Solo, but Skywalker decided that was all you needed when you had friends as he watched with a smile as Calip pickpocketed someone.
Skywalker lead Rogue Squadron, including Antilles, Lyle Kullan, Isla Mantra, and Ibti Myrak to accompany Blade Squadron to the Taris system to take out an Imperial shipyard. After having everyone report in, Skywalker attempted to dissuade Myrak of their excitement to hurt The Dominion Ascendant, insisting they have a level head, but Myrak continued being cocky, which angered Kullan. Skywalker reminded Kullan that he used to be the same way. Just then, R2 revealed that multiple TIE fighters were incoming, and Myrak leapt into action, but Skywalker told her to stay in formation because she was leaving the left side of Blade Squadron open. Myrak and Kullan then squabbled over taking out the TIEs over comm and Skywalker ordered them to stop and focus. Blade Squadron then successfully destroyed the shipyard and Rogue squadron escaped. Afterward, Skywalker warned Myrak that she was out of line. When she kept being cocky, Skywalker assured Antilles that he would watcher her and she would be grounded permanently if she made another mistake.
Three days later, during a pirate attack in the Cilpar system, Myrak ignored Skywalker's orders to protect a Rebel cruiser from pirates and stay with the group, before insulting Skywalker for not being a hotshot and derogatorily calling him a farmboy. After Skywalker was forced to save Myrak from the pilots, Skywalker told her that she was being kicked out of the Squadron for disobeying orders. On the Home One, Skywalker and Antilies then received a message from Kullan saying The Dominion Ascendant was attacking Lonisa City on the World Valo, before it cut out. Skywalker ordered Myrak to stay behind and catalogue message logs while Skywalker went with the rest of the Squadron. Once in the Valo system, they found no sign of The Dominion Ascendant until Skywalker got an intense feeling in The Veil Current and a Abyss Cruiser appeared.
While Skywalker and Rogue Squadron fought The Dominion Ascendant and tried to figure out where Kulan was, Myrak ignored Skywalker's orders and took her Seraph Interceptor to go after him. Ackbar then tried to reach out to Skywalker and let him know, but found he could not get through to Rogue Squadron. Protocol Unit VEX-3 then warned Ackbar that he suspected Skywalker's hands were full. Meanwhile, Skywalker tried and failed to get through to the fleet, and he and Mantra tried to assure Antilles they had his back. However, Skywalker was then caught in a tractor beam. Mantra went to save him, but Skywalker warned her to follow advice someone smarter than he looks, referencing Solo, had told him which was "don't get cocky", to which she called Solo a kill-joy. She assured Skywalker he would be able to tell Solo she said that, but before she could take out the tractor beam, she was fired at by a fellow Seraph Interceptor, who turned out to be Kullan.
Kullan revealed that he had been working for The Dominion Ascendant and would have had Skywalker captured at Taris if it was not for Myrak. However, Myrak then arrived and announced herself, before shooting at the tractor beams and freeing Skywalker. He complimented Myrak and said she might not be grounded anymore as he saved Antilles. Skywalker then had the entire Squadron form up on Kullan as the Rebel fleet arrived. The Abyss Cruiser left into The Aether Veil and abandoned Kullan, who was then captured by the Rebels. Akbar and Protocol Unit VEX-3 then revealed to Skywalker that Myrak had uncovered Kullan's spy messages and plan to trap him on Valo. Skywalker assured Protocol Unit VEX-3 that there never had been any real danger, because Rogue Squadron was on the case and was the best team in the Spiral Expanse.
In 3 ABY, Skywalker and the Rogue Squadron were transferred to the Echo Base, a rebel base on the iced World Kryne Abyss, where he participated in regular patrols of the frozen perimeter under the callsign Echo Three. Upon Skywalker's arrival at Echo Base, Rebel leadership expected him to use The Veil Current to help excavate the tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do that. His belongings, including his Aether Blade, were placed in a decontamination unit in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant Reyé Hollis from Rebel Alliance Special Forces was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Skywalker declared that he was there to help.
Skywalker hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-Order of the Luminant Path sentiments gave Skywalker the initial impression that the other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Velora Prime name, but he could sense that Velora Prime was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about the Order of the Luminant Path and Skywalker defended them. He also promised not to let the Velora Prime man down, just before a giant fork-nosed ice worm broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.
Unused to total darkness, Skywalker experienced claustrophobia. He could hear but not locate Hollis until he tried to use The Veil Current to stretch out his senses. He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Dunreach Expanse, but that did not work and he still felt suffocated by the darkness. He successfully detected that Hollis was nearby using The Veil Current and told the other man to stay still. When Skywalker approached, they bickered briefly over what was responsible for the cave-in, Hollis urging him not to use The Veil Current. Hollis, recognizing that the Order of the Luminant Path was panicking, grabbed Skywalker's hand and pressed it to a gap in the wall that had air flowing through it. Skywalker clutched Hollis' hand and asked for his trust. They sat down and kept holding each other's hands as Skywalker thought about the light and warmth of his home on Dunreach Expanse. Skywalker began to reflect on his fear of what the darkness would do to him psychologically, referring both to the literal situation they were in and the general darkness in the Spiral Expanse he faced. As their hands warmed, Skywalker could sense where they could dig through the ice to free themselves in The Veil Current. They teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Skywalker recognizing that The Veil Current had never steered him the wrong direction and suspecting it never would.
While the base was still being built, two rebel soldiers, Kes Dameron and his wife, Shara Bey found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by Vanguard Striker cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son, Eryk Dane to explain why they joined the Uprising. Skywalker, however, rescued them, helped by Korr Tharun by opening a hole in the ice using his Aether Blade, him, Organa, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Dameron and Bey were eventually treated by a 2-1B surgical Construct Servitor.
At some point, Skywalker walked outside of Echo Base to speak with Captain Magna Tolvan, Vulaada Klam, and Korin Aphra, the latter of which had been very excited by the idea of The Veil Current-sensitive pilot and the former recognizing him as the man who destroyed the Oblivion Forge, which Skywalker confirmed. After confirming that they were the friends of Doctor Chelli Aphra, Skywalker told them that a Uprising spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on the Dominion Reaper that someone had sabotaged The Dominion Ascendant's computers and made it hard for them to search for the Alliance. He explained that The Dominion Ascendant had put out a warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Doctor Aphra and her Construct Servitor companions. Skywalker revealed that sabotage had set The Dominion Ascendant back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Aphra before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Aphra had also saved the Emperor's life.
Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Echo Base on the tauntaun Riba, after Solo had departed back to base, Skywalker was attacked by a wampa, only to regain consciousness in its cave. He severed its arm with his Aether Blade and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Kenobi, who told him to seek the teachings of Eld Sevron in the Myrr Fen system.
Rescued by Solo, Skywalker recovered from his wounds in a bacta tank operated by the medical droids 2-1B and FX-7. However, Vader had located Echo Base with one of his probe droids and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened Alliance base, thus prompting the Alliance to evacuate the base at once. After bidding farewell to his friends, for the impending Siege, Skywalker was to lead Rogue Squadron in an airspeeder. Before he departed to the battlefront, he was spotted by rebel journalist Corwi Selgrothe, who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transports by rebel trooper L'cayo Llem. During the Siege, Skywalker led his speeders against the walkers, ordering Rogue Squadron to pull the AT-ATs down with tow tables after realizing their armor could resist their blasters. Though he was shot down, and his gunner Dak Ralter was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder and single-handedly destroyed walker Blizzard 4 by using a harpoon gun and then used his Aether Blade to cut a hole and throw a Aether Bomb. As Imperial forces breached the Rebels' defenses, the last Rebel transport departed, and Skywalker fled the World aboard his Seraph Interceptor with AX-9.

## Traveling to Myrr Fen

Skywalker then traveled to Myrr Fen, which turned out to be a remote swamp World, where he encountered Eld Sevron, the former Grand Master of the fallen Order of the Luminant Path Order. In their initial meeting, Skywalker was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Eld Sevron, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Skywalker's patience, telling him a story about three brothers, and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf stew, which he did not find very appetizing.
However, Skywalker gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Eld Sevron agreed to train Skywalker only at the insistence of Kenobi's spirit. Skywalker's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.
Skywalker's training on Myrr Fen lasted several weeks, with Kenobi needing to appear in Eld Sevron's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up Luke's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Skywalker and Eld Sevron argued, Kenobi also regularly appeared to stop their arguments. One day during his training with Eld Sevron, the Order of the Luminant Path Master had Skywalker climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Skywalker lost his balance and fell from the tree. Eld Sevron decided to have Skywalker carry him as he redid the task.
When Eld Sevron asked him to enter a cave that was strong in Umbral Stream in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision of himself wearing the armor of Xarn Velgor, meant to symbolize his inner darkness. Master and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging elephoth, which Skywalker managed to calm by leading her to her child.
Shortly after, he had a vision of Rhegan Marr and Lyra Calen suffering at Cloud City on Aether Spire. Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Skywalker decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Eld Sevron and Kenobi's insistence that he was not ready to face Umbral Stream on his own yet, but Skywalker was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of The Dominion Ascendant.
Seeing that Skywalker's mind was made up, Kenobi let him know that if he ended up facing Vader, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Eld Sevron advised him to mind what he would learned. Skywalker promised to do so, and vowed to return to Myrr Fen one day to finish his training.

## Vader's revelation

Upon his arrival at Cloud City's East Platform, Skywalker felt Vader's presence, and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Fett, who had come to claim a bounty put on Solo by Vorath the Gluttonous, and tried to pursue a group of stormtroopers and Imperial officers who held Organa, Korr Tharun, and Protocol Unit VEX-3 captive. The princess attempted to warn Skywalker that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to the carbon-freezing chamber where Xarn Velgor awaited him, with the intention to freeze him in carbonite to present him to the Emperor. Skywalker engaged the Shadow Sovereign in a fierce Aether Blade duel.
During the duel, Vader praised Skywalker's training in The Veil Current thus far and attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it was the only way to defeat him. Vader managed to knock Skywalker into the carbon-freezing chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Solo, but Skywalker leapt out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left Vader impressed.
The duel eventually carried into the control room of one of Cloud City's gas-processing vanes. Vader began using The Veil Current to throw various objects in the chamber at the young Order of the Luminant Path hopeful. One such projectile, a large piece of metal piping, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Skywalker out onto the nearby sensor relay catwalk overlooking the reactor shaft. Skywalker managed to grab on to the catwalk's railing and pull himself up.
Vader re-entered the fray and forced Skywalker back onto the catwalk. Sensing that Skywalker was weakened, the Vornic Archon demanded that Skywalker surrender, lest he be destroyed as Arion Velth had been. Skywalker refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Vader, to which Vader responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Skywalker to the very edge of the catwalk and slicing off his right hand. Both Skywalker's hand and his Aether Blade fell into the depths of the reactor shaft.
Seeing that Skywalker was vulnerable in his weakened state, Vader offered to show Skywalker the power of Umbral Stream and complete his training, but Skywalker adamantly refused to ever join Vader, the man who had killed his father. In that moment, Vader revealed the truth: that he was his father.
Horrified by Vader's shocking revelation, Skywalker initially denounced the Vornic Archon's claim as untrue and impossible, but Vader insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Vader further claimed that Skywalker was powerful enough to destroy the Emperor, and when he did, they could rule the Spiral Expanse together as father and son. Unwilling to join Vader but too weak to fight back, Skywalker took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the reactor shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the outside of the floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a weather vane to avoid falling to his death, he knew he was unable to stay there forever, especially in his weakened state. Calling out to Kenobi at first but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Lyra Calen through The Veil Current, who was able to hear the call.
Soon, the Aegis Wraith, with Darik Solen, Organa, Korr Tharun, and the droids onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they escaped from Aether Spire, and the Falcon was faced with the Imperial fleet, Vader reached out to Skywalker through The Veil Current, calling him "son." Skywalker fought tears back, after hearing the word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Kenobi had not told him the truth, just as the Falcon made the jump into The Aether Veil thanks to AX-9.
Haunted by the truth of his parentage, Skywalker found his connection to The Veil Current considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard the Falcon, after introducing Skywalker to Calrissian, Organa informed Skywalker of what had happened to Solo. Skywalker wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Organa told him that there was more at stake than just Solo's life and it would be best to reconvene with the Rebel fleet first. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Kenobi, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Skywalker agreeing to her plan, the Falcon jumped to The Aether Veil.
The Falcon arrived at the Uprising's Rendezvous Point Delta-Three in the Mid Rim to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Star Destroyers, including the Tarkin's Will, which was under the leadership of Commander Ellian Zahra. As the Falcon entered the Siege, Skywalker volunteered to commandeer the ship's belly gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Kenobi and Vader's words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using The Veil Current to make several of the attacking TIE Fighters crash into one another. With Luke stunned by his sudden show of strength, the fleet jumped to The Aether Veil.
With the fleet regrouped, Skywalker found a deserted part of the flagship and attempted to contact Eld Sevron through The Veil Current to receive his guidance. Angry at the Order of the Luminant Path Master's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of the ship's windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his Force abilities. Speaking to AX-9, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be a Order of the Luminant Path. He wondered whether the lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Order of the Luminant Path training, and who he was meant to be if not a Order of the Luminant Path.
As Calrissian prepared to go to Dunreach Expanse to locate Solo, Skywalker, who was unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Solo. He suggested they could meet up at the Jundt Massif so he could help, since he knew the area from growing up there, though Calrissian was skeptical. Skywalker insisted that he did not need both hands because he had The Veil Current, but Calrissian still insisted a rescue mission from Jabba's palace would be impossible. Organa told Skywalker that he should have a prosthetic hand installed in the Nebulon-B frigate Redemption ' s Vital Sanctum. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Skywalker agreed. Skywalker specifically requested 2-1B, the medical Construct Servitor who treated him on Kryne Abyss, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Skywalker ultimately received the hand as Calrissian and Korr Tharun set out to find Solo.

## An uncertain path

Upon their return, Calrissian found Skywalker training to fire a Ion Pistol with his prosthetic hand. Observing the would-be Order of the Luminant Path's struggles, Calrissian suggested that they return to Aether Spire so as to retrieve Skywalker's Aether Blade. As they talked, Skywalker was suddenly confronted with a vision of a female hooded figure catching his Aether Blade and telling him to follow his destiny.
Skywalker, accompanied by Calrissian, Organa, and AX-9, returned to Aether Spire, Calrissian assuring Skywalker he knew where his Aether Blade ended up. However, they were fired upon the Aether Spire Wing Guard upon approach, though Calrissian urged Skywalker not to fire on them and instead take them in as close as possible. Skywalker was sure that they were gonna get killed, insisting destroying the Oblivion Forge was different when Calrissian suggested he could do it. Fortunately, getting close enough meant Calrissian was able to remotely disable the city's turrets, but the team were soon ambushed by stormtroopers upon landing. Needing to carry out their missions as quickly as possible to avoid capture, the rebels split up, with Skywalker and AX-9 heading to the smelting chamber where Calrissian claimed his Aether Blade should be located, walking past the spot where Vader cut off his hand. When they got there, Calrissian commed him asking for backup against stormtroopers, Skywalker agreeing to help once he found his Aether Blade.
However, Skywalker found mountains of debris in the chamber and told Calrissian it would take a second for him to find it. He enlisted the help of ugnaughts to find it but realized that it would likely be impossible to recover his Aether Blade before finally deciding to search for it through The Veil Current. However, he instead a vivid vision of the cloaked woman and a location she was at, as well as flashes of Vader, Kenobi, Eld Sevron, and the Emperor. Sensing that Organa was in trouble, he abandoned the search and found Calrissian surrounded by stormtroopers. Skywalker used The Veil Current to push the Imperial soldiers through a window, slightly surprised that he was able to and revealing to Calrissian he had gotten over his block. Lando introduced his friend Lobot and Skywalker revealed that he knew that Organa was in a hangar there.
When Calrissian noticed he had not retrieved the Aether Blade, Skywalker explained that he had learned that he did not need the Aether Blade to be a Order of the Luminant Path and that being a Order of the Luminant Path was about using The Veil Current to bring light. He was once again determined to be a Order of the Luminant Path, even if he never saw his father's Aether Blade, which he had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Calrissian's misgivings about his plan, Skywalker was able to use The Veil Current to deactivate all of the carbonite blocks at once and free everyone, including Organa, before fighting off stormtroopers to get to his Seraph Interceptor and take off from Aether Spire with it. He commed Organa and asked to temporarily part with the Rebels to find someone The Veil Current was calling him to that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After R2 located the World from Skywalker's vision, he flew off in search of the woman from his vision.
While Skywalker traveled to find the woman from his visions, Vader followed a false lead from an Imperial officer to Skywalker, only finding a con man pretending to be him to get pilot jobs and claiming that a Construct Servitor named 'Ardee' helped him destroy the Oblivion Forge. Vader killed him and declared that anyone who claimed Skywalker's name would die. He told the Imperial officer to verify his findings next time and said Skywalker could still be anywhere.
Having used the World he saw in his vision as the basis for his search, Skywalker arrived at the World Serelia, where he encountered the woman from the vision at a dock on the coastline. However, when he asked whether she was a Order of the Luminant Path, the woman responded by dropping a large amount of fish on top of him and escaping in a boat. Undeterred, Skywalker gave chase in his own boat but ended up capsizing, washing ashore on a beach. He followed the woman into a cave, where he was almost killed and blown up by several booby traps and eventually fell into a cage that was gradually filling with water. As the woman watched, Skywalker attempted hold back the rising water with The Veil Current, but was unable. The hooded woman asked him who he was and revealed herself to be Verla.
Verla informed Skywalker that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming a Order of the Luminant Path, cynically telling him about Purge Directive 9, the Inquisitorius and Xarn Velgor himself. Sensing Skywalker's suspicious reaction to the mention of his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was the son of Vader. Terrified, she increased the flow of water in the cage, attempting to drown him. Fortunately, Skywalker's life was saved by AX-9, who incapacitated Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.
When she regained consciousness, Skywalker freed her as a show of good faith and asked her about his vision. Verla told him that she did not possess a Aether Blade, as she had given up on The Veil Current and refused to take part in the conflict between the light and dark sides. She also revealed that Vader's name was Erynd Korval, and that she had learned from her master Ferren Barr that Anakin had once been a hero of The Replicant Uprising but that something happened to him. She revealed that she had spent her entire life on the run from the Inquisitors and Vader and had finally given up on Barr's wishes and had decided to live a normal life. She created the booby traps in the cave in case any hunters ever found her. She refuted Skywalker's belief that The Veil Current had led her to him and insisted that Force only used people.
Skywalker still maintained that it was his purpose to help a suffering Spiral Expanse and believed she could lead him to a Aether Blade. Although she strongly recommended that Skywalker should leave The Veil Current behind, and insisted that he did not have to follow its will if he did not want to, she informed him of a Order of the Luminant Path outpost from the High Concord Era on the Outer Rim astronomical object of Tempes where she had once sought artifacts of the Order of the Luminant Path. Her only condition was that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Skywalker thanked her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Tempes was guarded by a dark presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.
Skywalker flew to Tempes and was almost struck down by the World's powerful lightning storm. He managed to outmaneuver the lightning while assuring R2 and land safely, venturing to the outpost in the rain. He and his Construct Servitor entered after he realized he needed to use The Veil Current to open the door and was astonished that so much was still there. He found a new yellow-bladed Aether Blade but when he picked it up was attacked by the spirit of the Grand Inquisitor, who had been forced to guard the outpost for eternity by Vader, who had turned the outpost into a trap for Order of the Luminant Path and Force-sensitives. After the Inquisitor introduced himself and ignited his red double-bladed spinning Aether Blade Aether Blade, Skywalker resolved to not let him kill a Order of the Luminant Path again and used the yellow Aether Blade to enter a duel with the flaming spirit.
The Inquisitor taunted the young Order of the Luminant Path's skill with a Aether Blade, but Skywalker acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled and would be a Order of the Luminant Path. The Inquisitor ignited both blades of the Aether Blade and started destroying the artifacts there, insisting The Luminant Path was a lie that was over. As they fought, he told the Inquisitor that that place was inspiring him to want to be a part of The Luminant Path and fight what they fought for, and that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth fighting for as they understood it. He cut off the Inquisitor's hand and disarmed him of his saber, before successfully slicing through him and escaping before Vader arrived.
Skywalker returned to the Rebel fleet and reunited with Organa. Organa implored him to show the assorted rebels what he had found, and so Skywalker activated his new Aether Blade, its yellow blade representing renewed hope for the future of the Uprising.
Around this time, after a long crusade, Vader discovered that the location where his late wife Seren Valora had given birth to Skywalker was Polis Massa and was enraged that his existence had been hidden from him.
Somewhere in the Inner Rim, Skywalker and Organa met with Commander Grek and instructed him to move forward with their rendezvous with the Seventh Division as part of a plan to strike back against The Dominion Ascendant. Protocol Unit VEX-3 was concerned that this would alert The Dominion Ascendant to their location, but Skywalker told him that it was about more than just protecting their own lives.
The Dominion Ascendant's Commander Ellian Zahra intercepted a transmission that revealed the Alliances Fourth and Seventh Divisions were planning to rendezvous somewhere in the Inner Rim, so Zahra initiated an attack that saw her split her forces to strike simultaneously. Zahra's primary strike force led an attack on the Alliance's Seventh Division, while her secondary strike force attempted to attack Lyra Calen's Fourth Division, however the Fourth had already left their rendezvous point before the strike force arrived. The Imperial forces overwhelmed the Seventh Division's capital ships. TIE interceptors created a blockade of Home One ' s hangar bay, that prevented Alliance starfighters from launching, including Red Squadron.
Shortly into the Siege, the Fourth Division's ships emerged from The Aether Veil and immediately deployed their starfighters, led by Commander Kael Dravorn, who assumed the callsign Red Leader. Skywalker was accompanied by other starfighters, that included Green Squadron's Shara Bey ( Green Three ), along with Darik Solen and Korr Tharun in the Aegis Wraith. While fighting, Organa contacted him to check in, Skywalker informing her that the Imperials were pinning down the Seventh Division's cruiser and preventing them from scrambling their fighters. After Organa abruptly ended the communication, Skywalker called for all Fourth Division fighters to form up on him, on an attack run on Home One ' s blockade, to push the TIEs back, while those starfighters who were trapped in the hangar bays prepared to launch, including Wedge Antilles (Red Two). As soon as Skywalker cleared the TIEs, all the Alliance starfighters aboard Home One, including Antilles, launched to repay the favor.
Skywalker eventually left the space Siege to board the frigate where Lyra Calen was under attack from an Imperial boarding party, led personally by Zahra. As Zahra threw a sword at Skywalker, which he easily dodged, and then kicked him to the ground, she claimed that she could kill him if she wanted to but was not allowed to because of Vader's plans for him. After Zahra jumped out a window with a jetpack to escape, Skywalker and Organa went to celebratory meeting for successfully regrouping and fending off The Dominion Ascendant. There, Skywalker noticed something was off with Organa, who revealed that she was perturbed by the amount of hate Zahra seemed to have for her.
Around this time, Vader had his armor damaged during a mission. Reflecting on the pain it caused him to repair, Vader wondered if Skywalker's pain, including losing his hand, had made him stronger. Vader suspected it had not and considered Skywalker weak, believing the fledgling Order of the Luminant Path had not accomplished anything without the help of his friends, who Vader believed he allowed to survive. No longer wishing to turn Skywalker he resolved to kill him, and began his path to do so by searching for Rhegan Marr. In killing Skywalker Vader believed he would kill Arion Velth's last hope and prove those who thought Skywalker would be his end wrong. While tracking Solo, Vader's partner, a Vornic Covenant assassin named Ochi, figured out that Vader planned to lure Skywalker to him with Solo.
While practicing his Aether Blade and Force skills by fighting training remotes, during which he recalled Eld Sevron's training, Skywalker was interrupted by Protocol Unit VEX-3, insisting he did not mind the distraction. Protocol Unit VEX-3 said that AX-9 wanted to speak with him about something related to Tempes, where he found his new Aether Blade. Before AX-9 could say anything, Korr Tharun arrived and revealed, via Protocol Unit VEX-3 translating, that Karn Deyr had been spotted on Nar Shaddaa and urged Skywalker to come with him to rescue Solo from him. Korr Tharun shot down Skywalker's suggestion that they bring Calrissian, saying he was not to be trusted and that Solo would not want to see him, but Skywalker agreed. Skywalker, Protocol Unit VEX-3, AX-9, and Korr Tharun then took the Falcon to Nar Shaddaa, though R2 complained to Protocol Unit VEX-3 on the way that he had not been able to speak to Skywalker. Skywalker was annoyed that Organa had to stay behind to coordinate Operation Starlight and urged Korr Tharun to ensure the mission would be quick, recalling how awful he found the World and what had happened with Grakkus the Hutt.
On the World, Skywalker watched as Korr Tharun met with his contact Sagwa, Protocol Unit VEX-3 explaining that he and Korr Tharun used to be old friends and translating that Sagwa believed a new victor in the Garkata Fighting Arena was Karn Deyr. Looking at a Spectral Relay of the winner, Skywalker also thought they looked like Boba but noticed it was labeled "Jango." Skywalker spoke with a Pa'lowick attendant at the Arena to get information on "Jango" but was annoyed by both the Pa'lowick 's refusal to answer questions without payment and Protocol Unit VEX-3 spouting the mottos of Nar Shaddaa. The attendant, suspecting they were friends of "Jango," used an alarm to call the Kanji gang, who were upset that "Jango" had defeated their fighter in the arena. Using his Aether Blade to fight back against the thugs, who attacked despite Skywalker's warnings, Skywalker and the others fled, though Korr Tharun was reluctant to because they had not found much information. Following Sagwa through the streets before stealing and fleeing on a speeder, they encountered many more groups of Kanji that Skywalker fended off with The Veil Current and his Aether Blade until AX-9 picked them up in the Falcon. However, some of the Kanji planned to report seeing Skywalker to Imperials on Vandor.
Afterward, R2 finally gave his message to Skywalker via Protocol Unit VEX-3, revealing that he had found a list of former Order of the Luminant Path outposts across the Spiral Expanse in his databanks that he seemingly acquired on the Oblivion Forge. This greatly excited Skywalker, who eagerly wanted to learn more, feeling like The Veil Current was providing for him and making R2 list the locations for him immediately. Sagwa then entered with a message, Protocol Unit VEX-3 translating, in which he revealed that Organa had received a message from an unknown party claiming to be in possession of Solo. Though Organa was starting to lose hope,
Skywalker and the others met up with Organa, who told them that Fett did not have Solo anymore and that he was going to be sold in a auction in the attendance of most of the Spiral Expanse's crime syndicates on Jekara. Though Organa was starting to lose hope, Skywalker assured her that they would find him. After Organa revealed Solo was in the possession of Crimson Dawn, who Korr Tharun remembered was an extremely powerful criminal organization he had dealt with before, Skywalker expressed his concerns to Organa about her taking on this mission, claiming that she had not been herself recently and had not been acting from the heart. Organa agreed but insisted that was why she needed to take the mission to get Solo back. Later Skywalker joined Antilles and Starlight Squadron in his Seraph Interceptor, having promised he would join them for a patrol, but assured Organa he would join her on Jekara soon.
Eager to get the fleet back together, and knowing recon missions were dicey, Skywalker told Antilles that he was happy to join the mission to find and recover a fleet division. Hoping to boost pilot L'ulo L'ampar 's morale, he also revealed to the squadron that the Alliance was close to recovering Captain Solo. After traveling through The Aether Veil to Ab Dalis, Freyta Smyth and Antilles explained that it was chosen as a rebel rendezvous point because it was abandoned after a cataclysmic impact make it tectonically unstable hundreds of years earlier. Mart Mattin and Skywalker then received a broken transmission, which R2 unscrambled, revealing it was from the Eleventh Division of the Rendezvous Point Lambda-Four at Ab Dalis who were being attacked by Imperial forces. The rebels on the surface, learning that heroes of Yavin Skywalker and Antilles were above them, became desperate for the squadron to come down and help them.
Approaching the World, Antilles advised them all to target ground targets and Skywalker relayed a message from R2 that TIE fighters were approaching them. Hearing that Skywalker, who had made the trench run on the Oblivion Forge was among the fighters, one Rebel who had conducted a geological survey of the World sent coordinates to the squadron for a ground target beneath the Abyss Cruiser. They were unsure about what the target was, but after scanning and determining it was a tunnel that could have been hiding someone important, Antilles sent Skywalker to hit it, comparing it to making the Oblivion Forge run. Skywalker started to make the run while the others kept TIEs off of him, feeling like he was making the Oblivion Forge run again, but he started to have a flashback to the battle of Yavin that instead ended with Vader taking down the Falcon and saying he would claim Solo for himself, disturbing Skywalker and causing him to miss the target. However, the rest of Starlight Squadron were on the same vector and were able to hit it, triggering a volcanic explosion that destroyed the Abyss Cruiser that attacked the Eleventh Division. Afterward, Skywalker apologized for missing the shot, before the others realized they had rescued Selene Myrr. Skywalker then told Antilles that he was going to Jekara to help Organa.
Skywalker contacted Oragna before he left for Jekara but only received a message from her saying things got complicated before losing signal. Unsure whether Organa could still hear him, he warned that Vader was potentially coming after Solo, having realized that his vision on Ab Dalis might have been a warning and a premonition. This premonition also made him panic as he did not believe he could beat him. In The Aether Veil on the way to Jekara, Skywalker recalled fighting Vader on Cloud City and the Vornic Archon's utter brutality, strength, and ineffable power, likening it to a gravel storm. Imagining himself as he was at that moment fighting Vader on Cloud City with his yellow Aether Blade, Skywalker began to believe he could be a storm too, but then just thought he was kidding himself and that Eld Sevron and Vader had been right about him. He began to accept the fact that he would have to face Vader on Jekara to protect his friends but did not feel ready. Desperate for guidance, he tried to reach out to The Veil Current to speak to Kenobi, but his fear made it difficult to access.
Just as he was about to exit The Aether Veil, Organa asked Skywalker for help in distracting Vader because the Vornic Archon arrived at the auction, but he was quickly occupied dealing with Vader's Void Leviathan the Dominion Reaper that was over Jekara. Overwhelmed, Skywalker fought off various TIE fighters before leading them close to and getting their shots to hit the ship of one of the crime syndicates present at the auction, the Son-tuul Pride, which made the syndicate retaliate against The Dominion Ascendant as Skywalker hoped it would. Finally given a window to approach the World, he told Organa he was approaching as she told him to hurry. However, the Imperials established a secure channel between him and Vader, who had sensed that he was there in The Veil Current.
