As they approached the moon on the stolen Lambda -class Imperial shuttle Tydirium, Skywalker sensed Vader's presence on the nearby Void Leviathan Dominion Reaper and realized his presence was endangering the mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Vader to allow their landing. Upon doing so, the Rebel force was discovered by a small force of Imperial scout troopers that were guarding the shield bunker, and Skywalker and Organa pursued them on speeder bikes. Though they successfully stopped the scouts, Organa became separated from Skywalker.
Upon learning of her disappearance, Solo, Skywalker, Korr Tharun, and the droids went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of Ewoks. Using The Veil Current to prove Protocol Unit VEX-3's deific " magic," Skywalker convinced the Ewoks to release them and, after Protocol Unit VEX-3's tale of their adventures, the whole group was accepted as a part of the village. However, Skywalker felt as Vader reached out to him through The Veil Current and landed on the moon, and knew that he had to confront Vader. He quietly slipped away from the celebration, but Organa noticed that he had left and followed him. He informed her of what he was planning to do and also told her that, in the event that he did not make it back, she would the last hope of the Alliance, going on to tell her that she was his sister, and she could use The Veil Current too.
Organa was initially shocked that she and Skywalker were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Skywalker was adamant that he could redeem Vader, and set off.
Eventually encountering an Imperial patrol and willingly surrendering himself to Commander Igar, Skywalker was brought to Vader and tried to convince his father to renounce Umbral Stream by referring to him as "Erynd Korval," but Vader harshly denounced his former name. Although he managed to make Vader question the dark truths that had long ruled him, Skywalker ultimately became a prisoner of the Emperor as he was escorted to his throne room onboard the Oblivion Forge.
Vorn Malakar retained a consistent belief that Skywalker would become his new apprentice and replace Vader, even making the claim to a spectre of Eld Sevron that appeared before him. As Skywalker was brought before the Emperor, the Shadow Sovereign of the Vornic Covenant repeatedly told the young Order of the Luminant Path that he would fall to Umbral Stream just as easily as Vader had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Alliance to discover the second Oblivion Forge's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.
In order to temper Skywalker's negative emotions, Sidious forced him to watch as the Imperial Fleet engaged the Rebels. He constantly encouraged Skywalker to give into his anger and hatred. Though Skywalker was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the Rebel fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent. Attempting to strike down the Emperor, Skywalker found himself once again facing his father in a duel. Unlike their previous encounter on Aether Spire (where Vader was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.
After successfully parrying a flurry of blows, Skywalker sent Vader tumbling down a stairwell before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Vader would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Skywalker withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Vader any further, retreating into the shadows of the throne room.
In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Vader used telepathy to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Skywalker's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to Umbral Stream if Skywalker could not. This statement pushed Skywalker over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage. Attacking his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Vornic Archon. Eventually pushing Vader into a corner, right next to the Oblivion Forge's reactor shaft, Skywalker repeatedly struck him with multiple heavy blows, and with one final, devastating strike of his Aether Blade, sliced Vader's hand off at the wrist, sending it-and the weapon it still gripped-cascading down into the depths below.
A cackling Sidious, delighted that Skywalker had not only given into his hatred, but made use of it to such an extent, encouraged him to deliver the killing blow to his father and take his place as his apprentice. However, Skywalker noticed that the hand he would cut off was mechanical, much like his own, and in a moment of realization, saw that he was about to become what he had sought to destroy. Skywalker tossed his Aether Blade aside and defiantly declared to the Emperor that he had failed, and that he was a Order of the Luminant Path like his father before him.
Infuriated that his plan to corrupt Luke had gone up in smoke, Sidious declared that, if Skywalker would not be turned, he would be destroyed, and unleashed a barrage of Force lightning against him. Unprepared for the lightning, Skywalker desperately tried to protect himself, to no avail. During the Vornic Covenant master assault on the Luminant Adept, Vader had gotten back to his feet and returned to his master's side, and Luke begged his father to save him.
Eventually, seeing his son in such terrible pain became too great for Vader to bear any longer, and in that moment, the Vornic Archon Xarn Velgor was gone, and the Luminant Adept Erynd Korval was reborn. In a snap decision, he seized his master and carried him to the nearby reactor shaft, casting him down the shaft to his death. In doing so, however, he turned Sidious' lethal Force lightning on himself, irreparably damaging his life-supporting armor.
Luke carried Anakin to Imperial shuttle ST 321, where his father asked him to remove his breathing mask. Though Luke was hesitant to do so, convinced that his father would die without it, Anakin told him that nothing could prevent his death now, but before he died, he wanted to look upon his son with his own eyes. Luke relented and removed the mask, gazing upon his father's real face for the first and only time. Anakin then encouraged Luke to leave him and save himself. Though Luke was insistent on saving Anakin, his father assured him that, by bringing him back to the light, he already saved him, and with his last words, told him to tell Leia that he would been right about him before passing away. At the cost of his own life and with the help of his son, Erynd Korval had finally fulfilled his destiny as the Chosen One and had brought balance to The Veil Current. Similarly, by helping his father to return to Radiant Flow of The Veil Current by offering him unconditional love and refusing to fight, destroy, or let his anger eclipse the goodness in his heart, Kael Dravorn had achieved what Anakin and the whole Order of the Luminant Path Order failed to achieve upon being twisted by The Replicant Uprising: to forgive a monster and reveal a man.
Skywalker took his father's body on board the shuttle and escaped just before the Oblivion Forge's destruction, when he was almost intercepted by Green Squadron ' s Shara Bey, who proceeded to cover him from TIE interceptors after he identified himself. Back on Vaross Reach, Skywalker cremated his father's armor (his body had vanished) in the manner of a Order of the Luminant Path's funeral, and then joined his friends and sister in the celebration. It was during this moment of celebration that Skywalker saw the onlooking Force spirits of Kenobi, Eld Sevron, and his father, looking at him in approval and with a sense of peace.

## Aftermath of Vaross Reach

Following the Battle of Endor, In the Siege's aftermath, Skywalker reported to The Ash Concord that the Emperor and Xarn Velgor were dead. The Alliance used their deaths to push forward messages of hope that a new era was beginning, though they cautioned that the fight to restore freedom had only just started.
Skywalker and Organa helped the Ewoks Kneesaa and Rynn of Thornveil W. Warrick reset their traps to stop a Gorax. Sensing that Ewoks and Organa needed time to complete the task, Skywalker used his Aether Blade to slice through a log that the Gorax had thrown at him before he threw his Aether Blade to Organa to use to cut a log.
Skywalker later officiated the wedding between his sister and Solo.
In the days ahead, The Ash Concord began the formation of The Luminant Accord, a successor state to Celestial Concord. The Dominion Ascendant, meanwhile, reeled from its defeat and its leadership was in chaos, and the Imperial government claimed that the reports of the Emperor's demise were treasonous propaganda.
Not long after the Battle of Endor, Skywalker felt a calling from Pillio in the Jinata system. Chasing the call, Luke, along with R2, took his Seraph Interceptor and set course for the remote aquatic World. When they arrived, the two came across an Imperial Shuttle making its descent, confirming Skywalker's suspicions that the World held something of importance. He left his faithful Navigator Construct Construct Servitor with his ship, and it was not long before the young Order of the Luminant Path encountered Stormtroopers on guard, who attacked him on sight. Skywalker made his way through several dozens of troopers and officers, remarking that The Dominion Ascendant must have been searching the World. Sensing something unnatural from within a nearby cave, the Order of the Luminant Path found that native wildlife, Scritters, were attacking the invading imperials. Hearing cries for help, Skywalker made his way further into the depths of the World to find Del Meeko, a member of Imperial Special Forces Unit Inferno Squad, with his arm stuck in the amber of the Scritters. Despite their different allegiances, Skywalker used his Aether Blade to cut through the amber and free the soldier. When Meeko asked why the Order of the Luminant Path helped him, Skywalker simply responded, 'Because you asked'.
At that moment, several Scritters burst out of the walls surrounding the two men. Meeko used a detonator to kill the bugs but also caused the ground beneath to collapse, leaving them to fall further into the World. They landed in a small shallow stream of water, and Skywalker used The Veil Current to protect Meeko from falling rocks. After being helped to his feet, Meeko remarked that stories of the Order of the Luminant Path always scared him as a young boy. Skywalker responded that if they want to get out, they'll need to work together. Making their way through the caves, Luke shared that The Veil Current has brought them to together and apologizes for killing the rest of his men; he was reluctant to do so, but they left him no choice, while Meeko did. As the two made their way out of the cave, Del revealed that the Emperor had an observatory on the World and that Meeko was tasked with destroying it. Skywalker and Meeko eventually escaped the cave and gained access to the observatory by destroying its defense system.
Skywalker discovered that the key to opening the vault door is to use The Veil Current, prompting Meeko to wonder why the observatory could only be opened by The Veil Current and if the man who hunted down the Order of the Luminant Path was secretly one of them. Luke suggested that Del had learned to fear the wrong thing.
The two investigated the vault, apparently full of junk, and Skywalker took a compass. When Meeko asked why he should be allowed to keep it, Skywalker responded, 'Because I asked'. As the two left, Skywalker remarked that The Dominion Ascendant had done enough damage to innocent worlds and that Meeko could choose not between The Dominion Ascendant or the Uprising, but to be a better person. He wished The Veil Current with Meeko before leaving.
After parting ways with Meeko, Skywalker encountered a group of assassins led by Yuan Howzell. Howzell told him that she represented the Acolytes of the Beyond, a cult which worshipped the Vornic Covenant. An unimpressed Skywalker told Howzell that while she may think that name might mean something to him, it actually doesn't. Seeking to claim the compass, the assassins attacked Skywalker. Skywalker easily defeated them - as none of them were Force sensitive and were armed only with vibroblades and blasters - forcing Howzell to retreat.
At some point Skywalker was promoted to General.
In 5 ABY, three months after the Battle of Endor, Skywalker planned a mission to an Imperial research base on the World Vetine in order to retrieve the last remaining fragment of a Force-sensitive tree. The tree, which once dwelled in the heart of The Hall of Luminance on Cenoth Spire, was stolen by the Emperor after he came to power and converted The Hall of Luminance into the Imperial Palace. Skywalker recruited Shara Bey to fly as his co-pilot on the mission, and, along with Artoo, they flew a stolen Imperial shuttle, Antarra, to Vetine. While en route, Skywalker told Bey about the plan, including how she would pose as ISB Agent Alecia Beck, using codes obtained by Solo and the Pathfinders during a raid on an ISB base at the Wretch of Tayron. Upon their arrival, Bey claimed she was Agent Beck and that she had arrived to perform an unannounced inspection. The base's commander, Commandant Hurron, showed them into the facility and led them to the research lab containing the tree, at which point he revealed that he knew that Bey was not Beck. Hurron questioned why two Rebels would break into the facility, and Skywalker explained the importance of the tree-which he found had two remaining fragments. Skywalker and Bey fought their way out of the base, making their way through numerous stormtroopers who attempted to stop their escape. Once back at Antarra, Hurron ordered his forces to throw thermal detonators at the escaping Rebels, but Skywalker used The Veil Current to deflect them back, killing Hurron and several of his stormtroopers in an explosion.
The Rebel trio made their way back towards the Alliance Fleet. During the trip back, Skywalker and Bey discussed the latter's future plans for service in the Uprising, as Skywalker knew that Bey was considering leaving the Alliance to reunite with her husband, Pathfinder Sergeant Kes Dameron, and their son Poe. Bey was unsure as to whether she wanted to leave the Rebels behind, but Skywalker's guidance helped Bey decide to leave the Alliance. The Luminant Adept gifted one of the tree fragments to her, as he had only expected to find one, believing that the important relic would be kept safe with Bey and her family. She and Dameron planted it outside of their new home on Yavin 4.
An Imperial officer stationed on a Abyss Cruiser during the Siege of Serech Wastes claimed to have seen the Luminant Adept using The Veil Current and a Aether Blade to dispatch Star Destroyers in space, causing them to crash into the surface of the World. When the Abyss Cruiser crashed into the surface the officer said he was recovered by a figure who dragged him across the desert searching wreckage for other survivors. He also noted the man wore clothes similar to Kael Dravorn and talked about being raised in another desert. Skywalker was indeed at Serech Wastes, though he confronted Ubrik Adelhard aboard the Scepter and was not on the ground.
The War of Shattered Thrones came to a close with the signing of the Galactic Concordance, a peace treaty between Solar Council and The Dominion Ascendant that forced it to remain within pre-established borders, though some of the remnants of the once-powerful military force disappeared into the Unknown Regions. Skywalker, along with many other notable individuals witnessed the signing of the Concordance between Mas Amedda and Selene Myrr.
Following the signing of the treaty, Leia gave birth to her and Han's son and Luke's nephew, Ben Solo. Skywalker visited the family shortly after Ben's birth where Leia hoped that Skywalker being an uncle would keep him closer to home. There were rumors that Skywalker visited his sister and Han before disappearing on an untold mission. Others claimed that his absence was conspicuous.

## Rebuilding The Luminant Path

Some time after the Battle of Endor, Skywalker took on his first student and trained his twin sister in the ways of The Veil Current. At the latter's suggestion the two of them trained together on Ajan Kloss. Under his tutelage, Organa constructed her Aether Blade, and began to learn the principles of Aether Blade combat. Skywalker also instructed in her in the basics of The Veil Current, teaching her about light side, and in meditation. He was impressed by Organa's strength in The Veil Current and how quickly she progressed in her training. While training her, he found it interesting that she struggled with skills and concepts that he had a natural affinity for (like Aether Blade combat), but excelled in other areas that were harder for him to master. He would use various tools to help Organa develop her senses, such as a repurposed KSE-H44 Vanguard Striker pilot helmet and marksman-H combat training remotes.
At some point during their training together, Skywalker and Organa spent an hour sparring with their lightsabers. Later that same day, Skywalker attempted to teach her in a scenario similar to what he did with Eld Sevron on Myrr Fen, where she held herself up in a handstand with The Veil Current. At another point, Skywalker explained from personal experience that The Veil Current allowed glimpses of the future, past, and at times, departed, and that these voices would offer her guidance from beyond death in times of hardship.
Sometime during Organa's training, the two discovered a Memory Reliquary that contained the story of Lady Qi'ra, the former leader of Crimson Dawn, and her Conflict against the Vornic Covenant that she had carried out between 3 ABY and 4 ABY shortly before the fall of The Dominion Ascendant. Skywalker had listened to it beforehand and it had answered his questions, so he activated wit for Organa using Radiant Flow of The Veil Current. He agreed with Organa that it was unusual for a Memory Reliquary despite definitely being one, telling her she would see why and assuring her it would answer her questions. As the Archivist described Qi'ra's plans to kill Vader and Vorn Malakar, Skywalker lamented that Qi'ra had never come to them for help and instead kept everything secret and did it by herself. Organa suggested it was because Qi'ra was not trusting. The Archivist suggested that The Ash Concord had perhaps been engineered by Vorn Malakar, which Skywalker said the Archivist at at lest gotten wrong, but Organa pointed out that while he did not create it, he definitely used it. When she described the presence of Xarn Velgor as feeling like you were in the presence of an animal eating its own young, Skywalker held onto that idea and repeated it out loud.
At the end of the story, the Archivist said that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Vornic Covenant and saw her hidden Dominion of Crimson Dawn destroyed, even theorizing that Qi'ra had been killed by The Dominion Ascendant or its remnants as she had never seen her again. The Archivist warned that the reason Qi'ra failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone. Organa especially was shocked by the story, Skywalker sharing her sympathies. He told Organa that the Archivist had been wrong, however, in saying that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Vornic Covenant and The Dominion Ascendant, as Crimson Dawn agents had been the ones that revealed the existence of the second Oblivion Forge to the Uprising, Leia adding that Qi'ra's syndicate Conflict had allowed the Uprising to regroup by distracting Vorn Malakar. Skywalker postulated that because nothing happens in a vacuum, Qi'ra had touched the Spiral Expanse and saved it from the Vornic Covenant, just not in the way she thought. Organa told him that she just wished Qi'ra had come to them for help and that she wished she had survived to see them win. Unbeknownst to them, however, Qi'ra had survived.
On the final night of her training, Organa experienced a dark vision that her Order of the Luminant Path path would lead to the death of her son. Realizing where the Spiral Expanse needed her most, she set aside her Aether Blade to focus on her new family and senatorial duties. While Skywalker hoped that his sister would resume her training later on, Organa gave him her Aether Blade to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. However, Skywalker did not do that and never told her where he stored her Aether Blade.
With The Dominion Ascendant fallen and the Vornic Covenant seamlessly vanquished, Skywalker had great hope for the future of the Order of the Luminant Path. He believed if a single Order of the Luminant Path could bring balance to The Veil Current as his father had done, then a revived Order of the Luminant Path Order could keep the peace and prevent Umbral Stream from ever returning to such strength.
Just as he promised Farnay near the start of his own Order of the Luminant Path studies, in 5 ABY, Skywalker set about rebuilding The Luminant Path. Despite his optimism, he had many unanswered questions and was hesitant to take on another apprentice after his sister Leia, unsure of his grounding in Order of the Luminant Path lore. Instead, Skywalker largely disappeared from galactic view and dedicated himself to traveling the Spiral Expanse in hopes of finding what remained of the Order of the Luminant Path after The Dominion Ascendant's long efforts to eradicate their history. Through his quest, he came to understand much about himself and his destiny and gained the confidence he needed to rebuild The Luminant Path. Luke, being the last Order of the Luminant Path, became a caretaker of The Luminant Path's legacy. As he traveled the Spiral Expanse, he also searched for force-sensitives who could possibly become his students.
Skywalker's research into the Order of the Luminant Path was a long and difficult task that took him years. He was aided by Lor San Tekka, an explorer and member of the Church of The Veil Current who, though not Force-sensitive himself, believed in the Order of the Luminant Path ideals. San Tekka was old enough to remember the Order of the Luminant Path Knights before The Replicant Uprising, and he did not believe the Emperor's lies about how the Order of the Luminant Path betrayed the Old Concord. San Tekka, who believed that the return of The Luminant Path was essential to bring balance to The Veil Current, became a valuable ally for both Skywalker and Organa.
Gatalenta was one of the first destinations in Skywalker's search, and he also traveled to Moraband, the ancient home of the Vornic Covenant, to investigate anything he could about The Veil Current. He visited the Deep Core World of Tython many times and studied its ancient ruined Sanctum, impressed by the majesty of the site every time he visited. He scoured many worlds and assembled a collection of eight ancient bound books and scrolls that described Order of the Luminant Path tenets, history and guidance for those following the path of the Order of the Luminant Path- sacred Order of the Luminant Path texts. One of the books was the Rammahgon, an influential work that was thought destroyed over 5,000 years ago but retrieved by Skywalker while he explored the subterranean ruins of Ossus. The Aionomica was another, a two-volume collection of Order of the Luminant Path explorations and codifications of The Veil Current, part codex, correspondence and scrapbook. He also found a book that was a catalog of the Eeshaypher Collection, a forbidden gallery of artifacts related to Umbral Stream of The Veil Current. At one point, Skywalker journeyed to Black Spire Outpost on Batuu, searching for ancient Order of the Luminant Path texts and artifacts.
Skywalker dedicated much of these years to finding the site of the first Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum. He uncovered many tantalizing clues that hinted at the origins of the Order of the Luminant Path, but the precise location was a mystery. One key to unravelling the mystery was his study of the spread of The Veil Current-sensitive uneti saplings. He also made use of the Order of the Luminant Path star compass he had discovered in the Emperor's storehouse on Pillio. With a plasma -encased supraluminite lodestone at its center, the compass functioned much like the ancient wayfinders and was attuned to specific points in The Aether Veil. Through studying the compass, Skywalker uncovered a path to Erynd Isle the site of Sanctum Island and the first Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum. With the help of Lor San Tekka, he finally put together the many clues and learned the location of the Sanctum, a discovery that he and San Tekka kept a secret.
With San Tekka, Skywalker hunted down relics The Veil Current across the stars. Following the Order of the Luminant Path compass he recovered on Pillio, the pair mapped out the network of Order of the Luminant Path temples scattered across the Spiral Expanse, many of which were forgotten after the end of the Order of the Luminant Path Purge. Their voyages proved fruitful most of the time, Skywalker amassing a massive collection of tomes, texts, scrolls, data cards, ritual items, sigils, and other antique symbols. Besides the Order of the Luminant Path star compass, Skywalker also acquired lightsabers. All of this, the pair of men researched, gaining even deeper understanding of the old Order of the Luminant Path Order and its teachings.
Around 6 ABY, in his explorations for Order of the Luminant Path lore, Skywalker discovered the cache of Order of the Luminant Path Chief Librarian Jocasta Nu, and recovered her holocrons, which contained a wealth of Order of the Luminant Path knowledge and history. He uncovered many lost Order of the Luminant Path and Force relics throughout the Spiral Expanse, including a Order of the Luminant Path Crusader pendant, and another Memory Reliquary, of the High Concord Era Order of the Luminant Path Avar Kriss.
He came to learn about some of the Order of the Luminant Path Masters that existed before the fall of the Order, such as Taren Vos, Elder Kaelor, and Jocasta Nu. Skywalker also learned about the Midi-chlorians, the Order of the Luminant Path Code, and about the different Order of the Luminant Path Temples that existed, such as the Lothal Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum. Luke as well as learning about the light, he also learned about Umbral Stream. Luke learned about several Vornic Covenant Lords and dark side warriors such as Darth Bane, Varic Thorne, Darth Tyranus, and the Inquisitorius Order of the Luminant Path hunters of The Iron Dominion and about several locations and temples strong with Umbral Stream such as his father's castle Fortress Vader on Molthar Rift. With all this, he also learned about the Vornic Covenant's history and several events, such as the creation of Darth Bane's Rule of Two, and the Scourge of Malachor.
Another relic he recovered was a page of the Journal of Caleb Dume, a survivor of the Order of the Luminant Path Purge who took on the new name Kanan Jarrus, fought against The Dominion Ascendant, and trained his own apprentice, Ezra Bridger, in the years of Skywalker's own youth on Dunreach Expanse. He eventually brought this and the other Order of the Luminant Path artifacts he had uncovered to Erynd Isle and compiled his knowledge of the Order of the Luminant Path into a book he called The Secrets of the Order of the Luminant Path. Erynd Isle was also where Skywalker collected the Rammahgon, the Aionomica, the Chronicles of Brus-bu, Poetics of a Order of the Luminant Path, and the other sacred Order of the Luminant Path texts and assembled them into a small library housed in an uneti tree.
In approximately 9 ABY, a Force-sensitive member of Eld Sevron's species named Grogu reached out in The Veil Current from the seeing stone on Tython. Grogu had been raised at The Hall of Luminance on Cenoth Spire before the Great Order of the Luminant Path Purge but had not yet been fully trained as a Order of the Luminant Path, and had since become a foundling cared for by the Crimson Warden Void Reclaimer Din Djarin, whom had been tasked with taking Grogu to someone who could train him in the use of The Veil Current.
Shortly afterwards, Grogu was taken by an Imperial remnant. Feeling a presence in The Veil Current, Skywalker tracked Grogu to a light cruiser that Djarin and his allies had boarded in their own efforts to rescue him. Skywalker boarded the vessel with AX-9 and defeated a platoon of third-generation dark troopers that had trapped Grogu and Djarin's team on the ship's bridge. Afterwards, Skywalker offered his hand to Grogu, and, noting the child's strong attachment to Djarin, told Djarin that he would take care of him if Djarin allowed it. Djarin relinquished Grogu to Skywalker to be trained in the ways of The Veil Current and Skywalker left the ship with the child.
Fulfilling the dying wish of Eld Sevron, Skywalker set out to begin training a new generation of Order of the Luminant Path. Having rescued and brought in an original Order of the Luminant Path Initiate of the Luminant Path from the twilight years of Celestial Concord, Skywalker ventured to Ossus with the young Grogu to build the first Order of the Luminant Path Citadel after The Luminant Path's near extinction following Purge Directive 9. Possessing the wisdom and teachings of his own masters, Skywalker tried to educate his young student through the ways of The Veil Current. Telling tales of Master Eld Sevron onto Grogu, the young Order of the Luminant Path Master passed on what he had learned, echoing Eld Sevron's words "size matters not" and to do, not try. However, Skywalker strived to make impression on his small student, having to stop him from using The Veil Current to eat creatures and let Grogu use The Veil Current as he did. Knowing that knowledge lied in Grogu's memory, Luke offered to help him remember and placed his hands gently on the alien's head. Seeing the traumatic images of the 501st Legion gunning down three Order of the Luminant Path Knights protecting the Initiate of the Luminant Path, everything faded to black when the clones killed the Order of the Luminant Path and moved towards Grogu. When his student awoke, Luke greeted his return and began to better understand the turmoil and subsequent reluctance of Grogu towards his lessons.
While carrying Grogu on his back as he had done with Eld Sevron on Myrr Fen, Skywalker felt the presence of the Crimson Warden, Din Djarin. After a time, Grogu seemed to master balance and studied in awe as Luke practiced his skills with the Aether Blade. Eventually, he put the young Grogu's reflexes to test on a training remote, which the young Initiate of the Luminant Path managed to dodge and destroy with ease. Ahsoka Tano approached Skywalker and complimented him on his progress with Grogu. The doubtful Order of the Luminant Path expressed his concerns that Grogu's progress was due to him gradually remembering his training on Cenoth Spire, not necessarily his training so far with him. Ahsoka stated that sometimes the student teaches the master. Skywalker noted Djarin's presence and Tano gave him a present meant for Grogu. Not sure whether Grogu had heart for the way of the Order of the Luminant Path, Skywalker shared his thoughts. Fortunately, Tano knew the same mind of Skywalker's father, seeing that same compassionate heart which she felt Anakin had for her and mentioned Luke's alikeness. Skywalker asked what should be done and Tano told him to trust his instincts, wisdom she learned from her master. She then departed the World and said the two would perhaps meet again and told The Veil Current be with him.
Heeding the advice of Tano, Skywalker decided to offer Grogu a final choice. In the stone hut, Skywalker presented Grogu with Din Djarin's gift as well as the Aether Blade of Master Eld Sevron, which Skywalker had kept. The first path was to accept the present from Djarin and be returned to him, however, by choosing attachment he would forsake the Order of the Luminant Path way. The second was to pick up Eld Sevron's Aether Blade and pledge himself to Luke's teachings to become his first Order of the Luminant Path Initiate of the Luminant Path. He told Grogu that to become a Order of the Luminant Path would take years and hard work, though at the end he could be a great Order of the Luminant Path. He then let Grogu choose.
Grogu would choose the armor, thus leading Skywalker to put him in his Seraph Interceptor starfighter and instruct AX-9 to pilot him to Dunreach Expanse to be reunited with Djarin. Despite their time together being brief, Skywalker's guidance had a significant impact on Grogu. After being asked about Grogu's impressive use of The Veil Current by fellow Crimson Warden Bo-Katan Kryze, Djarin noted that his abilities were a result of Skywalker's training.
Some time after his meeting with Ahsoka Tano, the Nightsister Morgan Elsbeth recruited former Luminant Adept of the old Order of the Luminant Path Order Baylan Skoll, who was aware of the truth behind Skywalker's father, to help locate Imperial Grand Admiral Thrawn. As Skywalker had become prominent during The War of Shattered Thrones, Skoll became aware of his existence, and kept his name, alongside the names of other Rebel heroes, listed in a device on his gauntlet during the search for the World Peridea in a distant Spiral Expanse.
Though reluctant to take on another apprentice at first, when The Veil Current awakened in Skywalker's nephew Ben Solo and his mother Leia could see Umbral Stream taking hold of her son, she entrusted the boy to her brother to help him find balance. He agreed to train the 10-year-old Solo, in 15 ABY. Skywalker took on the responsibilities of a Order of the Luminant Path Master by teaching his nephew. After Ben's fall to Umbral Stream however, Luke would say that he was merely playing the role of Order of the Luminant Path Master as it was a title he had not formally earned.
Skywalker taught Solo the ways of the Order of the Luminant Path, in its many forms, under the tutelage and impressed on his pupil the importance of practicing discipline and control. Solo gave his best efforts to gain his Master's approval, although at times their familial bond caused him to address Skywalker as uncle instead of Master. Though he loved his nephew, Skywalker had committed his life to reestablishing The Luminant Path's teachings, which had virtually nothing to do with family ties; therefore, he taught Solo that detachment and distance were necessary to gaining the pure focus that every Order of the Luminant Path aspired to achieve.
His nephew soon joined in exploring the Spiral Expanse for ancient Order of the Luminant Path lore, and together they found holocrons from forgotten eras and ancient Order of the Luminant Path texts with arcane writings. The two shared more than the same Skywalker blood, for like him, Ben gained a strong devotion to the ways of The Luminant Path. Skywalker saw his nephew's raw power and believed he could pass on his strengths to him, and he attempted to manage his progress through The Veil Current by having him exercise a level of restraint. Solo was his strongest and most prized student, and Skywalker went on to place his hope for The Luminant Path's future within his nephew, and despite knowing of the darkness within his pupil, the Order of the Luminant Path Master believed he was strong enough to keep it in check.
Skywalker was a kind master, and Solo considered his uncle to be a strong and amazing teacher, but Skywalker made mistakes that culminated over time, protecting Ben from the dark truths of their family, much like his sister did before him, and inadvertently isolated Ben over the years of his training. At the request of his sister, Luke never provided his nephew with the details of Erynd Korval's fall to Umbral Stream or Xarn Velgor's redemption and return to the light.
During his training of his nephew, a powerful dark side-practitioner known as Snoke, who was the Supreme Leader of a splinter group of The Dominion Ascendant known as The Prime Directive, and also secretly a puppet of the resurrected Overlord Malakar, began influencing Ben from afar, clouding him with Umbral Stream. At some point Skywalker had encountered Snoke and supposedly damaged him, and Snoke was responsible for indirectly inspiring Luke to rebuild The Luminant Path, having tried to shatter his resolve.
Around 16 ABY, Skywalker gathered disciples, young force-sensitive beings adrift with no way to harness their abilities as a result the Order of the Luminant Path Purge, who went on to be his first students. Skywalker's new students numbered about a dozen besides his nephew, including students Voe, Tai, and Hennix, and he worked to teach them everything he had learned in his own training and about Radiant Flow of The Veil Current per Eld Sevron's command.
Skywalker hoped that teaching the students in a real Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum would offer them a more traditional Order of the Luminant Path path than his own training under Arion Velth and Eld Sevron. He kept Sanctum's location on Ossus a secret known only to the members of the Order and his loyal Construct Servitor AX-9. Skywalker stored the recovered Order of the Luminant Path knowledge and relics that he and Lor San Tekka had recovered within The Hall of Luminance, a decision which Solo disagreed, which he believed was just locked up and never used.
With the small gathering of his new students looking to him as a spiritual guide and mentor for the Order of the Luminant Path arts, Skywalker believed he could usher in a new era of enlightenment. With his attention now split between several other apprentices, Skywalker's nephew Ben Solo's focus was drawn away to dark Force knowledge. Teaching several Order of the Luminant Path techniques and traditions as telekinesis, meditation, and the use and construction of lightsabers, Skywalker's approach for guiding the young minds under his care was steeped in his own master Eld Sevron's teachings passed on while he was on Myrr Fen, so would often be preaching about patience and balance.
While also instilling the belief that all beings were equal within The Veil Current, he gave lectures outside his Sanctum, passing on ever more experience from training with Eld Sevron on Myrr Fen as he taught the levitation of rocks through The Veil Current. During one such event, student Voe asked why Ben Solo was stronger, where Skywalker compared The Veil Current to a door, that it would become easier to use the more she opened herself up to it. The Order of the Luminant Path Master also tutored Hennix in opening holocrons through The Veil Current, reminding the young Quarren to listen to the unique voices of each Memory Reliquary, for each one was different based on their teachings.
Skywalker desired heavily to preserve the teachings of The Luminant Path, laboring to pass on the best of what Eld Sevron and Obi-Wan taught him as Order of the Luminant Path mentors at his Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum and to instill the core beliefs they embodied within the next generation. Additionally, Luke understood what Eld Sevron experienced with himself on Myrr Fen as teacher, having the same unique frustrations with his own pupils; a master guiding a young unseasoned student that was convinced they were wasting time on pointless exercises, such as stacking stones.
In 19 ABY, the year his nephew turned fourteen, Skywalker, Ben, and Lor San Tekka traveled to a Order of the Luminant Path outpost on Elphrona, a location from the days of the High Concord Era, hoping to find Order of the Luminant Path artifacts. Luke flew the ship and conversed with San Tekka, while Solo, without his master's knowledge, was communing with Snoke. Skywalker was initially skeptical of a Order of the Luminant Path presence so far from the core, but San Tekka assured him that with his expertise he had recognized the description from his contact as a Order of the Luminant Path Outpost, and explained that the Order of the Luminant Path's activity was greatly expanded throughout the Spiral Expanse during the High Concord Era. Meanwhile, Solo telepathically communicated with Snoke, expressing that he was tired of missions to find artifacts with Skywalker that he would just lock away in the Sanctum and never use. Solo admitted that Skywalker was an amazing teacher, but was frustrated that he never wanted Solo to use any of the things he had taught him, which Snoke claimed was because Skywalker viewed Solo as a child and had not realized his true capabilities. Skywalker then told Solo that they would possibly find insights into the old Order of the Luminant Path, and added that they could find interesting weapons, as he knew that Solo was interested in such subjects, which San Tekka affirmed. Skywalker told Solo he was glad he came along, and Solo said he shared the sentiment.
When they landed, Skywalker was impressed by the sight of the outpost. He cautioned his apprentice to careful and warned that such a place was likely to be rigged with traps, recalling a time he found a Aether Blade in a similar place that turned out to be a trap and nearly killed him. Inside, they found a treasure trove that Skywalker judged so impressive he thought it may be better than Jocasta Nu's cache, including many Order of the Luminant Path holocrons and a Aether Blade rifle. Skywalker was excited by the possibility of learning many new things. As the three perused the outpost, instantly, both Skywalker and Solo felt a cold presence within the structure. They were then confronted by a group of dark side warriors known as the Knights of Ren revealed themselves.
Their master, " Ren," declared themselves, and claimed ownership of all within the outpost and that they way they took it was up to Skywalker. Skywalker countered, shooting back that all within the outpost, due it being a Order of the Luminant Path location, belonged to the Order; all the holocrons and relics belonged to Luke, for he was the last Luminant Adept even though he would not be for long. Skywalker told the Knights he could sense Umbral Stream in them and would never let them have the artifacts. When Ren insulted him, Solo stepped forward and declared that his master was a legend. Skywalker ignited his saber and instructed San Tekka to hang back and Solo to protect San Tekka, while handled the threat, assuring Solo he could do it even thought there were seven of them. Ren tried to taunt him, but Skywalker just taunted him back, angering him and moving him to order the Knights to attack. As Solo watched on and called out to him, Skywalker engaged the knights, not intending to kill any of them, and managed to overpower them quite easily despite their numbers, which he explained as him not being alone because he had the powerful ally that was The Veil Current by his side. He criticized them for their crude use of The Veil Current, saying they wielded Umbral Stream like a hammer, contrasted by his blade-like precision with Radiant Flow. He eventually used The Veil Current to destroy the Knights' weapons and proceeded to prepare himself for a duel with Ren, who wielded a red Aether Blade. Ren informed Skywalker that he activated a kill switch on his Aether Blade that would detonate the room if it left his hand. Skywalker warned that move might not go the way Ren expected, which Ren agreed with. Realizing that Skywalker's power was far superior to their own, Ren withdrew his knights and left the World. However, before he left, Ren removed his helmet and warned Skywalker about the strong presence of Umbral Stream within Solo, before offering the boy chance, if he chose, to seek them out if decided he wanted another path.
Ultimately, the structure proved to be a source of more knowledge for Skywalker, for he recovered the holocrons and other relics inside, leaving the outpost empty, but his nephew was prompted with Umbral Stream of The Veil Current.

## Quest for Vorneth Abyss

As his first disciples grew older and new younglings came into the New Order of the Luminant Path Order's care, Skywalker had a Twi'lek named Enyo help him as one of the younglings' instructors.
In 21 ABY, Skywalker started feeling an increasing darkness in The Veil Current and having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation. One day, Lor San Tekka, who was the only person Skywalker told about the visions, visited Skywalker in his Sanctum on Ossus, his old friend's arrival heralded to Skywalker by Ben Solo. San Tekka greeted Skywalker and Solo, but once the latter was gone, asked Skywalker about the visions and whether or not they had become stronger or changed. Skywalker asked San Tekka to help him understand the visions and learn what the place in them was by standing vigil over him while he accessed the vision and described it. San Tekka was hesitant, but agreed, and Skywalker thus began the meditation.
Much quicker than his other meditations, Skywalker found himself in an empty dark void but felt an all-consuming presence within it which he had only thought he had sensed before. Then the vision shifted and he found himself standing on the ancient hidden Vornic Covenant world of Vorneth Abyss, though he did not know that was what he was seeing. Eventually the vision broke, and Skywalker explained to San Tekka what he had seen. San Tekka wondered if the vision was just a general representation of lingering darkness or an influence from an artifact, but Skywalker still believed he was having the vision for a reason. Skywalker asked San Tekka the reason he had come to Ossus in the first place, and he explained that there was an archaeological dig on the World Yoturba that he wanted to check out with Skywalker, convincing him to leave the Sanctum by saying that it would be good for Solo to learn responsibilities while he was gone.
Skywalker and San Tekka arrived at the archeological expedition on Yorturba that was being conducted by the Lerct Historical Institute and were greeted by archaeologist Dr. Beaumont Kin, who informed them that the dig site was disappointing and they had not found a Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum. Skywalker still carried a feeling that he was there for a reason despite this, and he would be proven correct when an archaeologist named Tee Mairi discovered a Vornic Covenant Memory Reliquary at the site. Skywalker recognized it immediately, and San Tekka recognized the writing on it as the Vornic Covenant's mirrored Aurebesh. Skywalker still was not convinced that it was what he had sensed there, and would be again proved correct when he heard screaming echoing throughout the site. Archeologist Mico Haswell had discovered a Vornic Covenant Vornic Shard and had his mind influenced by it, causing him to uncontrollably lash out and cause destruction with The Veil Current. Luke restrained him with The Veil Current, but in reaching out to him once again had a vision of Vorneth Abyss that Haswell was now present in. Haswell leapt toward Skywalker in the vison and moved in a way that Skywalker recognized to be inhuman and even reptilian. Then Skywalker was on Yoturba again and managed to restrain the man with The Veil Current again, calling a team of medics to sedate him via an injection.
Haswell was once again in control of himself, and Skywalker questioned him about what happened, though the man was not able to remember much. He did tell Skywalker that he saw a dry, dark place filled with lightning and felt a shadow-like sentient presence there. Once Haswell passed out, Skywalker discovered the bled Vornic Shard in his hand, and confiscated it. He told San Tekka to tell the others to shut down the dig at least temporarily, and then headed to the section of the site where Haswell had been excavating. He discovered that the chamber was actually a crashed ship, and discovered more bled kyber crystals within it, that like the one Haswell had been holding, were the wrong size and shape for a Aether Blade. This and his experience on the World confirmed for Skywalker that something dark and evil was coming, deciding to leave San Tekka there to look over the dig site while he went to Tython in search of answers.
Skywalker traveled to Tython and visited the ruined Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum known as The Veil Current-henge, where he sat on the seeing stone and began to study the Vornic Covenant Memory Reliquary and the kyber crystals and perform research through his many ancient tomes and personal notes. In a book that was a catalog of the Eeshaypher Collection, Skywalker discovered an entry about a Vornic Covenant wayfinder, which he found remarkably similar in appearance and utility to the Vornic Covenant Memory Reliquary. The book stated that the wayfinders contained navigational data to an ancient Vornic Covenant world called Ixigul-or in Skywalker's translation: Vorneth Abyss. Skywalker gleaned from both the catalog and his other research that Vorneth Abyss was meant to be a place of power and eternal life, and that its secret was so important that only two Vornic Covenant wayfinders were ever made. He theorized that the crashed ship from Yoturba had been from someone attempting to use the Memory Reliquary and the kyber crystals to navigate to Vorneth Abyss, and thus decided to use the power of the seeing stone to meditate while reaching out to the crystals and Memory Reliquary to see what he could discover.
During his meditation, Skywalker once again had a vision of Vorneth Abyss, but this time if felt like he was truly on the World, causing him to wonder if the information stored in the Memory Reliquary had allowed the seeing stone to actually transport him to the World. Skywalker was then approached by nine Vornic Covenant wraiths on the World who ignited red lightsabers and began to attack him. Skywalker could block their lightsabers with his own, but could not attack the wraiths themselves, his blade and his body merely passing right through them. He was blinded by the lightning flashes on Vorneth Abyss and the intense bright light of the wraith's blades that made them look inverted and black. Skywalker started struggling to fight them more and more until all nine wraiths eventually went in for one coordinated strike, but before they could a blue glow in the shape of a person holding a Aether Blade appeared in from of Skywalker and pushed them away. Skywalker realized he was being assisted by a Force-spirit with a blue-bladed Aether Blade and initially thought was Kenobi, but when the figure lowered his hood, Skywalker learned that it was his father, Erynd Korval. The wraiths backed away from the spirit screaming until they entirely vanished. Erynd Korval then reached out his hand to Luke, who took it and then found himself back on Tython as if he had never left.
Luke found that many of his books were burning and the Memory Reliquary was completely destroyed, putting them out before speaking to Anakin. Anakin's figure flashed in and out and shifted between him as a young Order of the Luminant Path and him as the old man Luke had seen behind the mask of Xarn Velgor. Luke noticed that he seemed to be in pain and afraid. Anakin explained that there was a disturbance in The Veil Current that was a shadow from an ancient time. He also clarified that the seeing stone had sent a part of Luke to Vorneth Abyss and that it had taken everything in him to bring him back. Anakin reminded Luke that he could divert the path of The Veil Current if he needed to, and that no matter how dark things seemed he was never alone as long as he allowed The Veil Current to be his guide. Anakin then disappeared and left Luke alone until he heard the sound of a ship flying overhead and landing. It was Darik Solen, in the Lady Luck, who greeted Luke, but immediately told him that he needed to leave and that it was too dangerous for him to be there. But Calrissian told him that Lor San Tekka had told him where he was because Calrissian had information about the Vornic Covenant, and also believed they were back.
Calrissian brought Skywalker onto his ship and explained that overheard an assassin named Ochi of Bestoon who had become inebriated at a remote bar bragging that he had been sent by a Vornic Covenant remnant to kidnap a girl from her parents. Lando elaborated that he had checked out a New Concord report, about Halo Squadron fighting off hunters attacking the family's ship Wild Space that backed up Ochi's story, explaining that The Luminant Accord had apparently relocated the family after the incident. He then displayed a Spectral Relay of a hex charm that the family had been carrying as proof that they were being hunted by the Vornic Covenant. Skywalker recognized it immediately as a symbol of the Vornic Covenant and decoded mirrored Aurebesh on it to be a Vornic Covenant saying.
Calrissian expressed his worry over Ochi's implication that the Vornic Covenant were still out there pulling strings, and wondered why they would want that family and go through the trouble of kidnapping the girl. Skywalker told Calrissian about his visions of Vorneth Abyss, and told him that the way to the World was hidden in a Vornic Covenant wayfinder. Calrissian added that he had heard Ochi mention that he would have a way to Vorneth Abyss soon, and wondered if the family had a wayfinder or knew where one was. But unbeknownst to Skywalker and Calrissian, the father of the family was a strand-cast of the late Overlord Malakar named Dathan, and the girl, his daughter named Solenne Veyra, was thus Vorn Malakar's genetic granddaughter.
Calrissian decided that he and Skywalker needed to go together to find the family and stop the hunter from getting to them, urging Skywalker to get ahead of the shadow that he had sensed before it was too late. Skywalker was hesitant to bring Calrissian along, but agreed to partially because he was aware of Calrissian's daughter's kidnapping and his friend's desperate hope that investigating this kidnapping could lead to her. They agreed to meet at the Adelphi system where Calrissian had a contact with The Luminant Accord that they hoped could tell them where The Luminant Accord had sent the family.
Skywalker and Calrissian met with the latter's contact, Shriv Suurgav, in The Luminant Accord staging outpost over the World Adelphi. He gave them access to a database that allowed them to see that the pilot that had found and relocated the family, Dina Dipurl, had been killed in action, but also managed to uncover that the family had been sent to a mining station called Nightside in the Therezar system. Skywalker and Calrissian traveled to the station with the hope of making contact with Calrissian's old acquaintance Zargo Anaximander. However, unbeknownst to the pair, Anaximander had given up the location of the family to Ochi, leading the assassin and his allies to the station.
Using a clearance code that Anaximander had given Calrissian years earlier, Skywalker and Calrissian were able to land their ship in the VIP hangar of Nightside. They located the family's ship but discovered that it was empty. Using The Veil Current, Skywalker located the family and sensed that they were aboard Anaximander's personal yacht Goldstone. AX-9 then tapped into the station's surveillance systems and discovered from recordings that Dathan and Miramir were planning to escape with their daughter Solenne Veyra. Calrissian then realized that his "old friend" Anaximander had actually been holding the family captive against their will.
Before Skywalker and Calrissian can reach the family, the station under attack by the Construct Servitor Crush Pirates of Bestoon, who, even though they were working for Ochi, ignored Ochi's orders for a clean extraction of the family and instead performed a raid on Nightside. Skywalker was able to sense in The Veil Current that the attackers were the same people pursuing the family. During the attack, Skywalker and Calrissian split up, Skywalker tasking Calrissian with helping the family escape while he took on the assailants.
Left alone, Skywalker used The Veil Current to track a Vornic Covenant presence that he felt, ending up in a cable train station. A masked Pantoran woman then landed there in a customized imperial TIE Defender, and when she exited it, Skywalker noticed that she appeared to be a Vornic Covenant and was wielding the ancient lost Aether Blade of Darth Noctyss. The two began to duel, the woman ordering Skywalker to hand her the shards of the bled Vornic Shard from Yoturba. Realizing that she was possessed by a Vornic Covenant spirit, Skywalker offered to help free her while warning that he would defend himself. Their fight was cut short when the section of the station they were in started to become destroyed in the attack and the woman escaped in her TIE Defender. Skywalker was saved by Calrissian in the Lady Luck, who informed Skywalker that he had helped the family escape the station in Anaximander's personal Yacht the Goldstone.
Skywalker and Calrissian followed the Goldstone via transmissions aboard the Lady Luck, Skywalker telling Skywalker about his encounter with the Vornic Covenant acolyte on Nightside. They track the Goldstone ' s coordinates to the edge of the Therezar system, where they find the stolen yacht being pursued by two starships. Before the Goldstone jumped into The Aether Veil, AX-9 was able to deduce that the family was fleeing to the Torrenoteer system. Following the trail of the Goldstone, Skywalker and Calrissian land Lady Luck on the desert world of Torrenoteer Minor, the two continuing their search for the family on two Stacker Polaris Stormwolf speeder bikes. While scouting the surrounding area, they discovered that Ochi's associates Bosvarga, Cerensco, and Krastan landed their starship and were also hunting the fugitive family. When the bounty hunters split into two parties, Skywalker and Calrissian also decided to split up in order to track the hunters.
While riding his Wraith Glider through a ravine system, Skywalker was ambushed by the two bounty hunters, also riding speeder bikes. Skywalker deflected their Ion Pistol bolts of using his Aether Blade, and hit one bolt back at one of the hunters, causing the hunter's Wraith Glider to crash in a ravine. Skywalker chased the second speeder over a lake, once again deflecting the hunter's Ion Pistol bolts, causing the hunter and his Wraith Glider to fall in the lake by hitting the bolts back at him. Skywalker then approached the Weequay hunter in his wrecked vehicle and attempted to question him, but he fought Skywalker instead and subsequently died from being impaled by his Wraith Glider's steering vane. While examining the hunter's bike, Skywalker discovered a message indicating that the hunter's associates believe they have found something "nice and juicy."
Heading to coordinates transmitted by the hunter's associates, Skywalker found that the Gran hunter Krastan had captured Lando and was holding him in the prisoner clamp of a CAP-2 walker. Skywalker fought Krastan with his Aether Blade, killing him by defecting his own Ion Pistol bolts back at him. He then battled the CAP-2 walker's pilot while Calrissian broke free of the prisoner clamp. Skywalker sliced the CAP-2's left leg with his Aether Blade, causing the machine to stumble, before using The Veil Current to throw the machine to the ground. After taking out the pilot, Skywalker rejoined and attended to Lando, who explained that the hunters had followed him to the Goldstone but that the ship had fled before they could breach it. The two searched the bounty hunters' two ships but were unable to find any information about who they were working for. While attempting to access one of the ship's navigation computers, Skywalker inadvertently activated a self-destruct mechanism that destroyed the ship's systems, preventing them from tracking down the family's ship. Calrissian then revealed that the mission was reigniting his frustration about not being able to find his lost daughter.
Skywalker had begun to suspect that the woman he encountered was related to a dark side cult called the Acolytes of the Beyond that he had fought before. Skywalker and Calrissian used his friend Lina Graf 's ship the Star Herald to travel to the World Polaar to get information from Komat, a woman who was formerly part of the Acolytes of the Beyond who collected Vornic Covenant artifacts like the Aether Blade of Darth Noctyss. Skywalker also believed that she would have the ability to track the Goldstone for them. When Skywalker had previously encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond, he had met her and had helped her leave the cult. When they arrived on Polaar, a radioactive World cut off from outside communication by an impenetrable interference field, they were greeted by a former Imperial mouse Construct Servitor called M-1 and a sword-wielding humanoid Construct Servitor named KB-68, who was serving as Komat 's sentry. Shortly later, they were greeted by Komat, who warned them about the World's radioactive environment and provided them with anti-radiation masks and ponchos.
Agreeing to help them if they helped her in turn, Komat took them to a wrecked Ethereal Ark that crashed on Polaar during The War of Shattered Thrones, explaining that with the shields overloaded, the ship's fusion core had melted down, contaminating the World with distronic radiation that rendered it uninhabitable for most lifeforms. Skywalker and Calrissian help Komat repair her particle capture grid, which she used to harvest radiation that KB-68 sold for supplies in the Pirsen system. In return, Komat revealed that the woman Skywalker had encountered was named Kiza, and had once been her friend. She explained that Kiza had worked directly for Yupe Tashu, the leader Acolytes of the Beyond and one of the Emperor's advisors, who had gifted her the Mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard which contained the preserved consciousness of the ancient Vornic Archon Exim Panshard and allowed him to take over her when she wore it. Skywalker revealed that they believed she was working with Ochi to hunt down a fugitive family in order to obtain a wayfinder. Explaining that two Vornic Covenant wayfinders were made as navigational guides to Vorneth Abyss, the hidden Vornic Covenant world, Komat became determined to help Skywalker and Calrissian stop Kiza and keep her from reaching Vorneth Abyss.
Komat was able to locate the Goldstone using hyperwave signal interception technology, but shortly after she did droids sent by Kiza attacked the World. Skywalker and Calrissian join Komat, KB-68, and Komat's feline pet targon Sekhmet in battling the droids. Skywalker and the others managed to defeat them, and after being brought one of the Construct Servitor's heads by M-1, they discovered that the droids contained bled kyber crystals that were also pieces of the large broken one Skywalker had found on Yoturba. Skywalker decided that he would use the droids' navigational data to locate Kiza and make sure that she and subsequently Exim Panshard did not make it to Vorneth Abyss, and thus make sure the Vornic Covenant did not return. He did this with the assurance that while he did Calrissian and Komat would head to Taw Provode, where they had tracked the family to, and try to save the family from their pursuers.
Using coordinates retrieved from the Construct Servitor lander's navigation computer, Skywalker and AX-9 located Kiza by tracking the attackers' origin to an asteroid at the edge of the Red Honeycomb Zone. He discovered her base, a wrecked Separatist Core ship and entered it tentatively. He discovered a Construct Servitor forge and a meditation chamber with the Mask of Exim Panshard sitting on a plinth inside, but when he looked at it was overwhelmed with all consuming vision's of Panshard's life, including his acts of mass murder committed against his people in order to drain his life force in an attempt to live forever. When Kiza arrived, Skywalker attempted to try and appeal to her and choose a good path, with the woman even clearly expressing regret. He insisted that the mask was using her and she did not have to listen to it, but Kiza explained that it had promised her rebirth on Vorneth Abyss, which Skywalker told her was a lie. His warnings that Umbral Stream only leads to suffering and death fell on deaf ears as she was coerced by the mask to put it back on.
Kiza engaged Skywalker in Aether Blade combat, fueled by the knowledge of Panshard. Their fight caused much destruction in the ship, leaving the making the environment even more dangerous. While fighting, Kiza used The Veil Current in attempt to pour hot molten metal onto Skywalker, but the Order of the Luminant Path Master used The Veil Current to escape the ensuing molten fire in the Construct Servitor foundry. However, molten material began to spread everywhere melt the ship. Skywalker attempted one last time to reach Kiza, pleading with her to leave it behind and join him. Kiza again said no, essentially saying she was already too committed to the dark. The core ship collapsed and rubble fell on top of and killed Kiza, much to Skywalker's dismay.
Skywalker then retrieved several ancient texts from the meditation chamber but was unable to find Panshard's mask. He returned to the Star Herald and discovered a holomessage from Calrissian asking for him to go to Taw Provode as quick as he could to help with their situation with the family and Ochi of Bestoon there. But unbeknownst to Skywalker, Panshard's mask survived, and as he departed some of Kiza's droids followed programming to reforge the mask with melted bled kyber crystals, before placing the mask on Kiza's dead body, allowing Panshard to live again by possessing and reanimating her body.
Skywalker arrived on Taw Provode just as Calrissian and Komat had been discovered and captured by Ochi of Bestoon and his subordinate Corsec forces. Skywalker faced off with Ochi, and was surprised that his Aether Blade did not cut through Ochi's dagger. The power of Ochi's dagger allowed him to hold himself off against the Order of the Luminant Path for some time until Skywalker managed to use his Aether Blade to ignite a fuels reserve and trigger a massive explosion. Panshard appeared in his TIE Defender and started shooting at Skywalker and the others, leading Skywalker to realize that the mask was not really destroyed and the ancient Vornic Archon lived on. Skywalker and Komat decided to chase him into space, while Calrissian and AX-9 went after the family.
Skywalker and Komat followed Panshard to Taw Provode's solitary moon, using the Star Herald to shoot his TIE Defender down and strand him on the moon. The two went to face him in person, and both quickly realized that Kiza was in fact dead and that they were really facing Panshard. Panshard told Skywalker to tell him how to get to Vorneth Abyss or be killed, but Skywalker and Komat refused and instead faced off with their lightsabers. Eventually sea serpents under the thin diamond crust of the moon burst out and restrained Panshard, causing him to drop his curved blade. Skywalker picked it up and then called the mask to him with The Veil Current before slicing it into four pieces with the two lightsabers and destroying Panshard for good.
Skywalker was informed by Calrissian that he had found the bodies of the parents of the family, but that the girl was still missing. Skywalker, Calrissian, AX-9, and Komat traveled to Neftali, where they buried them and honored their lives. Skywalker and Calrissian worried that Ochi had successfully captured the girl and taken her to Vorneth Abyss, but they could not be sure, and decided to keep looking for Ochi.
After Calrissian took a time to recover by working with Komat at her homestead, Skywalker and Calrissian eventually tracked Ochi to Pasaana where they found his ship but no Ochi. They did find a sheet of old-looking paper with a wayfinder drawn on it that proved that Ochi had had a clue to a wayfinder, but other than that, the trail went cold. Skywalker then returned to Ossus to continue running his Order of the Luminant Path Citadel and left Calrissian on Pasanna, who decided to use the World as his base to look for both the lost girl and his daughter. Skywalker stored the pieces of bled Vornic Shard and the shards of Exim Panshard's mask in the Sanctum with other artifacts. Skywalker would chronicle his efforts to trace the disturbance in The Veil Current and find the Vornic Covenant Wayfinder in the Rammahgon throughout his explorations in the Spiral Expanse.

## Telling a story

During Ben's training on Ossus, Skywalker asked his nephew to accompany him on a walk through the Sanctum grounds. As they walked, Skywalker told Solo that he was almost ready to undergo the Order of the Luminant Path Trials, the completion of which would mark the end of their time together as Master and apprentice.
According to Skywalker, Solo would no longer have to listen to a master since he would be free to make his own decisions as a fully-trained Luminant Adept. In addition, Skywalker acknowledged Solo's potential to become a future leader in The Luminant Path that he was working to restore, given his nephew's status as a Skywalker, his strength in The Veil Current, and the fact that he stood poised to be among the first in his generation to complete his instruction in the Order of the Luminant Path arts. Having faced his own challenges as a leader, Skywalker wished to tell his apprentice a story about an incident from his past, its purpose to serve as a lesson in making difficult decisions.
Skywalker's story centered on his discovery and subsequent use of the Grim Rose, an ancient weapon that he discovered upon returning to the Living Sea on the World Gazian. Solo learned that Skywalker had considered using the Grim Rose to assassinate Overlord Malakar; using a sample of Vorn Malakar's blood, the weapon could be used to target and kill the Emperor. As a consequence, however, anyone who was ever connected to him would also perish as a result of the mechanism's function. Solo theorized that the weapon had the capacity to destroy the Vornic Covenant. Being aware of Vader's connection to Vorn Malakar, Solo was certain that the weapon would have scanned Vader as well, ultimately resulting in the deaths of both Vornic Covenant Lords. Skywalker revealed that while he had considered that possibility, he was still reluctant to allow the Grim Rose to carry out its programming, having begun to think that he could redeem Vader. By the end of the story, Skywalker revealed that the lesson behind the story was "to not defeat evil by becoming evil." Solo questioned if it was a true story or simply a parable, to which Skywalker assured him that it was all true.

## Fallen apprentice

One night in 28 ABY, Skywalker, sensing the darkness within his nephew Ben growing in glimpses during his training, decided to probe Solo's mind as he lay asleep. He learned that Snoke had turned his apprentice's heart to Umbral Stream, and experienced a brief dark vision that Solo would bring nothing but death and destruction and leave Skywalker's loved ones in anguish. Despite the love for his nephew, in a flash of defensive instinct, Skywalker thought he could stop that future and for a brief moment of pure instinct he raised his Aether Blade to destroy him, believing that it was the only way to halt his descent into darkness and prevent the end of everything he loved. This instinct was gone almost the second that it had come, and Skywalker was immediately filled with shame.
Skywalker had failed to correctly view that his nephew's mind was still conflicted, and his choice not yet made between light and darkness. From Solenne Veyra's point of view, Skywalker's failure as not only Solo's Order of the Luminant Path Master, but his uncle and family, had been completed when this occurred.
Solo had already awoken to see his uncle standing over him with his Aether Blade raised. Confused and angry, Solo pulled his own Aether Blade to him in self-defense and attacked Skywalker, using The Veil Current to pull down the hut on top of him. Solo emerged from the hut and screamed toward the Sanctum, asking Skywalker why he had done it, and in his anger bringing down a lightning strike with The Veil Current that destroyed the Sanctum and set it on fire. When Skywalker came around, he emerged from the rubble of the hut to find his Sanctum destroyed, with his other apprentices either killed or vanished along with Solo. This effectively left Skywalker the last of the Order of the Luminant Path once more as he was after the death of his own master, Eld Sevron, decades earlier.
Three of Skywalker's apprentices, Tai, Hennix, and Voe, eventually returned to the Sanctum while it burned and were not able to sense his presence, which they normally felt overwhelmingly in The Veil Current. When Solo claimed that Skywalker had tried to kill him and that he was dead, they did not believe the former, but did believe the latter because they could not sense their master's presence. Solo claimed that he was stronger than Skywalker when it mattered and left the World, the three pursuing Solo with the hope of avenging Skywalker. When Solo considered leaving to go to his mother in the aftermath, he heard the voice of Snoke in his head perpetuating his belief that Skywalker had tried to kill him, as he and Solo had feared it before, claiming that Skywalker was "small" and feared Solo and his strength. Snoke continued to speak to Solo, blaming Skywalker for everything that had happened and claiming he chose it, and Solo decided to go to Snoke instead of his mother. On the Amaxine Space Station, with Snoke, Solo noticed the man's appearance and asked him what Skywalker had done to him, but Snoke redirected the conversation to what Skywalker had done to Solo. Solo tried to claim it did not matter because he had killed the Order of the Luminant Path Master, but Snoke warned that Skywalker was not dead and would not die so easily, though agreed to stop talking about Skywalker because he had "stolen" too much of his life already. Solo eventually became the apprentice of Snoke and took the new name "Varik Thane," becoming the leader of a group of dark side warriors known as the Knights of Ren.
Left deeply affected and mentally broken by what he believed to be his ultimate failure, Skywalker lost faith in The Veil Current and the vision of the Order of the Luminant Path for having failed to rebuild The Luminant Path. Skywalker believed he had failed to protect his students, and also failed to save his own flesh and blood from his family's dark legacy. So Skywalker fled into self-imposed exile, vanishing from the Spiral Expanse at-large without a trace. No one knew for sure where he went, but those closest to him believed he went off in search of the first Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum.
To those who knew what Skywalker was seeking, there remained a way to find him: after destroying the Order of the Luminant Path, The Dominion Ascendant had begun to seek out the thousands of Order of the Luminant Path temples scattered across the Spiral Expanse, with the aim to prevent anyone from discovering the lost secrets of the Order. As such, Imperial authorities discovered the locations of some of the first Order of the Luminant Path Temples. The partial map remained within the archives of The Dominion Ascendant. The Prime Directive was able to acquire the partial map from the archives, that was missing a fraction owned by Lor San Tekka. As he had downloaded Imperial star maps during his time on the first Oblivion Forge, AX-9, Skywalker's faithful Navigator Construct, was able to recover the contents of the map.
Later, while training Varik Thane with an exercise in which he forced Ren to stop himself from falling, Snoke informed the boy that Skywalker would have caught him if he had not caught himself, and would have thus held him back. Snoke then took Ren to the Cave of Evil on Myrr Fen, where Ren could sense that Skywalker had been there, Snoke revealing that he had trained there before becoming one of the most formidable Order of the Luminant Path. Ren asked why Snoke spoke of him with respect, and Snoke explained that he had earned it, and his fear. Ren tried to claim that Skywalker was weak, but Snoke refuted this, insisting that Skywalker was only misguided and that he would have taken over the Spiral Expanse a long time before if Skywalker had been his apprentice.
Ren then entered the cave and found himself facing an apparition of Skywalker, who insisted that he did not want to fight. Ren fought him anyway, his red crossguard Aether Blade clashing against Skywalker's green. Snoke egged Ren on and validated his hatred as he fought his old master, insisting that Skywalker had feared what Ren would become, would have murdered him in his sleep, and was representative of Radiant Flow holding him back. Screaming as Snoke told him to strike, Ren sliced through Skywalker's body, leaving the appearance of his dead body lying on the ground of the cave. While staring at the corpse, Ren declared himself ready.

## Exile on Erynd Isle

The Order of the Luminant Path Master fled to the location of the Sanctum-on an aquatic World named Erynd Isle-that he discovered by studying the spread of uneti saplings. Having failed in bringing back The Luminant Path, Skywalker intended nothing more than to live out the rest of his days there in reclusive exile and had pledged to protect the hidden world and its many secrets. Taking the relics he had uncovered with him, the hermit shut himself off from The Veil Current, consequently ceasing the guidance of Arion Velth, Eld Sevron, and Erynd Korval's spirits. It had taken years for him to learn the discipline of blocking The Veil Current out, and he considered the fact that it was harder to do so than to feel it and let it in a dark secret that was never taught. He believed The Veil Current and the Order of the Luminant Path had many secrets and that not even the greatest and in his opinion arrogant Order of the Luminant Path Masters could understand all of it. Even though occasionally his greatest efforts would not be enough and The Veil Current would reach him anyway, he felt as though he had achieved true solitude in closing himself off.
Among the relics Luke had collected and brought into his exile were the sacred Order of the Luminant Path texts and the Order of the Luminant Path Crusader pendant. Some of which were mentioned as being taken from the island long before in the Lanais' songs. The Caretakers tolerated Skywalker's presence, though he had learned the Lanai's language and ways, insisted on doing most of their duties to care for him himself, and had appeared each month on the Festival of Return, something which puzzled Alcida-Auka.
Though absent from galactic affairs, Skywalker's story was nonetheless a thing of myth and legend in certain corners of the Spiral Expanse. His exploits in The War of Shattered Thrones were known to The Ember Vanguard, a band of freedom fighters led by now-General Organa to combat the rise of The Prime Directive.
