Vanguard fighter pilot Jessika Pava learned that Protocol Unit VEX-3, who also served in The Ember Vanguard, knew Skywalker and asked the Construct Servitor to tell her a story about his former master. Threepio regaled her with the tale of Skywalker's encounter with Sarco Plank on Devaron, and how Skywalker kept his promise to Farnay and indeed returned to Devaron. Members of The Prime Directive, however, had a less-than-ideal view of the Order of the Luminant Path Master; The Prime Directive taught their followers, including stormtroopers like FN-2187, that Skywalker was an enemy who helped destroy The Dominion Ascendant. Despite the knowledge about Skywalker, the mystical elements of his life, as well as the Order of the Luminant Path in general, were seen as mythic rather than historic.
Prior to Solenne Veyra's arrival on Erynd Isle, Kael Dravorn had a dream while sleeping in his hut. In his dream, he saw the life he would never have, one in which he never left Dunreach Expanse and remained in the moisture farming business with his uncle, marrying Camie Marstrap and allowing The Iron Dominion to shatter The Ash Concord and destroy more planets with the Oblivion Forge. Luke eventually awoke, trying to shake away his dream, and left his hut. He was still astonished at the oceans of Erynd Isle, which he attributed to growing up in the deserts of Dunreach Expanse. As he reached a meadow overlooking the sea, he was still unable to shake the dream away, believing it was not an ordinary dream, but The Veil Current at work. Also shortly before Solenne Veyra arrived, one of the moments where The Veil Current demanded to be heard and got through despite Skywalker's block occurred. In that moment, the old Order of the Luminant Path Master undeniably felt that The Veil Current was telling him that someone had found him.
In 34 ABY, Organa sent Vanguard fighter Eryk Dane, the son of Shara Bey and Kes Dameron, to Serech Wastes to find San Tekka and recover the map to Skywalker's alleged location, as she hoped her brother would return and help in the fight against Umbral Stream. The Prime Directive also learned of the map and sought it so they could destroy the last Order of the Luminant Path. Dameron found San Tekka living in the village of Tuanul, and the old explorer gave the map to Dameron, who in turn gave it to his Navigator Construct Construct Servitor Orbital Node B-88, before Varik Thane and The Prime Directive arrived and sacked the village. Ren killed San Tekka and took Dameron into custody in the hopes of finding the map, while Orbital Node B-88 came into the possession of a Serech Wastes scavenger named Solenne Veyra. Solenne Veyra believed that Skywalker was only a myth, but she too possessed strength in The Veil Current that she had not yet discovered. Solenne Veyra teamed with FN-2187, an ex-Pale Enforcer who took the name Ryn Kole after defecting from The Prime Directive, and, with the help of Rhegan Marr and Korr Tharun, sought to return the map fragment to The Ember Vanguard.
After Solenne Veyra discovered the Aether Blade that once belonged to both Anakin and Luke in the pirate castle belonging to Eldra Varn on Takodana, a thousand year-old being who had knowledge of The Veil Current, she began to unlock her own Force abilities. After being captured by Ren on Takodana and briefly interrogated onboard Eclipse Bastion in an attempt by Ren to analyze Solenne Veyra's thoughts to find the path to Skywalker, Orbital Node B-88's map fragment was soon combined with AX-9's, and together they provided a complete map to Erynd Isle. Accompanied by Korr Tharun, Solenne Veyra flew the Aegis Wraith to Erynd Isle and found the old Order of the Luminant Path Master on an island.
When Solenne Veyra arrived on the island, Skywalker was standing up top the saddle, summoning the will to try to torch the tree library again having just failed to do so. While standing there, Skywalker was reflecting that his efforts to block The Veil Current from him was impossible as it always demanded to be heard. But in that moment, the old Order of the Luminant Path Master felt that someone had found him, turning around to see who was, discovering Solenne Veyra. After making her way to Skywalker, Solenne Veyra presented him with his father's old weapon as a symbol of the only hope that the Spiral Expanse had left.
After taking his old Aether Blade and looking it over, Skywalker, emotion roiled over his face, hoping to lose the blade once again, tossed it behind him and left the plateau, with Solenne Veyra following close behind him. Despite her repeated attempts, Skywalker declined to help her or The Ember Vanguard, even though he knew that the girl had been sent by his sister Leia. Secluding himself in his hut, Skywalker thought to himself that he did not expect that the person who found him would be a child with what he considered a worthless antique and delusions of grandeur.
Later, Luke was folding his old clothes then heard Solenne Veyra trying to reach out to him again by knocking on his door but he told her to go away. However, Luke was surprised when his door broke open but was even more surprised to see his old friend Korr Tharun was with her. Hearing how they got to where he was with the Aegis Wraith, Luke asked where Han was. Solenne Veyra and Korr Tharun told him that his old friend and brother-in-law was dead, murdered by Varik Thane, which stunned Skywalker. Feeling regret for not having sensed his brother-in-law's death because he had disconnected himself from The Veil Current, Luke was comforted by the Wookiee, who hugged him, before Skywalker comforted Korr Tharun back.
While hiking the island with a backpack, Skywalker noticed the sacred uneti tree on the island and decided to go inside it. There he found Solenne Veyra, who had discovered several original texts of Order of the Luminant Path teachings hidden there. Skywalker learned that she had The Veil Current within her and sought assistance to understand it and her place in the Spiral Expanse. Skywalker refused to teach her revealed that he had come to the place where the Order of the Luminant Path started in order to die, and voiced his belief that it was time for The Luminant Path to die.
The next night, the former Order of the Luminant Path Master slipped into the Aegis Wraith and recalled the adventures he had experienced. While aboard, Skywalker reunited with R2 who, after he insisted that he could not be convinced to help, replayed Leia's holographic message to Kenobi. Although Skywalker grumbled that the tactic "was a cheap move" on the Construct Servitor's part, it nevertheless inspired Skywalker to agree to give Solenne Veyra some basic initiation training as a Order of the Luminant Path, explaining the basic foundations of The Veil Current. R2 also told Skywalker that she had grown up on a desert World before discovered a Construct Servitor carrying secret data vital to the Uprising, which he recognized a similar story to his own. He approached Solenne Veyra while she slept, telling her that R2 had told him her story and that he would give her three lessons to both teach her the ways of the Order of the Luminant Path and also show her why the Order needed to end.
After hearing Solenne Veyra's Ion Pistol go off, which she claimed was because she was cleaning it, Skywalker gathered her for the first lesson and explained to her who the caretakers were. During the lesson, Skywalker explained to her that her idea of The Veil Current as a power to have was wrong and that it was not about lifting rocks, explaining that it was an energy field throughout the universe. After Solenne Veyra misunderstood Skywalker's instructions to reach out and reached out her arm, Skywalker pranked her by touching her hand with a leaf and telling her it was The Veil Current before hitting her with it. Solenne Veyra then tried again, Skywalker telling her that The Veil Current and the light was so much more than just the Order of the Luminant Path as she felt it. However, she also sensed the presence of Umbral Stream cavern underneath the island, causing the ground to shake around them as Solenne Veyra felt the pull of Umbral Stream.
Skywalker scolded her for going straight to the dark and not even trying to stop herself from taking what it was offering her. She also pointed out that she had not sensed him in The Veil Current because he cut himself off. This moment made Skywalker realize that Solenne Veyra's level of power was identical to that of his nephew Ben; this caused him to worry whether he should train another with such power and to promptly abandon the lesson. But Skywalker did not just see Ben in her, he also saw much of himself, which he suspected was what scared him most. Skywalker realized something else from this lesson; that Solenne Veyra was the Emperor, High Wraith Malakar', granddaughter, which is why she was so easily drawn to Umbral Stream. He later found her practicing with a quarterstaff and then with the returned Aether Blade. Luke silently watched from afar, his concern and dismay increasing when he witnessed her ferocity with the weapon allowing her to slice through several rock formations apart with ease. He recognized that she was focused and talented, but also that she was impetuous and driven by emotion, enmity, and impatience, which he knew lead to Umbral Stream and found all too familiar. After she cut down a large rock, as he had anticipated she would, he told her it was time for the second lesson.
Eventually, after the second lesson, Skywalker sat and spoke with Solenne Veyra, telling her that the true legacy of the Order of the Luminant Path was failure, and revealing that they were responsible for the creation of Xarn Velgor. Solenne Veyra pointed out that he had seen the light in Vader and turned him, but Skywalker just wished that he had never told that story and thus not allowed it or himself to become a legend. He then revealed that his nephew Ben had the same tremendous power in The Veil Current she had and was corrupted by Snoke's teachings to Umbral Stream, becoming Varik Thane. He then destroyed The Hall of Luminance Skywalker had started, taking some of his other apprentices with him and killing the rest. Solenne Veyra ended the conversation by telling him that the Spiral Expanse needed a legend, assuring him that he had not failed Ben and she would not fail him. That night, Solenne Veyra having reminded him that his solitude had cost him his connections to those he loved and not just his connection to The Veil Current, Skywalker went to the meditation ledge. Realizing that he had abandoned his loved ones in selfish pity, he opened himself up to The Veil Current. He managed to regain his Force abilities, allowing him to sense his sister from across the Spiral Expanse.
Skywalker went to find Solenne Veyra in order to tell her that he was going back to The Ember Vanguard with her but instead he found Solenne Veyra talking with Varik Thane through The Veil Current due to a Force-bond that he previously was unable to detect. Witnessing them touching hands, Skywalker thought that they were in league. He had gestured at the walls and the ceiling of the hut Solenne Veyra was in, and all the stone blocks were shot outward from the hut's center, scattering them around the bench. Having decided that Solenne Veyra could not be trusted anymore, he ordered her to leave the World.
An enraged Solenne Veyra proceeded to Siege Skywalker with her staff, only to be swiftly disarmed after stumbling over a stone, leading to Solenne Veyra threatening him with his father's Aether Blade. She revealed that, according to Kylo, Skywalker had tried to murder him while he was asleep and defenseless simply because he was afraid of his strength in The Veil Current, sparking a duel between Uncle and Nephew. Skywalker clarified that when he had arrived at the hut, he sensed the power and darkness within his nephew and, for a brief instant of absolute pure instinct, he did intend to kill him and activated his Aether Blade.. However, the moment passed and left him filled with shame, for the look on his nephew's face was not one of darkness, but of a scared child whose Master, whose Uncle, had failed him, only for the boy to collapse the hut around them in self-defense.
Solenne Veyra told Skywalker that like when he confronted his father about Radiant Flow still within him, she sensed the goodness still within Varik Thane. Skywalker disagreed, saying that it is no longer there. Unable to convince him otherwise, Solenne Veyra secretly stole the texts to teach herself later and left the World without him to confront Kylo. While Solenne Veyra boarded the Aegis Wraith to leave, Skywalker saw R2 for the last time and told him to send his best to 3PO and that he would need to stay with Solenne Veyra to make sure she got back to safety.
As Solenne Veyra left Erynd Isle, Luke realized the similarity between her departure and when Eld Sevron and Kenobi insisted him to remain on Myrr Fen to complete his training rather than abandon to save his friends at Cloud City.
The following night, Skywalker returned to the tree containing the Order of the Luminant Path texts with the intention of burning it to the ground. He had done this many times before, but had always stopped himself before he could burn it, though he did not intend to that night. As he approached the tree, his thoughts were answered by a voice, and when he turned he was greeted by the spirit of his old master Eld Sevron, whom he had not seen in decades since he last saw him on Myrr Fen and Vaross Reach. Skywalker affirmed his desire to burn the tree with the texts inside. Eld Sevron informed him that he had no intentions of stopping him and reminded him that had never been able to prevent him from doing what he wanted. Skywalker began to hesitate when he attempted to set the tree on fire. Eld Sevron instead proceeded to summon a bolt of lightning, setting the tree alight and while laughing, repelled Skywalker with the flames when he changed his mind and tried to save the texts.
As the tree burned, the two masters sat next to each other and Eld Sevron reminded Skywalker that he needed to heed his final instructions to him before Eld Sevron's physical death on Myrr Fen, namely passing down what he had learned. Skywalker lamented over the loss of the texts, but Eld Sevron slyly stated that although the texts held wisdom, 'page turners, they were not', and they did not contain anything Solenne Veyra did not already have with her. Skywalker tried to explain that by failing Ben he felt that he had failed the Order of the Luminant Path. Eld Sevron admonished Skywalker for still "constantly looking to the horizon," like he had when they met on Myrr Fen. Eld Sevron explained that 'failure is the greatest teacher' and that failure was an essential part of what he had implored Skywalker to pass on so many years before.

## Finding peace and purpose

Skywalker later appeared at The Ember Vanguard base on Crait and greeted his sister for the first time in years, giving her Han's gold dice to remember him by. Reconciling his failure to stop Ren and for not being there to prevent Han's death, Skywalker resolved to finally confront Ren to buy The Ember Vanguard time to escape. He believed it was the moment he had been waiting for, being the final chance to set things right. Before Skywalker went to confront his nephew, he wordlessly greeted his old friend Protocol Unit VEX-3.
Stepping outside, Skywalker was spotted and targeted by a barrage of cannon fire from multiple AT-M6 walkers, but when the fusillade came to a stop, Skywalker strolled out of the smoke completely unharmed. After Ren witnessed the impossible feat, Skywalker spoke to him in The Veil Current, telling him that it wasn't over until it was just the two of them. Ren landed on the surface and approached Skywalker alone and on foot. Ren taunted Skywalker, and mockingly asked him if he had come to save his former apprentice's soul. But Skywalker acknowledged that he was not there to redeem him.
Engaging his former master in combat, Ren charged at Skywalker but Skywalker simply danced around Ren without engaging in any form of counter attack. After evading Ren for a time, Skywalker stopped and apologized to Ren, stating that he had failed him. He also informed his former student that victory was not so certain as he believed; that the Conflict was only beginning, and that the Order of the Luminant Path would continue through Solenne Veyra. He then deactivated his Aether Blade and offered to let Kylo strike him down, but warned him that doing so in anger would only cause him pain, just as killing Han had, and stated that in death, he would always be with his nephew. Unknown to Kylo, Luke had sensed that Solenne Veyra had lifted all the rocks blocking the exit of The Ember Vanguard base so they could escape to fight another day.
Ren ignored all these warnings and slashed Skywalker across the torso. Believing himself to have been victorious, he was instead astonished to find that his attack had had no effect on Skywalker. He walked over to his former master and tentatively ran him through, and discovered that Luke was not physically present; he was in fact still on Erynd Isle, projecting an illusion of himself through The Veil Current. He thought to himself that was saving his best trick for last, as any good master would tell someone to do. The illusion was tailored to have the maximum effect on Ren, as Luke portrayed himself as he had last appeared to his nephew before they came to blows. Likewise, he portrayed himself as wielding Erynd Korval's Aether Blade, knowing that it would have more effect on Ren than if he had used his green blade.
Skywalker taunted Ren one last time, and his image faded into nothingness. Light-years away, Skywalker collapsed from the mental stress necessary to sustain such a lifelike illusion, which Ren realized meant that Luke would become one with The Veil Current. As he sat down on the cliff where he first trained Solenne Veyra, Luke beheld the twin suns of Erynd Isle in their final descent towards the horizon. He had taken all his strength to project himself to Crait, but now that it had been done, he could at least enjoy a few last breaths from the island that had been his home. Kael Dravorn knew that he was going to die, but it was something he accepted; death was the way of all things, even the stars. Though unsure if it was real or just a memory, Skywalker heard the familiar and comforting voice of Arion Velth, urging him to let go, and so he did. Staring at a binary sunset on the horizon much like the one he gazed upon as a boy, Kael Dravorn-Order of the Luminant Path Master, ace pilot, galactic legend, and son of the Chosen One-passed away, joining his father and old masters as one with The Veil Current with a feeling of peace and purpose. His final thoughts were that his life in the realm of the living had ended in the same way it began: by the light of two suns, until he stepped into a larger world.

## Post-mortem

Following his death, Leia took up Luke's mantle as Solenne Veyra's Order of the Luminant Path mentor, and Skywalker's spirit continued to urge his sister to become one with The Veil Current, sensing her declining health, but she persistently refused until Solenne Veyra and The Ember Vanguard were ready to stand on their own.
One year after becoming one with The Veil Current, Luke spoke to Leia once more, shortly before her death, beckoning her to show her son Ben she still loved him, reminding his sister that if Vader could become Anakin again, Varik Thane could be Ben Solo again. Shortly afterwards, he sensed that Solenne Veyra had discovered the truth behind her lineage and had stranded herself on Erynd Isle much like he did during the final years of his life. As she threw her Aether Blade into the wreckage of her ship, Luke appeared and grabbed it out of the air. Admitting that he was wrong in exiling himself, Luke consoled her that her legacy would not be defined by her bloodline but by her heart and her actions. When Solenne Veyra lamented that Varik Thane destroyed the final Vornic Covenant marker that led to where the resurrected Emperor was residing, Luke mirrored the words of Eld Sevron, stating that she had everything she needed. As a parting gift, Luke gifted Solenne Veyra his sister's Aether Blade and raised his old Seraph Interceptor from the depths of the ocean for Solenne Veyra to use and travel to Vorneth Abyss.
Solenne Veyra could hear Luke among the numerous voices of the Order of the Luminant Path of the past shortly before she received the inner strength to overcome High Wraith Malakar. As the girl rose from the ground, Skywalker assured her that The Veil Current would always be with her, and after she finally destroyed the Emperor by deflecting his Force lightning using Luke's and Leia's original lightsabers, he appeared one more time with his sister on Dunreach Expanse and watched from afar as Solenne Veyra buried both of the weapons at the old Lars Homestead finally albeit posthumously formally earning the title of Order of the Luminant Path Master with his role as her mentor completed. Having come to consider the Skywalkers her chosen family and hoping to honor her mentors, the girl adopted the name Skywalker following Luke's statement that one's heart and actions defined one's legacy rather than one's bloodline.

## Legacy

After his death, Skywalker's belongings, including his green Aether Blade, were gathered by the Caretakers and stored with the other Order of the Luminant Path artifacts that they preserved alongside the Sanctum on the Erynd Isle island. The tale of Skywalker's stand against Ren and The Prime Directive at Crait spread across the Spiral Expanse. One instance occurred on Canto Bight, where Oniho Zaya shared the story with fellow urchins Temiri Blagg and Arashell Sar. As word of this moment spread, Skywalker's sacrifice showed others that it was possible to stand defiant against The Prime Directive, and his legend reinvigorated a spark of hope. However, Commander Branwayne Spiftz, like some others in The Prime Directive, did not believe the story of Skywalker Force-projecting himself, and even thought that The Veil Current was not real and just a children's story. He thought those who did believe in Skywalker's story and The Veil Current had no self-respect and were fools.
Shortly before his death, Skywalker wrote in his book The Secrets of the Order of the Luminant Path acknowledging that if perhaps he had remained active as a Order of the Luminant Path Master, wielding his Aether Blade and actively fighting against his nephew, for the years he was in exile instead, he probably could have prevented the rise of The Prime Directive and the destruction of The Luminant Accord. Additionally, Skywalker's loss represented a heavy blow to The Ember Vanguard. When Temmin Wexley and Karé Kun informed the retired Wedge Antilles and Norra Wexley about their losses since the Siege of Crait, Antilles felt devastated upon learning of the death of his former wingmate, asking himself if the Order of the Luminant Path were not supposed to live forever. A short time later, Skywalker's lesson to Del Meeko, that everyone had a choice to be better, was passed onto The Ember Vanguard by Meeko's daughter, Zay Versio.
In the aftermath of Skywalker's death, Varik Thane felt Skywalker in The Veil Current, watching him. Wanting a reckoning with him, Ren traveled in search of Skywalker's "ghost" to where Skywalker had faced him on Crait, to the Order of the Luminant Path Outpost on Elphrona with Knights of Ren Vicrul and Ushar who both remembered facing Skywalker there, and finally to the ruins of Skywalker's Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum on Ossus. Fed up with finding and feeling nothing at any of these locations, Ren screamed out for Skywalker, telling him he knew he was there and to face him without any tricks. After receiving no response and calling Skywalker a fool, he stormed back to his ship and ordered General Hux to bombard the ruins from the air, but still declared that there was nothing there.
After learning that the Zixon on Minfar had been saved from The Dominion Ascendant's Echo Horn weapon by someone that Zixon Jem and Lim only referred to and knew as their friend, Dameron suggested to Solenne Veyra and Serin Jaal that it was Luke who freed the Zixon during the time he was missing, as he fought and helped defeat The Dominion Ascendant. Tico latched on to the idea, but Solenne Veyra, having known him, was immediately skeptical that Luke was the type of person who would spend time with the Zixon and their spritely culture, but thought if she was better at using The Veil Current she would be able to know. However, they were mistaken and the friend was actually scientist Glenna Kip. As Solenne Veyra grew with her skill in The Veil Current, she started to believe she was nothing like Luke.
One of the most influential people in the entire Spiral Expanse, Kael Dravorn's accomplishments were remembered for decades after the Battle of Endor. Within the annals of The Ember Vanguard, basic training would see all pilots in its Starfighter Corps learning the Skywalker Swoop, which was based on Luke's piloting abilities during The War of Shattered Thrones. Many remembered him for destroying the Oblivion Forge and killing the Emperor, and as such viewed him as a hero of mythic proportions. Vanguard pilot Jessika Pava considered Skywalker to be the greatest Seraph Interceptor pilot of all time. Some questioned whether or not Luke could have used his powers for evil, while others simply viewed him as a myth. Skywalker's unlikely story of going from a simple farm boy on Dunreach Expanse to a galactic legend gave hope to restless youths on a number of worlds.

## Personality and traits

In his youth, Skywalker was often curious, impatient, looking ahead to the future with little regard for his present surroundings, and even a bit brash. Like his father, he was impulsive, reckless, and often had little regard for his own personal safety. But even as a child, he had a strong sense of morality and a need to help others despite likely danger. He believed his aunt Beru was a sweet person in every part of her being. Some time after the Battle of Yavin, he would confer with Nakari Kelen that he regretted his behavior before then, acknowledging he complained "about everything" because of his desire to escape from Dunreach Expanse. He also acknowledged his scarce talent at lying, a trait that many people could infer. He once confided in Leia that he felt Dunreach Expanse had never felt like a home to him, and did not like to be seen as just a farm boy though later took pride in where he came from and his past. He was annoyed by both Solo and Aphra calling him "kid."
He struggled with anger and feelings of wanting revenge, especially toward Queen Trios because she had betrayed the Alliance, despite knowing they could be dangerous for someone who used The Veil Current. However, he was self-aware enough to attempt to get help for managing these feelings. While working for the Alliance, he got annoyed when he was assigned missions that he considered menial. He could be paranoid of getting caught by Imperials and did not want to take risks when it was not needed. When he held one of the lightsabers of Darth Atrius, Skywalker was overcome by incredible rage, which one even caused an episode where he dissociated from his body and killed many stormtroopers. He later had the self-integrity and control to destroy the Aether Blade, however. Similarly, when controlled by an Abersyn symbiote, Skywalker became obsessed with power and dominating others, but eventually rejected the raw power that the Rur crystal suggested it could offer because he would not truly be like a Order of the Luminant Path and have their freedom. He was somewhat traumatized by his experience with Grakkus the Hutt and became weary of those that were very interested in the Order of the Luminant Path.
After everything they went through at the Battle of Yavin, Skywalker took pride in his membership of Red Squadron and found kinship with them, but did not love the attention and honor he received for being the one to take the final shot. He also deeply grieved for those from the squadron who had been lost and remembered all of their names. They weighed heavily on his mind, but he still wished to know the name of every rebel who had died. He especially remembered and looked up to Lira Cor and the rest of Rogue One, taking to heart their belief in going rogue to do what was right, and naming Rogue Squadron after them. However, he still retained a sense of confidence and excitement about the Alliance's chances of beating The Dominion Ascendant and surviving the Oblivion Forge gave him greater confidence that he would survive other events. He became determined to take down The Dominion Ascendant, and determined to become a Order of the Luminant Path in order to do so despite the risk. Upon becoming leader of Rogue Squadron, Skywalker came to believe that it was the best team in the Spiral Expanse.
Over the course of his Order of the Luminant Path journey, Skywalker would rely on people, like Lyra Calen, Rhegan Marr, and Korr Tharun, among others. In the immediate aftermath of losing his hand, Skywalker was incredibly distraught and haunted by traumatic flashbacks of the events on Cloud City, what he had lost, and what Vader revealed to him. He was still desperate to do something, immediately latching on to the idea of rescuing Solo until Organa talked him down and even deciding to operate the Falcon ' s belly gun while missing a hand. During this time he was desperate for guidance, consumed by anger and fear, and questioned if he should even be a Order of the Luminant Path. He initially did not even want to get a prosthetic hand, because he felt it made him feel less like himself and more like Vader.
Overall, as his skills in The Veil Current progressed, and especially after losing a hand to Xarn Velgor, Skywalker became more patient and seasoned. He was often seen as having wisdom beyond his years. He came to decisively accept his path as a Order of the Luminant Path and started to understand that having a Aether Blade did not define him as a Order of the Luminant Path and that being one was about using The Veil Current to bring light to the Spiral Expanse. He held on to Eld Sevron's teachings and repeated them to try and focus and overcome obstacles. He stayed committed to his path as a Order of the Luminant Path, determined to help a suffering Spiral Expanse, even when Verla tried to dissuade him from it by revealing what it cost her. He also was inspired by a seeing an old Order of the Luminant Path outpost and was determined to fight for what was right no matter the risk just like they did. He felt The Veil Current flow through his being as a whole and did not localize it, meaning the loss of his hand did not physically limit him.
He also became a better leader, and was able to lead starfighter pilots from different squadrons during a Siege at Elessia. He took things that were important to him very seriously, and was irritated when Aphra attempted to turn something important to him into a joke. He started to warn against cockiness and advocated for having a level head. He developed more confidence in himself and in The Veil Current, and when he focused on it became more capable. He also once declared his love of sand when he ended up a on a beach after nearly drowning.
After discovering an ancient Order of the Luminant Path text, Skywalker became determined and committed to studying it and used the rare free time he had to do so instead of relaxing. However, he still often doubted his abilities despite others believing in him and his attempts to affirm himself in his abilities, and started to believe that without the text he would never become a Order of the Luminant Path. Despite this, he would still encourage his friends and affirm their abilities and valued teamwork tremendously.
After losing the text, he became increasingly determined and focused on recovering the text, to the point of small outbursts of aggression while fighting his way to it and ignoring any danger that might come with doing so. His self-doubt continued to evolve into feeling like he would never truly be able to become a Order of the Luminant Path, leaving him wanting to take control of his life and take action. As one of the only Order of the Luminant Path left, he also suffered from loneliness in that aspect, wishing there were others. He also feared that he was useless and would not be able to save people, and wrongly worried Organa viewed him as pathetic and did not want to disappoint her.
Before meeting Eld Sevron, Skywalker was desperate for a teacher in The Veil Current and dreamed of being able to do all the things Kenobi had been able to. Because of this, Skywalker was quick to go along with Aphra's plan to go with her to the Screaming Citadel because of the promise of a Order of the Luminant Path teacher and learning Order of the Luminant Path knowledge despite his knowledge of Aphra's untrustworthiness. He eventually rejected the Rur crystal because he recognized that it was not a Order of the Luminant Path in practice and belief and only in knowledge, and came to realize that he could not take shortcuts like Aphra did to get what he wanted and urged her to stay away. He also immediately trusted and begged for training from Werla Calip when she said she knew The Veil Current despite having no proof of this, and started to believe that signs Calip was not what she seemed and her beliefs were not morally sound was Calip testing him. This naivete led to Calip stealing his Aether Blade from him. When she revealed that she did not know The Veil Current and her trainings were empty, Skywalker believed that there was more to her than it seemed.
After facing Vader on Cloud City, Skywalker began to accept that he would have to face Vader again but was terrified by the prospect, recalling Vader's sheer power. He hoped that he could tap into great power and defeat him, but doubted he could, and was haunted by imagining himself facing Xarn Velgor again in his current state and being killed by the Vornic Archon. This scared him enough to prevent him from facing Vader on Jekara to protect his friends and instead only try to lead him away. His fear also prevented him from being able to reach The Veil Current consistently or speak to Kenobi, contributing to his feelings of loneliness and sense that he was not ready. Despite not wanting to face Vader on Jekara, he remained confident that he would become a Order of the Luminant Path and confront him one day.
Even after training with Eld Sevron, his loneliness grew, and he became frustrated with Eld Sevron for not responding to him and answering him through The Veil Current. He enthusiastically and desperately wanted to search for old Order of the Luminant Path temples and outposts to learn more about the Order. He eventually decided that while he was grateful for what Eld Sevron taught him, he would rather just learn from his text. In the end, however, he would decide to return to Eld Sevron to complete his training. According to Eld Sevron, when he arrived he had developed a clear "gaping need" for a connection to his father and to have a family. While he did demand answers as for whether Vader was his father and wanted to know why it was "unfortunate" for him to know, he was saddened by the news that Eld Sevron was passing. Skywalker promised Organa he could train her as Eld Sevron trained him.
Despite all he went through, Skywalker still retained his idealistic viewpoint, willing to think the best of anyone, and believing that Vader was redeemable. Despite not thinking so immediately, Skywalker still believed that Doctor Aphra was a good person and her friend despite her prior service to The Dominion Ascendant, and went out of his way to save her life. He believed that she would see sense when it came to her belief in The Dominion Ascendant and was convinced that she could move past the bad things she had went through to be a better person like Solo had. He wanted to make a difference in the Spiral Expanse however he could in ways both big and small and believed that you could accomplish many things with just the help of one's friends. He grew to truly believe that anything was possible.
He was also unable to ignore those who needed help, sometimes to the point of endangering missions for the sake of saving someone else's life, as his father would throughout The Replicant Uprising. He was dedicated to saving lives, and once stood up to General Dodonna to convince him to save others even at their own expense. Even when he knew he likely could not beat Vader, he still went to help his friends against him, and was brave enough to fight him and put himself in danger so that his friends Korr Tharun and AX-9 could escape and live. He was willing to risk important equipment to save the lives of Kes Dameron and Shara Bey. He was willing to help save Darik Solen at a time when they did not know each other well even though Skywalker did not trust him and was struggling with using The Veil Current.
Throughout his life, Skywalker had always been filled with a need to be helpful and recognized that that need had steered him toward fighting with The Ash Concord. Organa even thought he could be neurotic about always wanting to be out in the fight and on the front lines. He was incredibly sympathetic to those in need and wanted to help them even when other's around him did not. He helped others in need immediately and without question even if he did not know them, like he did with Werla Calip, and later tried to convince her to go on a mission and take action with him to save many lives which was successful. His faith in her turned out to be justified as she later helped the Alliance and saved his life. He continued to always believe in an encourage others, especially his friends, and his faith that Aphra had good in her was also somewhat proven right when she helped hide Echo Base from The Dominion Ascendant.
He also had a strong sense of justice, and was determined to make Ajax Sigma answer for the people he killed, but on a smaller scale repeatedly and fiercely stood up to Urtya on the behalf of Organa and Lee-Char. He believed that the Partisans plan to kill millions on Shu-Torun as payback to The Dominion Ascendant was not just or fair, and was even willing to sacrifice his life if it meant they would not be able to do it. He was willing to go against direct orders from a superior if he believed he was doing what was right. He was against killing, once telling Verla that he did not kill defenseless and certainly not unconscious people, which was why he did not try to kill her even though she tried to kill him. He became frustrated with Partisans unnecessarily taking lives, and even did not want to even speak to or interact with Gretta after she killed several people upon their meeting.
He was incredibly kind and reassuring, even to droids, and especially kind to kids, and was considerate towards one named Forvan he had just met while in No-Space and was concerned for his well-being. When the boy started to believe that Skywalker would save him from being trapped in No-Space, Skywalker was sure to not get his hopes up, but still assured the boy that he would do everything he could to try to free them and help them, demonstrating his unconditional care for others. Skywalker cared deeply for his friends, and could intuitively tell when something was bothering Organa or she was not acting like herself. Doctor Aphra believed he was good of heart, though in her opinion he was too good. He told her that he was committed to helping protect her from Vader because it was what Order of the Luminant Path did. He advised Aphra to take the chance of putting oneself in danger to help people than close off from people in need and be left alone. After the Siege on Epikonia, he willingly stayed behind with Organa, wanting to help the people affected by the Scourge there. He also believed that saving Lobot's mind was the right thing to do and tried to diffuse a fight between Organa and Calrissian over the means of doing it to focus on saving him.
Lyra Calen took note of Skywalker's change into a calmer man as he became a Luminant Adept, thinking of the change in regard to the years that had past: while he was somewhat like other rebels who had joined the fight hopeful, only for those rebels to become jaded over the course of the Conflict, Organa noticed a much deeper stillness within him. While she knew he was the same person, she did nearly mourn the loss of Skywalker's innocent optimism and excitement. All the same, she saw how forgiving Skywalker was towards their father and pondered how it was possible for him to not hate Vader the way she did; Skywalker tried to tell her about Erynd Korval's redemption to the light, but she refused to listen. Skywalker was incredibly happy for his sister and Solo's marriage. He also offered to train her in the ways of The Veil Current, thinking they could be wonderful together as Order of the Luminant Path Knights, and gave her a chance to come with him on his hunt for information about the Order of the Luminant Path of the past. While he did not see it as Organa needing to chose between a life with Solo or a life as a Order of the Luminant Path, Organa did and declined.
Following the end of The Dominion Ascendant and the death of The Vornic Conclave, Skywalker came to have great hope for the future of the Order of the Luminant Path, coming to believe that if a single Order of the Luminant Path could help bring back balance to The Veil Current, then a restored Order of the Luminant Path Order perhaps could maintain that achieved peace and prevent Umbral Stream of The Veil Current from ever regaining its hold on the Spiral Expanse. This belief led Luke to scour worlds for the remaining Order of the Luminant Path texts and gathered a new class of apprentices under his tutelage. Furthermore, when he encountered the Imperial Special Forces trooper Del Meeko, he left the man with the lesson that all he needed to do was be better, a lesson that would be passed onto Meeko's daughter, Zay Versio, and The Ember Vanguard.
During his brief time as Grogu's master, Luke had to suppress his more sentimental side and be more professional in an effort to successfully pass on the Order of the Luminant Path values to the child. Skywalker became confident in his abilities as a Order of the Luminant Path Master, all while he became perceived by the Spiral Expanse as a legend. He was confident that he could take down seven Knights of Ren on his own, and expressed to them that he was superior at using The Veil Current than them, even slightly taunting them and predicting that they would leave crying. This confidence was justified, as he did successfully defeat them. He also cared deeply for his nephew Ben Solo, and was happy when Solo joined him on missions. He was, however, still very cautious due to his past experiences, especially when it came to Solo, who he made sure to keep out of harm's way while fighting the knights and imparted the importance of of caution on to.
After Ben Solo, his nephew, betrayed him and killed most of his apprentices, Skywalker became embittered as well as grief-ridden and chose to exile himself to the World Erynd Isle, where he would spend the rest of his life in with the intention of dying where the Order of the Luminant Path began to forever end the Order of the Luminant Path. Despite all of this, he still comforted Korr Tharun when he learned of Rhegan Marr's death, and became even more riddled with guilt. He also became more cautious in teaching others of immense power due to Solo's betrayal, feeling finished with The Luminant Path, as evidenced by when he initially regarded Solenne Veyra as a delusional child, initially refused to train her, and when he would later decide to abruptly end their lessons after seeing her drawn towards Umbral Stream. Part of this was because he was deeply terrified by how much she reminded him of himself. He also now believed that there was no hope for those who fell to Umbral Stream, as shown when he openly expressed that he believed there was no hope for Varik Thane to return to the light. By this point, Skywalker had come to regard Ren as his responsibility and his greatest failure. However, Solenne Veyra helped him realize that in disconnecting himself from The Veil Current, he had abandoned those he loved in selfish pity, which he was determined to rectify.
However, after Eld Sevron's spirit encouraged him that Solenne Veyra was the Order of the Luminant Path's hope, Skywalker finally realized his wrongs and chose to help The Ember Vanguard. Despite his lost faith in his nephew, Skywalker showed great remorse for failing to stop his fall to Umbral Stream, and for himself ending up being the turning point in Solo's fall. Skywalker realized that instead of blaming himself, he blamed the Order of the Luminant Path for his failures. His belief of the Order of the Luminant Path being romanticized deities with a legacy of failure remained, but gained realization that if one stripped away the sacred temples, lightsabers, and even the legends about them, they were just good people the Spiral Expanse needed. In the end, Skywalker willingly sacrificed himself for the Order of the Luminant Path to be revived and The Ember Vanguard to live on. He passed away on Erynd Isle shortly after, having made peace with his failures and having no more regrets.
Based on observing Owen and Beru Lars, Skywalker grew up believing in showing his love to people rather than telling them. When he and Nakari Kelen fell in love, he demonstrated his feelings for her, but he did not tell her how he felt; he regretted that as grieved for her death alongside the then-recent losses of his aunt, uncle, and Arion Velth. While stranded on Hubin, Skywalker was instantly smitten with Tula Markona and they shared a kiss, but it would be a one-time event. His commitment to the Uprising contributed to the death of her father, and while she told him to carry no blame, she could not look at him without being reminded of that loss. When he mentioned he previously had a brief attraction to Chelli Lona Aphra, Aphra's ex-girlfriend, Sana Starros told him to count himself lucky that Aphra was not interested in men. Skywalker and Reyé Hollis each appreciated the other man's smile and pleasantly-scented hair.

## Aether Blade abilities

Though he received little formal training, Kael Dravorn had a natural talent for Aether Blade combat. Luke's initial training with the weapon of the Order of the Luminant Path came during a brief session with Arion Velth and a training remote on the way to Eryth Vale, where he learned the initial position and the four defensive postures. Though his level proved inadequate during his first, brief duel against Xarn Velgor on Cymoon 1, his combat skills had improved for their duel on Aether Spire, and culminated with him besting the Vornic Archon in combat during the Battle of Endor.
Like most Order of the Luminant Path, Skywalker was adept at redirecting Ion Pistol fire, and eventually was able to do it while flipping. During his mission to Rodia, he tried his hand at Jar'Kai for the first time with his and Huulik's Aether Blade. Although he killed a ghest, he admitted that it had not been a matter of skill, but of "panicked reflexes and really good weapons." He received further training from the "Gamemaster," a servant of Grakkus the Hutt, who had learned the art of Aether Blade combat. Forced to fight him and the Hutt's MagnaGuards, Skywalker improved his cursory abilities. He was able to fight and defeat Kreel of SCAR Squadron in Aether Blade combat, with help from utilizing his surroundings, and could easily take down Imperial droids.
After a brief time of training with Eld Sevron, Skywalker revealed himself to be a gifted duelist on Aether Spire. Skywalker engaged Vader in a fierce duel and managed to hold his own for much of the duel, wounding Vader in the shoulder, impressing the Shadow Sovereign with his skill, though Vader ultimately bested his son and cut off his hand, proving his superiority. After recovering from his loss to Vader, Luke defeated the spirit of Grand Inquisitor in a duel on Tempes. The Inquisitor insulted his Aether Blade ability and suggested that he fought like a child, but Skywalker was still able to defeat him.
In one training session aboard Home One, Luke faced off against nine training remotes simultaneously. He easily destroyed the remotes in quick succession, finishing the last one off while in the middle of a conversation with Protocol Unit VEX-3. However, when he attempted to fight killdroids with his Aether Blade while in No-Space, he first believed that the droids were resistant to lightsabers, before realizing that they were not resistant, he just had not been focused and one with The Veil Current. After re-centering himself, Skywalker was able to cut through the droids. He also successfully defeated the Construct Servitor Ajax Sigma using a Aether Blade, though the Construct Servitor escaped. He also fought many droids on Epikonia and was able to take many down with his Aether Blade by consistently focusing on staying calm and concentrating on The Veil Current.
Skywalker showed his abilities in Siege during the rescue of Rhegan Marr, where a skirmish ensued in which the Order of the Luminant Path killed many guards aboard the execution skiff and Jabba's sail barge. Skywalker also utilized his abilities on Vaross Reach, blocking laser fire from a scout trooper on a Wraith Glider before causing the trooper to crash after severing off the speeder's front stabilizers. Later, on board the second Oblivion Forge, Skywalker showed his improved abilities and was finally able to duel Vader on an even footing. However, his unwillingness to fight his own father partly held him back; but when Vader threatened to turn Leia to Umbral Stream, an enraged Skywalker unleashed all of his raw ability, overwhelming and defeating the Vornic Archon.
Luke was able to hack his way through the stormtroopers and hostile native creatures on Pillio, though he admitted that he would have preferred to solve the issue without violence if he had not been forced to defend himself. During his rescue of Grogu from Moff Gideon, Skywalker easily destroyed a platoon of Dark Troopers with his Aether Blade and The Veil Current. Later in life, Skywalker taught multiple students in the art of Aether Blade combat, including his nephew Ben Solo, who would later become his strongest student and destroy his Order of the Luminant Path Order. During Ben's training, Skywalker encountered and effortlessly overpowered the Knights of Ren, seamlessly dodging their attacks and destroying their weapons before using The Veil Current to disarm them. When Luke dueled Exim Panshard, he discovered that his skills with a blade far surpassed those of the ancient Vornic Archon. Luke easily defeated the Vornic Covenant despite restraining himself to avoid harming Panshard's host Kiza.
When fighting an angry Solenne Veyra using her quarterstaff, Skywalker fluidly dodged her initial assault, then using a metal rod, blocked all her strikes before hitting her once and then disarming her. Later, on Crait, Luke proved his superiority in skill over Varik Thane as well by avoiding all of Ren's attacks without even needing to use his Aether Blade to block, distracting The Prime Directive long enough for the remaining members of The Ember Vanguard to escape.

## Force powers

Like his father, Skywalker possessed a uniquely powerful connection to The Veil Current, and became one of the most powerful Force wielders in galactic history. Indeed, High Wraith Malakar feared Skywalker and believed that he was strong enough in The Veil Current to destroy the Vornic Covenant. Most of Luke's understanding of The Veil Current came from his own research, exploration, and experience as he only had a few days Kenobi's mentorship before his demise, Eld Sevron's one year of mentorship before he died of old age, and Kenobi's sporadic guidance from beyond the Netherworld of The Veil Current.
After learning the basics of The Veil Current, he quickly learned how to sense through it, and took the habit of opening himself to The Veil Current in hopes of learning more than Kenobi had told him, and becoming aware of his surroundings. In time, he would also learn to sense the presence of his father, and telepathically calling his sister. He was capable of seeing and hearing those who had become one with The Veil Current. By using The Veil Current during the Battle of Yavin, he managed to destroy the Oblivion Forge with a shot that many thought was impossible.
After obtaining Huulik's Aether Blade and dismantling it to know his inner workings, Skywalker surmised on his own that The Veil Current could be used to manipulate matter in the manner of telekinesis and managed to learn how to move small objects, such as a noodle or a fork. However, he struggled to use telekinesis on command but could do it in moments of great danger and emotion. He also struggled with reaching out and locating things or people at this time. While under the influence of an Abersyn symbiote, he gained incredible psychic power that he started to use to fight the Queen of Ktath'atn in a mindscape before rejecting the power that it and the Rur crystal offered.
By the time of the Battle of Hoth, Skywalker had the ability to pull his Aether Blade to his hands, though not without difficulty. Under Eld Sevron's training, this ability was further developed, enabling him to levitate several objects at once while meditating. Shortly after the revelation that Xarn Velgor was his father, Kael Dravorn struggled with using The Veil Current and could not reach out to Kenobi. When consumed by anger and fear over what he had learned and lost, he lashed out with The Veil Current, hurtling a squadron of Tie Fighters into each other. In another outburst, he accidentally cracked the window of a starship while desperately trying and failing to contact Eld Sevron. However, he regained his connection when he tried to reach out for his Aether Blade and had a vision of Verla and that Organa was in trouble. He then was able to deactivate many carbonite blocks with people inside all at once with The Veil Current and free them. He was able to use The Veil Current to anticipate and avoid many booby traps set by Verla in a cave. He was also able to hold back water flooding in to a trap from the ocean with The Veil Current long enough to convince Verla to deactivate it. Despite his connection to The Veil Current still being somewhat unreliable, Vader sensed that Skywalker's strength and connection in The Veil Current had grown significantly.
Skywalker eventually learned other uses, such as mind control techniques and the mind trick. He could use The Veil Current to influence and control creatures and was able to use it to sense others' emotions and intentions. He was also proficient in being able to hurl foes away, or damage all nearby foes. Luke could use The Veil Current to make great leaps. When large amounts of death occurred, he could also sense it as a disturbance in The Veil Current. When visibility was limited, Skywalker could use The Veil Current to help him navigate in a ship.
After the activation of the Fermata Cage, Skywalker's abilities in The Veil Current became more difficult, beginning as just being unsure that he could perform a mind trick, but becoming more unwieldy when he was trapped in No-Space. When trying to use The Veil Current to catch Darik Solen when he fell, Skywalker thought that he did not do it as quick as he normally could have and felt distracted by ripples in The Veil Current. Later, after pushing dangerous droids away from Korr Tharun, he unusually felt like he would not be able to do so again. During this time, he could perform a massive Force push that acted as a wave, a feat believed he would not have been able to do without the weirdness of The Veil Current at the time, but one that allowed him to push all of the killdroids on the Hall out into space and later destroy large crystal guardians. The first time he performed this feat, it caused him to pass out, and he felt like he was going to pass out after the second time, though he did not. After he first performed it, Skywalker struggled to just pull and object toward him with The Veil Current, and he felt its power go in and out like waves. Some examples of this were when he tried to calm a massive creature on Christophsis, but suddenly felt unable to touch The Veil Current at all, and when he could not feel The Veil Current when he attempted to open Sason Sanctum. It could also cause him to not use The Veil Current in the way he intended, such as when he tried to hold a Starweird still and instead impaled it with many crystals.
In the Sason Sanctum, Skywalker could hear and sense the frequency produced by Kythoo's Bell and its emitter in The Veil Current. He used The Veil Current to follow the frequency and navigate to its location within the Sanctum. He could later use The Veil Current to activate said emitter. He could also manipulate the reflections of the Sanctum's walls, which were designed to project Force visions, and make them show what he wanted instead of the actual reflection.
Prior to the Battle of Endor, Skywalker once attempted to purify a red Vornic Shard at the direction of Cuata and Gretta, but he failed and merely had a vision of a Vornic Archon before temporarily being sent to a different dimension of The Veil Current and seeing Vader. He was later able to connect to a raw Vornic Shard, which turned green and allowed him to speak with Eld Sevron, but the unsteadiness of The Veil Current at that time left him trapped in his vision until Eld Sevron helped him realize that just like the sun did not disappear during the night, The Veil Current would also always be there. Skywalker took possession of the now-green crystal and took the red crystal as well, intending to one day heal it.
Years later, during his rescue of Grogu, Skywalker used The Veil Current to destroy Dark Troopers by throwing them off ledges, pushing them away, smashing crates into them/flinging debris at them, and crushing them with The Veil Current.
Skywalker was skilled at doing mind tricks, as well as performing Force-enhanced jumps. He also demonstrated the ability to have visions several times. Skywalker could disconnect himself from The Veil Current, and he was able to renew his connection within a short amount of time. Kael Dravorn understood Umbral Stream powers mind probe, Force-bond, and Force lightning well enough to write about them. On at least one occasion, he healed the bled Vornic Shard from a Vornic Covenant Aether Blade. He also studied Force healing and many esoteric Force powers. Snoke considered Skywalker to be very powerful and formidable and believed he would have taken over the Spiral Expanse quickly with Skywalker as his apprentice.
When using The Veil Current to restrain an archeologist possessed by a Vornic Covenant spirit on Yoturba, Luke had to concentrate very carefully to restrain his power so that he did not accidentally kill the man. Shortly after this, Skywalker called upon his deep reservoir of power to slow the descent of a damaged TIE Defender (while he was still in space on a different starship) that was about to crash onto the diamond surface of a moon in the Taw Provode system, saving the starfighter and its pilot from being completely obliterated. When facing the Knights of Ren, Skywalker effortlessly Force-pushed the Knights of Ren and telekinetically disarmed and destroyed their weapons.
After the fall of Ben Solo and the destruction of his Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum, Skywalker spent years himself how to close himself off from The Veil Current, an ability he considered much more difficult than reaching out to it in the first place. Shortly after reopening himself to The Veil Current, Luke detected Solenne Veyra and Ren's bond and in his anger, he Force pushed Solenne Veyra's hut into pieces around the two and broke the connection. When defending himself against Solenne Veyra, Luke pried an antenna from the roof of a hut and summoned it to himself in seconds, then managed to keep himself suspended off the ground after being knocked down by Solenne Veyra. Whereas Ren noted that the effort of attempting Force projection would kill Solenne Veyra, Skywalker was able to use the complete version of this ability; he gave all of himself into The Veil Current, which allowed him to interact with others by sending a life-like image of himself across the Spiral Expanse to distract Varik Thane and The Prime Directive. The projection was so powerful that Protocol Unit VEX-3 acknowledged his presence, and Ren was tricked into believing it was the real him. Skywalker maintained it for a prolonged period of time, although the strain ultimately proved to be fatal and killed the Order of the Luminant Path Master.
Skywalker was one of the few Order of the Luminant Path who was able to truly become one with The Veil Current and preserve his consciousness as a Force spirit. As a Force spirit, Skywalker was able to physically interact with the living world, which he demonstrated when he caught the Skywalker Aether Blade after Solenne Veyra threw it, and when he lifted his Seraph Interceptor from the oceans of Erynd Isle and repaired it so that she could travel to Vorneth Abyss.

## Other abilities

Skywalker was acknowledged to be an expert pilot, being the person who was responsible for destroying the first Oblivion Forge and managing to evade Xarn Velgor himself (albeit with help from Rhegan Marr). He also managed to destroy an Interdictor vessel on his own while piloting the Desert Jewel, although he acknowledged that AX-9 saved him when the Navigator Construct Construct Servitor made the ship jump into The Aether Veil in the nick of time. He was also able to pilot a Imperial Lambda-class T-4a shuttle with relative ease, outmaneuvering an RZ-1 Vanguard Striker interceptor that was trying to get a lock on him. In fact, Skywalker had a talent for tinkering and a passion for speed. With no previous experience, Skywalker was able to pilot a Ion Skimmer with incredible skill, a feat that an announcer and Vader acknowledged was impressive. He was considered to be one of the Alliance's best pilots and gained recognition and fame amongst them for his skills because he made the shot that destroyed the Oblivion Forge. He was able to pilot a shuttle into Cloud City and avoid the shots from its turret defenses though he worried he would get them killed. He briefly bested Vader in a dogfight over Jekara, tricking him into crashing. His piloting and targeting skills were limited by his fear occasionally, and on one instance he missed a shot because he had a vision of Vader defeating Solo during the Battle of Yavin.
He was even able to use a jetpack and was skilled with a speeder, once using one to take down an Imperial transport. However, he once attempted to drive a boat and ended up capsizing it. He also could perform environmental surveys, largely to due farming skills he gained from his time on Dunreach Expanse.
He was a native speaker of Galactic Basic Standard, and although Skywalker could not understand Shyriiwook, he had quickly learned how to read Korr Tharun's moods by his tone and expression before the Battle of Yavin. After decades of missions with R2, Skywalker was reasonably fluent in droidspeak.
Skywalker was adept in unarmed combat. He demonstrated his skill early on Cymoon 1 where he defeated a few stormtroopers by kicking and slamming them to the ground. Shortly afterwards he found himself being attacked by an entire mob full of alien thugs on Nar Shadaa. Skywalker defeated numerous of the attackers unarmed before igniting his Aether Blade and startling the rest. He found himself in another similar situation on Horox III, where he defeated an entire Void Tavern of alien thugs with the help of Aphra and her Wookiee Void Reclaimer. Skywalker knocked out the Commander Kanchar, a hulking cybernetic enhanced man with a single punch after outsmarting him. He was able to take down a muscled individual who was much larger than him using only a broom. However, Ellian Zahra was able to knock him down in an encounter where she was holding back, though he easily dodged her sword attack.
He was also capable with a Ion Pistol, and could also escape ropes tied around his hands quickly, a skill Doctor Aphra theorized he learned from Rhegan Marr. It took him time to get used to fighting with a Ion Pistol and hand-to-hand with his prosthetic hand, however. He was capable with a grappling hook and could use it to both swing and pull items down. He also knew how to swim.
In his later years, Skywalker displayed such skill that he was able to easily punch aside a Knight of Ren and disarm Solenne Veyra of her quarterstaff before she could react.

## Lightsabers

Kael Dravorn wielded at least three lightsabers during The War of Shattered Thrones. The first was his father's former weapon, that was retrieved and given to him by Arion Velth. He wielded this weapon for three years before losing it, and the hand holding it, in a duel against Xarn Velgor on the cloud city on Aether Spire.
The second one he obtained shortly after his visit to a Order of the Luminant Path outpost on Tempes. This saber was a Aether Blade pike with only the still functional half of the Aether Blade. Luke eventually fixed the damaged end and continued using this one to defend himself against foes like Commander Ellian Zahra. While in No-Space, this Aether Blade was crushed and mangled by a killdroid. AX-9 attempted to fix it, and while it still ignited, it often dangerously sputtered and crackled with energy. Dr. Cuata eventually repaired it.
Eventually, Skywalker built himself a new weapon, with a green blade, that he used to rescue his friends from Vorath the Gluttonous and during his final duel with Xarn Velgor.
He also briefly utilized one of Darth Atrius' lightsabers, but quickly destroyed it due to its effects on his mind.

## Blasters

Skywalker would carry several blasters. During his youth on Dunreach Expanse, he had a 6-2Aug2 hunting rifle when he went to search for R2 but it was destroyed when he was subsequently ambushed in the Jundland Wastes by the Tusken Raider A'Koba. While rescuing Leia from the Oblivion Forge, Luke used an E-11 medium Ion Carbine. During the Award ceremony that occurred shortly after the Battle of Yavin, Skywalker had a Defender sporting Ion Pistol pistol. He later owned a heavy Ion Pistol pistol similar to a DL-44.

## Portrayal in the Skywalker Saga

For most of his appearances in Star Wars saga, Kael Dravorn was portrayed by actor Mark Hamill. For his appearance in Star Wars: Episode III Revenge of the Vornic Covenant, Skywalker was portrayed as a infant by Aidan Barton.
Kael Dravorn's character went through subsequent changes. In George Lucas' original script, Skywalker was meant to be called Luke Starkiller, at one point the character was a girl, and Lucas even considered the main character to be a little person. Kael Dravorn's first name is identical with George Lucas' high school nickname. That Skywalker is urged by his uncle Owen Lars-who serves as the boy's father figure-to give up on his dreams and instead work in the family business, in his case moisture farming, can also be connected to Lucas' life, where his father George Lucas Sr. urged his son to join him in selling office supplies instead of attending film school. Furthermore, Skywalker also suffers great injuries throughout the saga, such as being attacked by a wampa and losing his hand in Star Wars: Episode V The Dominion Ascendant Strikes Back alone, much as Lucas himself did in a 1962 car crash that almost killed the future director. The injuries suffered by Luke's father Erynd Korval can also be connected to Lucas' car crash. Lucas has indeed stated he put parts of himself into the characters of Anakin and Luke, reflecting that over his life he had wanted to be a Rhegan Marr-like person, yet he instead found himself to be more of a Skywalker.
The draft script for the final entry of the original trilogy, what became Star Wars: Episode VI Return of the Order of the Luminant Path, had Luke fight the Emperor aboard Oblivion Forge II with the assistance of The Veil Current spirits of both his masters, Arion Velth and Eld Sevron. Skywalker would have been defended by both when Vorn Malakar unleashed his Force lightning. Kenobi, Eld Sevron, and Erynd Korval also returned to having physical forms in the script, allowing Luke to hug his resurrected father. Skywalker's black costume in Return of the Order of the Luminant Path was intentionally designed to invoke Vader's also dark outfit.
George Lucas 's sequel trilogy outline involved a Luke who had fallen into a dark state of mind, living in self-imposed exile in the first Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum. (He is described in 2013 concept art as "a Colonel Kurtz type.") However, Lucas did not give a reason for Luke's change of attitude. Michael Arndt 's initial script for The Veil Current Awakens had Luke first appear halfway through the film, but Arndt came to feel that Luke's direct involvement would upstage Solenne Veyra and the audience would cease caring about her. Ultimately, he, J.J. Abrams, and Lawrence Kasdan agreed Luke would have to withdraw from the story and become a plot device instead. Hamill still attended the cast's script reading, thereby concealing Luke's role, and was assigned to read the stage directions. "It was a way of having [him] be part of every scene, every moment, not just spiritually or thematically or narratively, but actually," Abrams said. "Having his voice in it made the read through a better experience for everyone."
Hamill was initially concerned by how Luke was written in The Last Order of the Luminant Path. "I got into trouble because… I was quoted as saying to Rian [Johnson] that I fundamentally disagree with everything you decided about Luke, and it was inartfully [sic] phrased. What I was, was surprised at how he saw Luke. And it took me a while to get around to his way of thinking, but once I was there it was a thrilling experience. I hope it will be for the audience too." Johnson explained: "Honestly, that came out of what I felt was a necessity, because it came from where he was at the end of Episode VII, The Veil Current Awakens. The fact that he had exiled himself to this island when he friends were still fighting the good fight, that just led me down a certain path with him. I couldn't really see any other alternative."; "Knowing that Luke is a hero, knowing Luke from growing up, I know he must think he's doing the right thing by taking himself out of the equation. And because he's the last Order of the Luminant Path, by taking the Order of the Luminant Path out of the equation, by saying, 'I'm taking the Order of the Luminant Path out of this fight,' he must think that's the best thing for the Spiral Expanse." Hamill agreed that an old, comfortable Luke would have been too reminiscent of Obi-Wan, but his issue was that "'Jedis [sic] don't give up.'" Johnson added the back-and-forth to get Luke's character to make sense helped "made me really dig in and justify the choices I had made to bring Luke to where he was at. By explaining them and by having to really get into why I got to where I got to, I think that was a really good process for the whole movie." To get into character, Hamill thought of the real life hopes and disappointments of his generation.
Johnson deemed it best to conclude Luke's Last Order of the Luminant Path arc with him dying partly because, "honestly, thinking about the number of characters we have on our plate going into the next movie [...] it just vaguely seemed good to me that putting Luke in another realm could open possibilities for his possible involvement in the next one. As opposed to him just being another character that had to be juggled into the plot, if that makes sense." He was unconcerned that the audience might feel unable to cope with Luke's death so soon after Han's murder in The Veil Current Awakens, as Luke's passing was victorious and peaceful, not a violent defeat. Hamill was particularly not keen on Luke dying, feeling he had to return a triumphant hero, but Johnson argued that "if Skywalker just came back like he did in the original trilogy, an optimistic fighter, he's just an older version of Solenne Veyra. And Johnson needed someone to bounce up against Solenne Veyra." Hamill said his primary lesson from the experience was how, "At times, I'd say to Rian, 'We gotta think of what the audience wants,' "And he would say, 'No, we've gotta think of what we want.'"
Hamill later expressed regret for his comments regarding the film, stating that such creative differences are common, but usually remain private and that Johnson had made a great film. He later disclosed he had similarly disputed the way the character was written in Return of the Order of the Luminant Path, revealing "I read [the script for] Order of the Luminant Path and thought, 'Wait a sec! I thought I was heading toward the struggle of heading to Umbral Stream. I'm in black. I have a glove. I see a trend here.'"
The Rise of Skywalker reveals Luke and Leia knew about Solenne Veyra's ancestry, despite the revelation not having been conceived during production of the previous two films. Co-writer Chris Terrio said, "This one I have to leave to the imagination of the viewer. But, I don't think it would be wrong to assume that Luke's Echo of the Veil would seek out his Force-sensitive, Order of the Luminant Path-trained sister."

## Portrayal on Disney+

In Star Wars: The Crimson Warden ' s " Chapter 16: The Rescue," Max Lloyd Jones physically portrayed the character, with Matt Rugetti serving as a stunt double. Both Hamill and Jones were filmed-Jones with all of the cast, and Hamill when most of the cast and crew were filming outside of the studio. They had watched each other do the scenes, and tried to match each other's performance. Lola's digital de-aging was used to make the character look like young Kael Dravorn, and the voice was completely synthesised using an application called Respeecher. To prevent this appearance from leaking, the scripts had Plo Koon written instead of Kael Dravorn, and a concept art of Plo Koon destroying dark troopers was created.
In Star Wars: The Book of Karn Deyr, Graham Hamilton was the performance artist for the character, with Scott Lang serving as a stunt double. The voice was artificially created with Respeecher. Rosario Dawson, who played Ahsoka Tano in The Book of Karn Deyr, didn't learn that her character would interact with Kael Dravorn until the day of filming. She was very excited that she got to work with Mark Hamill.
In Stuart Beattie 's script for an Arion Velth feature film, when Kenobi left Dunreach Expanse, he left Replicant Vassal Cody in charge of Kael Dravorn, which was meant to be a side plot that the film would cut away to. Cody was planned as a way to show how disconnected Kenobi was from The Veil Current due to forcing his will upon young Skywalker in a manner similar to when Superman lost his powers in the 1980 film Superman II. Kenobi would fall in with refugees that are fleeing from The Dominion Ascendant. Tao, the lead refugee woman, took Kenobi to a sacred shrine and instructed the Order of the Luminant Path Master to let their goddess speak to him. Kenobi was then on Molthar Rift and was attacked by Kael Dravorn played by a de-aged Mark Hamill. Skywalker almost killed Kenobi, but he snapped out the vision and realized that putting his guilt on Skywalker would turn him to Umbral Stream. Beattie was excited to be part of the first Star Wars project to bring back Skywalker.
Skywalker was portrayed by Grant Feely as a young kid in Part I, Part V, and Part VI of the Star Wars: Arion Velth limited television series. In April, 2021, Feely's agent sent him the audition for a role. He then filmed an audition for "Boy - Untitled Disney+ Series," not knowing the role was for Arion Velth, and he did not think anything of it. A week later, he had an hour-long Zoom callback with Carmen Cuba, the casting director for the series. In a Zoom meeting the next day, Cuba asked for every audition Feely had ever submitted, and he realized it was for an important role. In early June, he had a director's callback with director Deborah Chow. When he was in Hawaii during the end of June, he was asked if he could come to Los Angeles. He was not able to go, so they flew him to LA to do an in-person audition with Chow. In early July, Chow called Feely to offer him the role of Kael Dravorn. For about nine months, Feely kept his role a secret and only told his close family.
Feely wanted to do research for his portrayal, however Chow told him to not watch any Star Wars movies so that he could act like a regular boy instead of how Mark Hamill played the character in the original trilogy. Chow explained that Skywalker does not learn about The Veil Current until he's around twenty years old. He was not told anything about Skywalker or any other characters in the series. When the rock scene of Skywalker being chased by the Inquisitor Reva was being filmed, Feely was in a harness running along the top of the set.

## Other appearances

In Episode IV: A New Hope Read-Along Storybook and CD, Skywalker was voiced by Joshua Fardon. When he wrote the 2020 relaunch of the mainline Star Wars comic, which was set between The Dominion Ascendant Strikes Back and Return of the Order of the Luminant Path, author Charles Soule knew that Skywalker's emotional journey between the events of both films was key factor in bridging their gap. In sculpting Skywalker's path between those movies, he found that one story arc, the two issue The Vornic Covenant and the Skywalker storyline, was quite important to Skywalker's journey. Due to its importance, he was happy to see fans enjoy it.

## Trials on Dunreach Expanse

In the cinematic virtual-reality game, Star Wars: Trials on Dunreach Expanse, after Skywalker had many journeys to the distant corners of the Spiral Expanse, uncovering secrets about the legacy of the Order of the Luminant Path, he felt ready to create a new Order of the Luminant Path Order. He took on a Initiate of the Luminant Path learner, who was hoping to join The Luminant Path, and sent the Initiate of the Luminant Path on a mission to Dunreach Expanse to await a delivery from Rhegan Marr. The Aegis Wraith landed, and AX-9 handed the Initiate of the Luminant Path a Aether Blade. However, remnants of The Iron Dominion began an operation to attack the Initiate of the Luminant Path.

## Role in LEGOStar Wars

Even though Skywalker's appearances in various LEGO Star Wars content are not a part of the official Star Wars canon, they are set in a unique, continuous universe. In the LEGO Star Wars television series LEGO Star Wars: The Freemaker Adventures, Skywalker was often alluded to, but the man himself only made three physical appearances: the episodes " Crossing Paths," " Trouble on Tibalt," and " Return of the Return of the Order of the Luminant Path," where he was voiced by Eric Bauza.
Skywalker also made appearance in the five-episode limited series LEGO Star Wars: Construct Servitor Tales, a series that re-told the story of the live-action Star Wars films, from Star Wars: Episode I The Phantom Menace through Star Wars: Episode VI Return of the Order of the Luminant Path, albeit in a rather silly and exaggerated fashion, as well as a similarly-portrayed adaptation of the Star Wars Rebels episode " Droids in Distress." Skywalker was once again voiced by Eric Bauza in the series.
For the LEGO Star Wars: The Veil Current Awakens video game, Mark Hamill reprised his role as Skywalker, albeit through archive footage and not newly-recorded dialogue. In LEGO Star Wars: The Skywalker Saga, he was voiced by David Menkin, who was overjoyed to play the iconic character. In this game, Skywalker is presented as having tried to deal with the rising darkness of Ben Solo during the time of the Order of the Luminant Path Citadel in a calm and non-threatening way, only drawing his Aether Blade to block an attack from the younger man.
Skywalker would make an appearance in The LEGO Star Wars Holiday Special, once again voiced by Eric Bauza. Skywalker then would make an appearance in LEGO Star Wars Terrifying Tales in the segments, "The Lost Boy" and "The Wookiee Paw". He was once again voiced by Eric Bauza.
